```
1→"""Queue/stage mutation helpers for triage workflow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix
     9→from desloppify.engine.plan_ops import purge_ids
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import PlanModel
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import TRIAGE_IDS, TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS
    12→
    13→from .plan_state_access import ensure_queue_order, ensure_skipped_map
    14→
    15→STAGE_ORDER = ["observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check", "value-check"]
    16→
    17→
    18→def has_triage_in_queue(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
    19→    """Return True when any triage stage IDs are currently queued."""
    20→    order = set(ensure_queue_order(plan))
    21→    return bool(order & TRIAGE_IDS)
    22→
    23→
    24→def clear_triage_stage_skips(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
    25→    """Remove skipped markers for triage stages before reinjection."""
    26→    skipped = ensure_skipped_map(plan)
    27→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    28→        skipped.pop(sid, None)
    29→
    30→
    31→def inject_triage_stages(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
    32→    """Inject the canonical triage stage IDs at the queue front."""
    33→    order = ensure_queue_order(plan)
    34→    clear_triage_stage_skips(plan)
    35→    remaining = [issue_id for issue_id in order if issue_id not in TRIAGE_IDS]
    36→    order[:] = [*remaining, *TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS]
    37→    normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(order)
    38→
    39→
    40→def purge_triage_stage(plan: PlanModel, stage_name: str) -> None:
    41→    """Remove one triage stage workflow item from the plan."""
    42→    purge_ids(plan, [f"triage::{stage_name}"])
    43→
    44→
    45→def cascade_clear_dispositions(meta: dict[str, Any], from_stage: str) -> None:
    46→    """Reset issue_dispositions when an earlier stage reruns.
    47→
    48→    - observe rerun: wipe the entire disposition map (verdicts change)
    49→    - reflect rerun: clear decision/target/decision_source from all entries
    50→      (observe verdicts remain, but reflect decisions are outdated)
    51→    """
    52→    dispositions = meta.get("issue_dispositions")
    53→    if not dispositions:
    54→        return
    55→    if from_stage == "observe":
    56→        meta["issue_dispositions"] = {}
    57→    elif from_stage == "reflect":
    58→        for entry in dispositions.values():
    59→            entry.pop("decision", None)
    60→            entry.pop("target", None)
    61→            entry.pop("decision_source", None)
    62→
    63→
    64→def cascade_clear_later_confirmations(
    65→    stages: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
    66→    from_stage: str,
    67→) -> list[str]:
    68→    """Clear later-stage confirmations after mutating an earlier stage."""
    69→    try:
    70→        idx = STAGE_ORDER.index(from_stage)
    71→    except ValueError:
    72→        return []
    73→    cleared: list[str] = []
    74→    for later in STAGE_ORDER[idx + 1:]:
    75→        if later in stages and stages[later].get("confirmed_at"):
    76→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_at", None)
    77→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_text", None)
    78→            cleared.append(later)
    79→    return cleared
    80→
    81→
    82→def print_cascade_clear_feedback(
    83→    cleared: list[str],
    84→    stages: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
    85→) -> None:
    86→    """Render confirmation-clearing feedback after stage rewrites."""
    87→    if not cleared:
    88→        return
    89→    print(colorize(f"  Cleared confirmations on: {', '.join(cleared)}", "yellow"))
    90→    next_unconfirmed = next(
    91→        (
    92→            stage
    93→            for stage in STAGE_ORDER
    94→            if stage in stages and not stages[stage].get("confirmed_at")
    95→        ),
    96→        None,
    97→    )
    98→    if next_unconfirmed:
    99→        print(
   100→            colorize(
   101→                f"  Re-confirm with: desloppify plan triage --confirm {next_unconfirmed}",
   102→                "dim",
   103→            )
   104→        )
   105→
   106→
   107→__all__ = [
   108→    "STAGE_ORDER",
   109→    "cascade_clear_dispositions",
   110→    "cascade_clear_later_confirmations",
   111→    "has_triage_in_queue",
   112→    "inject_triage_stages",
   113→    "print_cascade_clear_feedback",
   114→    "purge_triage_stage",
   115→]
   116→
```

```
    65→
    66→
    67→def triage_phase_banner(
    68→    plan: PlanModel,
    69→    state: dict | None = None,
    70→    *,
    71→    snapshot: TriageSnapshot | None = None,
    72→) -> str:
    73→    """Return a banner string describing triage status."""
    74→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    75→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    76→    run_hint = (
    77→        f"Run: {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX} "
    78→        f"(or {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE})"
    79→    )
    80→    resolved_state = state or {}
    81→    resolved_snapshot = snapshot or build_triage_snapshot(plan, resolved_state)
    82→
    83→    if not resolved_snapshot.has_triage_in_queue:
    84→        undispositioned = len(resolved_snapshot.undispositioned_ids)
    85→        if undispositioned:
    86→            return (
    87→                "TRIAGE RECOVERY NEEDED — "
    88→                f"{undispositioned} review work item(s) still need cluster/skip dispositions. "
    89→                f"{run_hint}"
    90→            )
    91→        if resolved_snapshot.is_triage_stale or meta.get("triage_recommended"):
    92→            return (
    93→                "TRIAGE RECOMMENDED — review work items changed since last triage. "
    94→                f"{run_hint}"
    95→            )
    96→        return ""
    97→
    98→    if resolved_state and has_objective_backlog(resolved_state, None):
    99→        undispositioned = len(resolved_snapshot.undispositioned_ids)
   100→        if undispositioned:
   101→            return (
   102→                "TRIAGE PENDING — "
   103→                f"{undispositioned} review work item(s) still need cluster/skip dispositions after current work. "
   104→                f"{run_hint}"
   105→            )
   106→        return (
   107→            "TRIAGE PENDING — queued and will activate after objective work "
   108→            "is complete."
   109→        )
   110→    progress = resolved_snapshot.progress
   111→    if progress.completed_count:
   112→        total_stages = len(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   113→        return (
   114→            f"TRIAGE MODE ({progress.completed_count}/{total_stages} stages recorded) — "
   115→            f"complete all stages to exit. {run_hint}"
   116→        )
   117→    return (
   118→        "TRIAGE MODE — review work items need analysis before fixing. "
   119→        f"{run_hint}"
   120→    )
   121→
   122→__all__ = [
   123→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ADD",
```

```
   126→        StagePrerequisite("sense-check", require_confirmation=True),
   127→    ),
   128→    "commit": (
   129→        StagePrerequisite("value-check"),
   130→    ),
   131→}
   132→
   133→TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES: dict[str, set[str]] = {
   134→    stage: {prerequisite.stage_name for prerequisite in prerequisites}
   135→    for stage, prerequisites in TRIAGE_STAGE_PREREQUISITES.items()
   136→}
   137→
   138→_STAGE_LABEL_BY_NAME = dict(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   139→
   140→
   141→def _confirm_stage_command(stage_name: str) -> str:
   142→    return f"desloppify plan triage --confirm {stage_name}"
   143→
   144→
   145→def _first_unmet_prerequisite(
   146→    stage_name: str,
   147→    recorded: dict[str, bool],
   148→    confirmed: dict[str, bool],
   149→) -> StagePrerequisite | None:
   150→    for prerequisite in TRIAGE_STAGE_PREREQUISITES[stage_name]:
   151→        if not recorded.get(prerequisite.stage_name, False):
   152→            return prerequisite
   153→        if prerequisite.require_confirmation and not confirmed.get(prerequisite.stage_name, False):
   154→            return prerequisite
   155→    return None
   156→
   157→
   158→def compute_triage_progress(stages_data: dict) -> TriageProgress:
   159→    """Return canonical triage-stage progression for display and validation."""
   160→    stage_map = stages_data if isinstance(stages_data, dict) else {}
   161→    readiness: list[StageReadiness] = []
   162→    recorded: dict[str, bool] = {}
   163→    confirmed: dict[str, bool] = {}
   164→
   165→    for stage_name, _label in TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS:
   166→        payload = stage_map.get(stage_name, {})
   167→        is_recorded = isinstance(payload, dict) and stage_name in stage_map
   168→        is_confirmed = bool(payload.get("confirmed_at")) if isinstance(payload, dict) else False
   169→        readiness.append(
   170→            StageReadiness(
   171→                name=stage_name,
   172→                recorded=is_recorded,
   173→                confirmed=is_confirmed,
   174→            )
   175→        )
   176→        recorded[stage_name] = is_recorded
   177→        confirmed[stage_name] = is_confirmed
   178→
   179→    current_stage: str | None = None
   180→    blocked_reason: str | None = None
   181→    next_command: str | None = None
   182→
   183→    for stage_name, label in TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS:
   184→        if recorded[stage_name]:
   185→            continue
   186→        missing = _first_unmet_prerequisite(stage_name, recorded, confirmed)
   187→        if missing is None:
   188→            current_stage = stage_name
   189→            next_command = triage_manual_stage_command(stage_name)
   190→        else:
   191→            prerequisite_label = _STAGE_LABEL_BY_NAME.get(missing.stage_name, missing.stage_name)
   192→            if missing.require_confirmation and recorded.get(missing.stage_name, False):
   193→                blocked_reason = f"{label} blocked until {prerequisite_label} is confirmed."
   194→                next_command = _confirm_stage_command(missing.stage_name)
   195→            else:
   196→                blocked_reason = f"{label} blocked until {prerequisite_label} is recorded."
   197→                next_command = triage_manual_stage_command(missing.stage_name)
   198→        break
   199→
   200→    return TriageProgress(
   201→        stages=tuple(readiness),
   202→        current_stage=current_stage,
   203→        blocked_reason=blocked_reason,
   204→        next_command=next_command,
   205→        completed_count=sum(1 for stage in readiness if stage.recorded),
   206→        confirmed_count=sum(1 for stage in readiness if stage.confirmed),
   207→    )
   208→
   209→
   210→def triage_run_stages_command(
   211→    *,
   212→    runner: str = "codex",
   213→    only_stages: str | tuple[str, ...] | list[str] | None = None,
   214→) -> str:
   215→    """Return the canonical staged triage runner command."""
   216→    resolved_runner = str(runner).strip().lower()
   217→    if resolved_runner not in TRIAGE_RUNNERS:
   218→        supported = ", ".join(TRIAGE_RUNNERS)
   219→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported triage runner: {runner!r}. Valid: {supported}")
   220→
   221→    command = f"desloppify plan triage --run-stages --runner {resolved_runner}"
   222→    if only_stages is None:
   223→        return command
   224→
   225→    if isinstance(only_stages, str):
   226→        stages = [only_stages]
   227→    else:
   228→        stages = [str(stage).strip().lower() for stage in only_stages if str(stage).strip()]
   229→
   230→    invalid = [stage for stage in stages if stage not in _RUNNER_STAGE_NAMES]
   231→    if invalid:
   232→        supported = ", ".join(sorted(_RUNNER_STAGE_NAMES))
   233→        bad = ", ".join(sorted(set(invalid)))
   234→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported triage stage(s): {bad}. Valid: {supported}")
   235→
   236→    return f"{command} --only-stages {','.join(stages)}"
   237→
   238→
   239→def triage_runner_commands(
   240→    *,
   241→    only_stages: str | tuple[str, ...] | list[str] | None = None,
   242→) -> tuple[tuple[str, str], tuple[str, str]]:
   243→    """Return the preferred staged-runner commands for Codex and Claude."""
   244→    return (
   245→        ("Codex", triage_run_stages_command(runner="codex", only_stages=only_stages)),
   246→        ("Claude", triage_run_stages_command(runner="claude", only_stages=only_stages)),
   247→    )
   248→
   249→
   250→def triage_manual_stage_command(stage: str) -> str:
   251→    """Return the manual fallback command for a triage stage."""
   252→    resolved_stage = str(stage).strip().lower()
   253→    if resolved_stage not in _MANUAL_STAGE_COMMANDS:
   254→        supported = ", ".join(sorted(_MANUAL_STAGE_COMMANDS))
   255→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported triage stage: {stage!r}. Valid: {supported}")
   256→    return _MANUAL_STAGE_COMMANDS[resolved_stage]
   257→
   258→
   259→__all__ = [
   260→    "StagePrerequisite",
   261→    "StageReadiness",
   262→    "TriageProgress",
   263→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES",
   264→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS",
   265→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_PREREQUISITES",
   266→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ADD",
   267→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_CREATE",
   268→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH",
   269→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH_COMPACT",
   270→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_STEPS",
   271→    "TRIAGE_CMD_COMPLETE",
   272→    "TRIAGE_CMD_SENSE_CHECK",
   273→    "TRIAGE_CMD_COMPLETE_VERBOSE",
   274→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CONFIRM_EXISTING",
   275→    "TRIAGE_CMD_ENRICH",
   276→    "TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE",
   277→    "TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE",
   278→    "TRIAGE_CMD_REFLECT",
   279→    "TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE",
   280→    "TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX",
   281→    "TRIAGE_RUNNERS",
   282→    "compute_triage_progress",
   283→    "triage_manual_stage_command",
   284→    "triage_run_stages_command",
   285→    "triage_runner_commands",
   286→]
   287→
```

```
    23→
    24→def cmd_stage_observe(
    25→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    26→    *,
    27→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
    28→    has_triage_in_queue_fn=has_triage_in_queue,
    29→    inject_triage_stages_fn=inject_triage_stages,
    30→) -> None:
    31→    """Record the OBSERVE stage: verify each queued issue against the code."""
    32→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
    33→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
    34→
    35→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
    36→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
    37→    state = runtime.state
    38→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
    39→
    40→    if not has_triage_in_queue_fn(plan):
    41→        start_outcome = ensure_triage_started(
    42→            plan,
    43→            services=resolved_services,
    44→            state=state,
    45→            attestation=attestation,
    46→            start_message="  Planning mode auto-started (6 stages queued).",
    47→            deps=TriageLifecycleDeps(
    48→                has_triage_in_queue=has_triage_in_queue_fn,
    49→                inject_triage_stages=inject_triage_stages_fn,
    50→            ),
    51→        )
    52→        if start_outcome.status == "blocked":
    53→            return
    54→
    55→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
    56→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
    57→    existing_stage = stages.get("observe")
    58→
    59→    report, is_reuse = resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
    60→    if not report:
    61→        _print_observe_report_requirement()
    62→        return
    63→
    64→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
    65→    review_issues = getattr(si, "review_issues", getattr(si, "open_issues", {}))
    66→    issue_count = len(review_issues)
    67→    if issue_count == 0:
    68→        cleared = record_observe_stage(
    69→            stages,
    70→            report=report,
    71→            issue_count=0,
    72→            cited_ids=[],
    73→            existing_stage=existing_stage,
    74→            is_reuse=is_reuse,
    75→            dimension_names=[],
    76→            dimension_counts={},
    77→        )
    78→        resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
    79→        print(colorize("  Observe stage recorded (no issues to analyse).", "green"))
    80→        if is_reuse:
    81→            print(colorize("  Observe data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
    82→            if cleared:
    83→                print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
    84→        return
    85→
    86→    by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(si)
    87→    if not validate_stage_report_length(
    88→        report=report,
    89→        issue_count=issue_count,
    90→        guidance=(
    91→            "  Verify each issue with code evidence, record the verdicts you reached,"
    92→            " and cite the files you read."
    93→        ),
    94→    ):
    95→        return
    96→
    97→    from .evidence_parsing import (
    98→        format_evidence_failures,
    99→        parse_observe_evidence,
   100→        resolve_short_hash_to_full_id,
   101→        validate_observe_evidence,
   102→    )
   103→
   104→    valid_ids = set(review_issues.keys())
   105→    cited = resolved_services.extract_issue_citations(report, valid_ids)
   106→    evidence = parse_observe_evidence(report, valid_ids)
   107→    evidence_failures = validate_observe_evidence(evidence, issue_count)
   108→    blocking = [failure for failure in evidence_failures if failure.blocking]
   109→    advisory = [failure for failure in evidence_failures if not failure.blocking]
   110→    if blocking:
   111→        print(colorize(format_evidence_failures(blocking, stage_label="observe"), "red"))
   112→        return
   113→    if advisory:
   114→        print(colorize(format_evidence_failures(advisory, stage_label="observe"), "yellow"))
   115→
   116→    assessments = [
   117→        {
   118→            "hash": entry.issue_hash,
   119→            "verdict": entry.verdict,
   120→            "verdict_reasoning": entry.verdict_reasoning,
   121→            "files_read": entry.files_read,
   122→            "recommendation": entry.recommendation,
   123→        }
   124→        for entry in evidence.entries
   125→    ]
   126→
   127→    # On fresh observe run, cascade-clear dispositions and undo auto-skips
   128→    if not is_reuse:
   129→        from ..confirmations.basic import _undo_observe_auto_skips
   130→
   131→        _undo_observe_auto_skips(plan, meta)
   132→        cascade_clear_dispositions(meta, "observe")
   133→
   134→    # Populate issue_dispositions from assessments using collision-aware resolution
   135→    dispositions: dict[str, dict] = {}
   136→    for entry in evidence.entries:
   137→        full_id = resolve_short_hash_to_full_id(entry.issue_hash, valid_ids)
   138→        if full_id:
   139→            dispositions[full_id] = {
   140→                "verdict": entry.verdict,
   141→                "verdict_reasoning": entry.verdict_reasoning,
   142→                "files_read": entry.files_read,
   143→                "recommendation": entry.recommendation,
   144→            }
   145→    meta["issue_dispositions"] = dispositions
   146→
   147→    cleared = record_observe_stage(
   148→        stages,
   149→        report=report,
   150→        issue_count=issue_count,
   151→        cited_ids=sorted(cited),
   152→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   153→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   154→        assessments=assessments,
   155→        dimension_names=dim_names,
   156→        dimension_counts=by_dim,
   157→    )
   158→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   159→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   160→        plan,
   161→        "triage_observe",
   162→        actor="user",
   163→        detail={"issue_count": issue_count, "cited_ids": sorted(cited), "reuse": is_reuse},
   164→    )
   165→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   166→
   167→    print(colorize(f"  Observe stage recorded: {issue_count} issues analysed.", "green"))
   168→    if is_reuse:
   169→        print(colorize("  Observe data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   170→        if cleared:
   171→            print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   172→        return
   173→
   174→    print(colorize("  Now confirm your analysis.", "yellow"))
   175→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm observe", "dim"))
   176→    print_user_message(
   177→        "Observe recorded. Before confirming — did the subagent"
   178→        " verify every issue with code reads? Check: are there"
   179→        " specific file/line citations in the report, or just"
   180→        " restated issue titles? Each issue needs a verdict:"
   181→        " genuine / false positive / exaggerated / not-worth-it. Don't confirm"
   182→        " until the analysis is backed by actual code evidence."
   183→    )
   184→
   185→_cmd_stage_observe = cmd_stage_observe
   186→
   187→
   188→__all__ = ["_cmd_stage_observe", "cmd_stage_observe"]
```

```
1→"""Reflect stage command flow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→from desloppify.state_io import utc_now
     9→
    10→from ..display.dashboard import print_reflect_result
    11→from ..stage_queue import cascade_clear_dispositions, cascade_clear_later_confirmations, has_triage_in_queue
    12→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    13→from ..validation.reflect_accounting import (
    14→    ReflectDisposition,
    15→    parse_reflect_dispositions,
    16→    validate_reflect_accounting,
    17→)
    18→from ..validation.stage_policy import auto_confirm_observe_if_attested
    19→from .flow_helpers import validate_stage_report_length
    20→from .records import resolve_reusable_report
    21→from .rendering import _print_reflect_report_requirement
    22→
    23→
    24→def _validate_recurring_dimension_mentions(
    25→    *,
    26→    report: str,
    27→    recurring_dims: list[str],
    28→    recurring: dict[str, dict[str, list[str]]],
    29→) -> bool:
    30→    if not recurring_dims:
    31→        return True
    32→    report_lower = report.lower()
    33→    mentioned = [dim for dim in recurring_dims if dim.lower() in report_lower]
    34→    if mentioned:
    35→        return True
    36→    print(colorize("  Recurring patterns detected but not addressed in report:", "red"))
    37→    for dim in recurring_dims:
    38→        info = recurring[dim]
    39→        print(
    40→            colorize(
    41→                f"    {dim}: {len(info['resolved'])} resolved, "
    42→                f"{len(info['open'])} still open — potential loop",
    43→                "yellow",
    44→            )
    45→        )
    46→    print(colorize("  Your report must mention at least one recurring dimension name.", "dim"))
    47→    return False
    48→
    49→
    50→def _validate_reflect_submission(
    51→    *,
    52→    report: str,
    53→    plan: dict,
    54→    state: dict,
    55→    stages: dict,
    56→    attestation: str | None,
    57→    services: TriageServices,
    58→) -> tuple[object, int, dict, list[str], set[str], list[str], list[str], list[ReflectDisposition]] | None:
    59→    if "observe" not in stages:
    60→        print(colorize("  Cannot reflect: observe stage not complete.", "red"))
    61→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
    62→        return None
    63→
    64→    triage_input = services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
    65→    if not auto_confirm_observe_if_attested(
    66→        plan=plan,
    67→        stages=stages,
    68→        attestation=attestation,
    69→        triage_input=triage_input,
    70→        save_plan_fn=services.save_plan,
    71→    ):
    72→        return None
    73→
    74→    review_issues = getattr(triage_input, "review_issues", getattr(triage_input, "open_issues", {}))
    75→    issue_count = len(review_issues)
    76→    if not validate_stage_report_length(
    77→        report=report,
    78→        issue_count=issue_count,
    79→        guidance="  Describe how current issues relate to previously completed work.",
    80→    ):
    81→        return None
    82→
    83→    recurring = services.detect_recurring_patterns(
    84→        review_issues,
    85→        triage_input.resolved_issues,
    86→    )
    87→    recurring_dims = sorted(recurring.keys())
    88→    if not _validate_recurring_dimension_mentions(
    89→        report=report,
    90→        recurring_dims=recurring_dims,
    91→        recurring=recurring,
    92→    ):
    93→        return None
    94→
    95→    valid_ids = set(review_issues.keys())
    96→
    97→    # Exclude issues already auto-skipped by observe from reflect accounting
    98→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    99→    dispositions = meta.get("issue_dispositions", {})
   100→    auto_skipped_ids = {
   101→        issue_id for issue_id, disp in dispositions.items()
   102→        if disp.get("decision_source") == "observe_auto"
   103→    }
   104→    accounting_ids = valid_ids - auto_skipped_ids
   105→
   106→    accounting_ok, cited_ids, missing_ids, duplicate_ids = validate_reflect_accounting(
   107→        report=report,
   108→        valid_ids=accounting_ids,
   109→    )
   110→    if not accounting_ok:
   111→        return None
   112→
   113→    from .evidence_parsing import (
   114→        format_evidence_failures,
   115→        validate_reflect_skip_evidence,
   116→    )
   117→
   118→    blocking_skips = [
   119→        failure
   120→        for failure in validate_reflect_skip_evidence(report)
   121→        if failure.blocking
   122→    ]
   123→    if blocking_skips:
   124→        print(colorize(format_evidence_failures(blocking_skips, stage_label="reflect"), "red"))
   125→        return None
   126→
   127→    # Parse structured disposition ledger from Coverage Ledger section
   128→    disposition_ledger = parse_reflect_dispositions(report, valid_ids)
   129→
   130→    return (
   131→        triage_input,
   132→        issue_count,
   133→        recurring,
   134→        recurring_dims,
   135→        cited_ids,
   136→        missing_ids,
   137→        duplicate_ids,
   138→        disposition_ledger,
   139→    )
   140→
   141→
   142→def _persist_reflect_stage(
   143→    *,
   144→    plan: dict,
   145→    meta: dict,
   146→    stages: dict,
   147→    report: str,
   148→    issue_count: int,
   149→    cited_ids: set[str],
   150→    missing_ids: list[str],
   151→    duplicate_ids: list[str],
   152→    recurring_dims: list[str],
   153→    disposition_ledger: list[ReflectDisposition],
   154→    existing_stage: dict | None,
   155→    is_reuse: bool,
   156→    services: TriageServices,
   157→) -> tuple[dict, list[str]]:
   158→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   159→
   160→    # On fresh reflect run, cascade-clear reflect decisions from dispositions
   161→    if not is_reuse:
   162→        cascade_clear_dispositions(meta, "reflect")
   163→
   164→    reflect_stage: dict = {
   165→        "stage": "reflect",
   166→        "report": report,
   167→        "cited_ids": sorted(cited_ids),
   168→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   169→        "issue_count": issue_count,
   170→        "missing_issue_ids": missing_ids,
   171→        "duplicate_issue_ids": duplicate_ids,
   172→        "recurring_dims": recurring_dims,
   173→    }
   174→    if disposition_ledger:
   175→        reflect_stage["disposition_ledger"] = [d.to_dict() for d in disposition_ledger]
   176→        # Write reflect decisions to the disposition map
   177→        dispositions = meta.setdefault("issue_dispositions", {})
   178→        for d in disposition_ledger:
   179→            entry = dispositions.setdefault(d.issue_id, {})
   180→            decision = "skip" if d.decision == "permanent_skip" else d.decision
   181→            entry["decision"] = decision
   182→            entry["target"] = d.target
   183→            entry["decision_source"] = "reflect"
   184→
   185→    stages["reflect"] = reflect_stage
   186→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
   187→        reflect_stage["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
   188→        reflect_stage["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
   189→    cleared = cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "reflect")
   190→
   191→    services.save_plan(plan)
   192→    services.append_log_entry(
   193→        plan,
   194→        "triage_reflect",
   195→        actor="user",
   196→        detail={"issue_count": issue_count, "reuse": is_reuse, "recurring_dims": recurring_dims},
   197→    )
   198→    services.save_plan(plan)
   199→    return reflect_stage, cleared
   200→
   201→
   202→def _cmd_stage_reflect(
   203→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   204→    *,
   205→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   206→) -> None:
   207→    """Record the REFLECT stage: compare current issues against completed work."""
   208→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   209→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   210→
   211→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   212→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   213→    state = runtime.state
   214→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   215→
   216→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   217→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to reflect on.", "yellow"))
   218→        return
   219→
   220→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   221→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   222→    existing_stage = stages.get("reflect")
   223→
   224→    report, is_reuse = resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
   225→    if not report:
   226→        _print_reflect_report_requirement()
   227→        return
   228→
   229→    submission = _validate_reflect_submission(
   230→        report=report,
   231→        plan=plan,
   232→        state=state,
   233→        stages=stages,
   234→        attestation=attestation,
   235→        services=resolved_services,
   236→    )
   237→    if submission is None:
   238→        return
   239→    (
   240→        triage_input, issue_count, recurring, recurring_dims,
   241→        cited_ids, missing_ids, duplicate_ids, disposition_ledger,
   242→    ) = submission
   243→    reflect_stage, cleared = _persist_reflect_stage(
   244→        plan=plan,
   245→        meta=meta,
   246→        stages=stages,
   247→        report=report,
   248→        issue_count=issue_count,
   249→        cited_ids=cited_ids,
   250→        missing_ids=missing_ids,
   251→        duplicate_ids=duplicate_ids,
   252→        recurring_dims=recurring_dims,
   253→        disposition_ledger=disposition_ledger,
   254→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   255→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   256→        services=resolved_services,
   257→    )
   258→
   259→    print_reflect_result(
   260→        issue_count=issue_count,
   261→        recurring_dims=recurring_dims,
   262→        recurring=recurring,
   263→        report=report,
   264→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   265→        cleared=cleared,
   266→        stages=stages,
   267→    )
   268→
   269→
   270→def cmd_stage_reflect(
   271→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   272→    *,
   273→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   274→) -> None:
   275→    """Public entrypoint for reflect stage recording."""
   276→    _cmd_stage_reflect(args, services=services)
   277→
   278→
   279→__all__ = ["_cmd_stage_reflect", "cmd_stage_reflect"]
```

```
1→"""Organize stage command flow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→
     9→from ..display.dashboard import print_organize_result
    10→from ..completion_flow import count_log_activity_since
    11→from ..review_coverage import open_review_ids_from_state
    12→from ..stage_queue import has_triage_in_queue
    13→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    14→from ..validation.stage_policy import (
    15→    ReflectAutoConfirmDeps,
    16→    auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize,
    17→)
    18→from ..validation.organize_policy import (
    19→    _clusters_enriched_or_error,
    20→    _manual_clusters_or_error,
    21→    _organize_report_or_error,
    22→    _unclustered_review_issues_or_error,
    23→    _validate_organize_against_ledger_or_error,
    24→)
    25→from ..validation.stage_policy import require_prerequisite
    26→from .records import record_organize_stage
    27→
    28→
    29→def _require_reflect_stage_for_organize(stages: dict) -> bool:
    30→    return require_prerequisite(
    31→        stages,
    32→        flow="organize",
    33→        messages={
    34→            "observe": (
    35→                "  Cannot organize: observe stage not complete.",
    36→                '  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."',
    37→            ),
    38→            "reflect": (
    39→                "  Cannot organize: reflect stage not complete.",
    40→                '  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "..."',
    41→            ),
    42→        },
    43→    )
    44→
    45→
    46→def _enforce_cluster_activity_for_organize(
    47→    *,
    48→    plan: dict,
    49→    stages: dict,
    50→    manual_clusters: list[str],
    51→    open_review_ids: set[str],
    52→    is_reuse: bool,
    53→    attestation: str | None,
    54→) -> bool:
    55→    """Require enough cluster operations after reflect unless explicitly attested."""
    56→    if not open_review_ids:
    57→        return True
    58→    reflect_ts = stages.get("reflect", {}).get("timestamp", "")
    59→    if not reflect_ts or is_reuse:
    60→        return True
    61→    activity = count_log_activity_since(plan, reflect_ts)
    62→    cluster_ops = sum(
    63→        activity.get(key, 0)
    64→        for key in ("cluster_create", "cluster_add", "cluster_update", "cluster_remove")
    65→    )
    66→    min_ops = max(3, len(manual_clusters))
    67→    if cluster_ops >= min_ops:
    68→        return True
    69→    if attestation and len(attestation.strip()) >= 40:
    70→        print(
    71→            colorize(
    72→                f"  Note: only {cluster_ops} cluster op(s) logged (expected {min_ops}+). "
    73→                "Proceeding with attestation override.",
    74→                "yellow",
    75→            )
    76→        )
    77→        return True
    78→    print(
    79→        colorize(
    80→            f"  Cannot organize: only {cluster_ops} cluster operation(s) logged "
    81→            f"since reflect (need {min_ops}+).",
    82→            "red",
    83→        )
    84→    )
    85→    print(
    86→        colorize(
    87→            "  Cluster operations (create/add/update/remove) are logged automatically\n"
    88→            "  when you use the CLI. Did you create clusters, add issues, and enrich them?",
    89→            "dim",
    90→        )
    91→    )
    92→    print(
    93→        colorize(
    94→            '  Override: pass --attestation "reason why fewer ops are expected" (40+ chars).',
    95→            "dim",
    96→        )
    97→    )
    98→    return False
    99→
   100→
   101→def _validate_organize_submission(
   102→    *,
   103→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   104→    plan: dict,
   105→    state: dict,
   106→    stages: dict,
   107→    report: str | None,
   108→    attestation: str | None,
   109→    is_reuse: bool,
   110→    services: TriageServices,
   111→) -> tuple[list[str], str] | None:
   112→    open_review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
   113→    triage_input = services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   114→    if not auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize(
   115→        args=args,
   116→        plan=plan,
   117→        stages=stages,
   118→        attestation=attestation,
   119→        deps=ReflectAutoConfirmDeps(
   120→            triage_input=triage_input,
   121→            detect_recurring_patterns_fn=services.detect_recurring_patterns,
   122→            save_plan_fn=services.save_plan,
   123→        ),
   124→    ):
   125→        return None
   126→
   127→    manual_clusters = _manual_clusters_or_error(plan, open_review_ids=open_review_ids)
   128→    if manual_clusters is None:
   129→        return None
   130→    if not _clusters_enriched_or_error(plan):
   131→        return None
   132→    if not _unclustered_review_issues_or_error(plan, state):
   133→        return None
   134→    if not _validate_organize_against_ledger_or_error(
   135→        plan=plan, stages=stages,
   136→    ):
   137→        return None
   138→    if not _enforce_cluster_activity_for_organize(
   139→        plan=plan,
   140→        stages=stages,
   141→        manual_clusters=manual_clusters,
   142→        open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   143→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   144→        attestation=attestation,
   145→    ):
   146→        return None
   147→
   148→    normalized_report = _organize_report_or_error(report)
   149→    if normalized_report is None:
   150→        return None
   151→
   152→    from .evidence_parsing import (
   153→        format_evidence_failures,
   154→        validate_report_references_clusters,
   155→    )
   156→
   157→    cluster_ref_failures = validate_report_references_clusters(normalized_report, manual_clusters)
   158→    if cluster_ref_failures:
   159→        print(
   160→            colorize(
   161→                format_evidence_failures(cluster_ref_failures, stage_label="organize"),
   162→                "red",
   163→            )
   164→        )
   165→        return None
   166→    return manual_clusters, normalized_report
   167→
   168→
   169→def _persist_organize_stage(
   170→    *,
   171→    plan: dict,
   172→    meta: dict,
   173→    report: str,
   174→    open_review_ids: set[str],
   175→    existing_stage: dict | None,
   176→    is_reuse: bool,
   177→    manual_clusters: list[str],
   178→    services: TriageServices,
   179→) -> tuple[list[str], dict]:
   180→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   181→    cleared = record_organize_stage(
   182→        stages,
   183→        report=report,
   184→        issue_count=len(open_review_ids),
   185→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   186→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   187→    )
   188→    services.save_plan(plan)
   189→    services.append_log_entry(
   190→        plan,
   191→        "triage_organize",
   192→        actor="user",
   193→        detail={"cluster_count": len(manual_clusters), "reuse": is_reuse},
   194→    )
   195→    services.save_plan(plan)
   196→    return cleared, stages
   197→
   198→
   199→def _cmd_stage_organize(
   200→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   201→    *,
   202→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   203→) -> None:
   204→    """Record the ORGANIZE stage: validates cluster enrichment."""
   205→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   206→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   207→
   208→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   209→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   210→
   211→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   212→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to organize.", "yellow"))
   213→        return
   214→
   215→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   216→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   217→    existing_stage = stages.get("organize")
   218→
   219→    is_reuse = False
   220→    if not report and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
   221→        report = existing_stage["report"]
   222→        is_reuse = True
   223→
   224→    if not _require_reflect_stage_for_organize(stages):
   225→        return
   226→
   227→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   228→    state = runtime.state
   229→    open_review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
   230→
   231→    validated = _validate_organize_submission(
   232→        args=args,
   233→        plan=plan,
   234→        state=state,
   235→        stages=stages,
   236→        report=report,
   237→        attestation=attestation,
   238→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   239→        services=resolved_services,
   240→    )
   241→    if validated is None:
   242→        return
   243→    manual_clusters, normalized_report = validated
   244→    cleared, stages = _persist_organize_stage(
   245→        plan=plan,
   246→        meta=meta,
   247→        report=normalized_report,
   248→        open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   249→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   250→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   251→        manual_clusters=manual_clusters,
   252→        services=resolved_services,
   253→    )
   254→
   255→    print_organize_result(
   256→        manual_clusters=manual_clusters,
   257→        plan=plan,
   258→        report=normalized_report,
   259→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   260→        cleared=cleared,
   261→        stages=stages,
   262→    )
   263→
   264→
   265→def cmd_stage_organize(
   266→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   267→    *,
   268→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   269→) -> None:
   270→    """Public entrypoint for organize stage recording."""
   271→    _cmd_stage_organize(args, services=services)
```

```
    57→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    58→    services: TriageServices | None,

    60→) -> tuple[TriageServices, dict, dict, dict, str | None, str | None]:

   108→def _require_cluster_update_activity(

   223→def run_stage_enrich(
   224→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   225→    *,
   226→    services: TriageServices | None,
   227→    deps: EnrichStageDeps | None = None,
   228→) -> None:
   229→    """Record the ENRICH stage with validation and optional auto-confirm."""
   230→    resolved_deps = deps or EnrichStageDeps()
   231→    resolved_services, plan, state, stages, report, attestation = _resolve_enrich_stage_context(
   232→        args=args,
   233→        services=services,
   234→        deps=resolved_deps,
   235→    )
   236→
   237→    if not resolved_deps.has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   238→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to enrich.", "yellow"))
   239→        return
   240→
   241→    existing_stage = stages.get("enrich")
   242→    report, is_reuse = resolved_deps.resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
   243→
   244→    if not resolved_deps.require_organize_stage_for_enrich(stages):
   245→        return
   246→
   247→    if not _require_confirmed_organize_stage(
   248→        plan=plan,
   249→        stages=stages,
   250→        attestation=attestation,
   251→        services=resolved_services,
   252→        deps=resolved_deps,
   253→    ):
   254→        return
   255→
   256→    if not is_reuse and not _require_cluster_update_activity(
   257→        plan=plan,
   258→        state=state,
   259→        stages=stages,
   260→        attestation=attestation,
   261→        deps=resolved_deps,
   262→    ):
   263→        return
   264→
   265→    get_project_root = resolved_deps.get_project_root
   266→    if get_project_root is None:
   267→        from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
   268→
   269→    triage_ids = active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state) or None
   270→    quality_report = evaluate_enrich_quality(
   271→        plan,
   272→        get_project_root(),
   273→        phase_label="enrich",
   274→        bad_paths_severity="warning",
   275→        missing_effort_severity="warning",
   276→        include_missing_issue_refs=False,
   277→        include_vague_detail=False,
   278→        stale_issue_refs_severity=None,
   279→        triage_issue_ids=triage_ids,
   280→    )
   281→    underspec = quality_report.failure("underspecified")
   282→    total_bare = underspec.total if underspec else 0
   283→
   284→    if underspec:
   285→        _print_underspecified_step_error(underspec.rows, deps=resolved_deps)
   286→        return
   287→
   288→    print(resolved_deps.colorize("  All steps have detail or issue_refs.", "green"))
   289→    _print_enrich_warnings(report=quality_report, deps=resolved_deps)
   290→
   291→    report = resolved_deps.enrich_report_or_error(report)
   292→    if report is None:
   293→        return
   294→
   295→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
   296→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   297→    cleared = resolved_deps.record_enrich_stage(
   298→        stages,
   299→        report=report,
   300→        shallow_count=total_bare,
   301→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   302→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   303→    )
   304→
   305→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   306→
   307→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   308→        plan,
   309→        "triage_enrich",
   310→        actor="user",
   311→        detail={"shallow_count": total_bare, "reuse": is_reuse},
   312→    )
   313→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   314→
   315→    print(
   316→        resolved_deps.colorize(
   317→            f"  Enrich stage recorded: {total_bare} step(s) still without detail.",
   318→            "green",
   319→        )
   320→    )
   321→    if is_reuse:
   322→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Enrich data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   323→        if cleared:
   324→            resolved_deps.print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   325→    else:
   326→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Now confirm the enrichment.", "yellow"))
   327→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm enrich", "dim"))
   328→
   329→    resolved_deps.print_user_message(
   330→        "Enrich recorded. Before confirming — check the subagent's"
   331→        " work. Could a developer who has never seen this code"
   332→        " execute every step without asking a question? Every step"
   333→        " needs: file path, specific location, specific action."
   334→        " 'Refactor X' fails. 'Extract lines 45-89 into Y' passes."
   335→    )
   336→
   337→
   338→def cmd_stage_enrich(
   339→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   340→    *,
   341→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   342→) -> None:
   343→    """Public entrypoint for enrich stage recording."""
   344→    run_stage_enrich(args, services=services)
   345→
   346→
   347→__all__ = ["ColorizeFn", "EnrichStageDeps", "cmd_stage_enrich", "run_stage_enrich"]
   348→
```

```
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from collections.abc import Callable
     7→from dataclasses import dataclass

     9→
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
```

```
    25→ColorizeFn = Callable[[str, str], str]
    26→
    27→
    28→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    29→class ValueCheckStageDeps:
    30→    """Dependency container for focused value-check stage testing."""
    31→
    32→    has_triage_in_queue: Callable[[dict], bool] = has_triage_in_queue
    33→    resolve_reusable_report: Callable[[str | None, dict | None], tuple[str | None, bool]] = (
    34→        resolve_reusable_report
    35→    )
    36→    record_value_check_stage: Callable[..., list[str]] = record_value_check_stage
    37→    value_check_targets: Callable[[dict, dict | None], list[str]] = value_check_targets
    38→    manual_clusters_with_issues: Callable[[dict], list[str]] = manual_clusters_with_issues
    39→    colorize: ColorizeFn = colorize
    40→    default_triage_services: Callable[[], TriageServices] = default_triage_services
    41→    print_cascade_clear_feedback: Callable[[list[str], dict], None] = print_cascade_clear_feedback
    42→    get_project_root: Callable[[], Path] | None = None
    43→
    44→
    45→def _report_length_ok(report: str | None, *, colorize_fn: ColorizeFn) -> bool:
    46→    if not report:
    47→        print(colorize_fn("  --report is required for --stage value-check.", "red"))
    48→        print(
    49→            colorize_fn(
    50→                "  Include a Decision Ledger with one line per live queue target: keep, tighten, or skip.",
    51→                "dim",
    52→            )
    53→        )
    54→        return False
    55→    if len(report.strip()) < 100:
    56→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Report too short: {len(report.strip())} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
    57→        return False
    58→    return True
    59→
    60→
    61→def _quality_problems(plan: dict, *, deps: ValueCheckStageDeps) -> list[str]:
    62→    get_project_root = deps.get_project_root
    63→    if get_project_root is None:
    64→        from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    65→
    66→    repo_root = get_project_root()
    67→    quality_report = evaluate_enrich_quality(
    68→        plan,
    69→        repo_root,
    70→        phase_label="sense-check",
    71→        bad_paths_severity="warning",
    72→        missing_effort_severity="failure",
    73→        include_missing_issue_refs=True,
    74→        include_vague_detail=True,
    75→        stale_issue_refs_severity=None,
    76→    )
    77→    return [issue.message for issue in quality_report.failures]
    78→
    79→
    80→def _ledger_failures(report: str, *, targets: list[str]) -> list[str]:
    81→    parsed = parse_value_check_decision_ledger(report)
    82→    failures: list[str] = []
    83→    if not parsed.entries:
    84→        failures.append(
    85→            "Report is missing a `## Decision Ledger` with `- cluster-or-id -> keep|tighten|skip` lines."
    86→        )
    87→        return failures
    88→    if parsed.duplicates:
    89→        dupes = ", ".join(parsed.duplicates[:5])
    90→        failures.append(f"Decision Ledger repeats target(s): {dupes}")
    91→    missing = [target for target in targets if target not in parsed.entries]
    92→    extra = [target for target in parsed.entries if target not in targets]
    93→    if missing:
    94→        failures.append(
    95→            "Decision Ledger is missing target(s): " + ", ".join(missing[:5])
    96→        )
    97→    if extra:
    98→        failures.append(
    99→            "Decision Ledger references non-live target(s): " + ", ".join(extra[:5])
   100→        )
   101→    return failures
   102→
   103→
   104→def run_stage_value_check(
   105→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   106→    *,
   107→    services: TriageServices | None,
   108→    deps: ValueCheckStageDeps | None = None,
   109→) -> None:
   110→    """Record the VALUE-CHECK stage after final keep/tighten/skip review."""
   111→    resolved_deps = deps or ValueCheckStageDeps()
   112→    resolved_services = services or resolved_deps.default_triage_services()
   113→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   114→    state = resolved_services.command_runtime(args).state
   115→
   116→    if not resolved_deps.has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   117→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to value-check.", "yellow"))
   118→        return
   119→
   120→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   121→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   122→    existing_stage = stages.get("value-check")
   123→    report = getattr(args, "report", None)
   124→    report, is_reuse = resolved_deps.resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
   125→
   126→    if not stages.get("sense-check", {}).get("confirmed_at"):
   127→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Cannot value-check: sense-check stage not confirmed.", "red"))
   128→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Run: desloppify plan triage --confirm sense-check", "dim"))
   129→        return
   130→
   131→    problems = _quality_problems(plan, deps=resolved_deps)
   132→    if problems:
   133→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Cannot record value-check — plan still has issues:", "red"))
   134→        for problem in problems:
   135→            print(resolved_deps.colorize(f"    • {problem}", "yellow"))
   136→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Fix those before recording the final keep/skip pass.", "dim"))
   137→        return
   138→
   139→    if not _report_length_ok(report, colorize_fn=resolved_deps.colorize):
   140→        return
   141→
   142→    targets = resolved_deps.value_check_targets(plan, state)
   143→    ledger_failures = _ledger_failures(report, targets=targets)
   144→    if ledger_failures:
   145→        for failure in ledger_failures:
   146→            print(resolved_deps.colorize(f"  {failure}", "red"))
   147→        return
   148→
   149→    evidence_failures = validate_report_has_file_paths(report)
   150→    cluster_names = resolved_deps.manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   151→    evidence_failures.extend(validate_report_references_clusters(report, cluster_names))
   152→    blocking = [failure for failure in evidence_failures if failure.blocking]
   153→    if blocking:
   154→        print(
   155→            resolved_deps.colorize(
   156→                format_evidence_failures(blocking, stage_label="value-check"),
   157→                "red",
   158→            )
   159→        )
   160→        return
   161→
   162→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   163→    cleared = resolved_deps.record_value_check_stage(
   164→        stages,
   165→        report=report,
   166→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   167→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   168→    )
   169→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   170→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   171→        plan,
   172→        "triage_value_check",
   173→        actor="user",
   174→        detail={"target_count": len(targets), "reuse": is_reuse},
   175→    )
   176→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   177→
   178→    print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Value-check stage recorded.", "green"))
   179→    if is_reuse:
   180→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Value-check data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   181→        if cleared:
   182→            resolved_deps.print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   183→    else:
   184→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Now confirm the value-check.", "yellow"))
   185→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm value-check", "dim"))
   186→
   187→
   188→def cmd_stage_value_check(
   189→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   190→    *,
   191→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   192→) -> None:
   193→    """Public entrypoint for value-check stage recording."""
   194→    run_stage_value_check(args, services=services)
   195→
   196→
   197→__all__ = [
   198→    "ValueCheckStageDeps",
   199→    "cmd_stage_value_check",
   200→    "run_stage_value_check",
   201→]
   202→
```

```
    44→def _print_completion_coverage_warning(*, organized: int, total: int) -> None:
    45→    """Print warnings for incomplete cluster coverage at completion time."""
    46→    if total <= 0:
    47→        return
    48→    if organized == 0:
    49→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: no issues have been organized into clusters.", "red"))
    50→        print(colorize(f"  {total} issues are waiting.", "dim"))
    51→        return
    52→    if organized < total:
    53→        remaining = total - organized
    54→        print(
    55→            colorize(
    56→                f"  Warning: {remaining}/{total} issues are not yet in any cluster.",
    57→                "yellow",
    58→            )
    59→        )
    60→
    61→
    62→def _print_completion_jump_back_guidance() -> None:
    63→    """Print the post-summary guidance for revisiting earlier triage stages."""
    64→    print()
    65→    print(
    66→        colorize(
    67→            "  To revise an earlier stage: desloppify plan triage --stage <observe|reflect|organize|enrich|sense-check|value-check>",
    68→            "dim",
    69→        )
    70→    )
    71→    print(colorize("  Pass --report to update, or omit to keep existing analysis.", "dim"))
    72→

    74→def _record_incomplete_recovery(
    75→    *,
    76→    plan: dict,
    77→    state: dict,
    78→    services: TriageServices,
    79→    reason: str,
    80→) -> list[str]:
    81→    """Persist the current missing-disposition set for recovery guidance."""
    82→    missing = sync_undispositioned_triage_meta(plan, state)
    83→    services.save_plan(plan)
    84→    if missing:
    85→        print(
    86→            colorize(
    87→                f"  Triage recovery blocked: {len(missing)} issue(s) still need cluster/skip dispositions ({reason}).",
    88→                "yellow",
    89→            )
    90→        )
    91→        for issue_id in missing[:5]:
    92→            print(colorize(f"    {issue_id}", "dim"))
    93→        if len(missing) > 5:
    94→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(missing) - 5} more", "dim"))
    95→    return missing
    96→

    98→def _resolve_confirm_existing_context(
    99→    *,
   100→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   101→    plan: dict,
   102→    services: TriageServices,
   103→    stages: dict,
   104→) -> tuple[object, bool] | None:
   105→    """Load confirm-existing triage context and validate the stage prerequisites."""
   106→    runtime = services.command_runtime(args)
   107→    state = runtime.state
   108→    si = services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   109→    has_only_additions = bool(si.new_since_last) and not si.resolved_since_last
   110→    if not _confirm_existing_stages_valid(
   111→        stages=stages,
   112→        has_only_additions=has_only_additions,
   113→        si=si,
   114→    ):
   115→        return None
   116→    return si, has_only_additions
   117→
   118→
   119→def _resolve_confirm_existing_inputs(
   120→    *,
   121→    note: str | None,
   122→    strategy: str | None,
   123→    confirmed: str | None,
   124→    plan: dict,
   125→    state: dict,
   126→    meta: dict,
   127→    si,
   128→    has_only_additions: bool,
   129→) -> tuple[str, str, str] | None:
   130→    """Validate and resolve note/strategy/confirmed inputs for confirm-existing."""
   131→    if not _confirm_note_valid(note):
   132→        return None
   133→    resolved_strategy = _resolve_confirm_existing_strategy(
   134→        strategy,
   135→        has_only_additions=has_only_additions,
   136→        meta=meta,
   137→    )
   138→    if resolved_strategy is None:
   139→        return None
   140→    if not _confirm_strategy_valid(resolved_strategy):
   141→        return None
   142→    confirmed_text = _confirmed_text_or_error(
   143→        plan=plan,
   144→        state=state,
   145→        confirmed=confirmed,
   146→    )
   147→    if confirmed_text is None:
   148→        return None
   149→    if not _note_cites_new_issues_or_error(note, si):
   150→        return None
   151→    return note or "", resolved_strategy, confirmed_text
   152→
   153→
   154→def _cmd_triage_complete(
   155→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   156→    *,
   157→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   158→) -> None:
   159→    """Complete triage — requires organize stage (or confirm-existing path)."""
   160→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   161→    strategy: str | None = getattr(args, "strategy", None)
   162→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   163→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   164→
   165→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   166→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to complete.", "yellow"))
   167→        return
   168→
   169→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   170→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   171→
   172→    state = resolved_services.command_runtime(args).state
   173→    review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
   174→
   175→    # Organize gate
   176→    if not _require_organize_stage_for_complete(plan=plan, meta=meta, stages=stages):
   177→        return
   178→    if not _auto_confirm_stage_for_complete(
   179→        plan=plan, stages=stages, stage="organize",
   180→        attestation=attestation, save_plan_fn=resolved_services.save_plan,
   181→    ):
   182→        return
   183→
   184→    # Enrich gate — compute underspecified steps once, share across require + confirm
   185→    underspec = _underspecified_steps(plan)
   186→    if not _require_enrich_stage_for_complete(
   187→        plan=plan, meta=meta, stages=stages, underspec=underspec,
   188→    ):
   189→        return
   190→    if not _auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete(
   191→        plan=plan, stages=stages, attestation=attestation,
   192→        save_plan_fn=resolved_services.save_plan, underspec=underspec,
   193→    ):
   194→        return
   195→
   196→    # Sense-check gate
   197→    if not _require_sense_check_stage_for_complete(plan=plan, meta=meta, stages=stages):
   198→        return
   199→    if not _auto_confirm_stage_for_complete(
   200→        plan=plan, stages=stages, stage="sense-check",
   201→        attestation=attestation, save_plan_fn=resolved_services.save_plan,
   202→    ):
   203→        return
   204→
   205→    # Value-check gate
   206→    if not _require_value_check_stage_for_complete(plan=plan, meta=meta, stages=stages):
   207→        return
   208→    if not _auto_confirm_stage_for_complete(
   209→        plan=plan, stages=stages, stage="value-check",
   210→        attestation=attestation, save_plan_fn=resolved_services.save_plan,
   211→    ):
   212→        return
   213→
   214→    if not _completion_clusters_valid(plan, state):
   215→        _record_incomplete_recovery(
   216→            plan=plan,
   217→            state=state,
   218→            services=resolved_services,
   219→            reason="completion validation failed",
   220→        )
   221→        return
   222→
   223→    organized, total, _clusters = triage_coverage(plan, open_review_ids=review_ids)
   224→    if total > 0 and organized == 0:
   225→        _print_completion_coverage_warning(organized=organized, total=total)
   226→        return
   227→    _print_completion_coverage_warning(organized=organized, total=total)
   228→
   229→    strategy = _resolve_completion_strategy(strategy, meta=meta)
   230→    if strategy is None:
   231→        return
   232→    if not _completion_strategy_valid(strategy):
   233→        return
   234→
   235→    # Show summary
   236→    _print_complete_summary(plan, stages)
   237→
   238→    organized, total, _ = triage_coverage(plan, open_review_ids=review_ids)
   239→
   240→    _print_completion_jump_back_guidance()
   241→
   242→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   243→        plan,
   244→        "triage_complete",
   245→        actor="user",
   246→        detail={
   247→            "strategy_len": len(strategy.strip()),
   248→            "coverage": f"{organized}/{total}",

   250→    )
   251→
   252→    apply_completion(args, plan, strategy, services=resolved_services)
   253→
   254→    print_user_message(
   255→        "Hey — triage is done. Run `desloppify next` and start"
   256→        " executing the plan. Don't stop until you're done —"
   257→        " unless I've told you otherwise, just go."
   258→    )
   259→
   260→
   261→def _cmd_confirm_existing(
   262→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   263→    *,
   264→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   265→) -> None:
   266→    """Fast-track: confirm existing plan structure is still valid."""
   267→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   268→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   269→    strategy: str | None = getattr(args, "strategy", None)
   270→    confirmed: str | None = getattr(args, "confirmed", None)
   271→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   272→
   273→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   274→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to confirm.", "yellow"))
   275→        return
   276→
   277→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   278→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   279→
   280→    # Require a prior completed triage — can't skip the full flow on first run
   281→    if not _require_prior_strategy_for_confirm(meta):
   282→        return
   283→
   284→    context = _resolve_confirm_existing_context(
   285→        args=args,
   286→        plan=plan,
   287→        services=resolved_services,
   288→        stages=stages,
   289→    )
   290→    if context is None:
   291→        return
   292→    si, has_only_additions = context
   293→    state = resolved_services.command_runtime(args).state
   294→
   295→    clusters_with_issues = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   296→    if not clusters_with_issues:
   297→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: no clusters with issues exist.", "red"))
   298→        print(colorize("  Use the full organize flow instead.", "dim"))
   299→        return
   300→
   301→    resolved_inputs = _resolve_confirm_existing_inputs(
   302→        note=note,
   303→        strategy=strategy,
   304→        confirmed=confirmed,
   305→        plan=plan,
   306→        state=state,
   307→        meta=meta,
   308→        si=si,
   309→        has_only_additions=has_only_additions,
   310→    )
   311→    if resolved_inputs is None:
   312→        return
   313→    note_text, strategy, confirmed_text = resolved_inputs
   314→
   315→    # Record organize as confirmed-existing and complete
   316→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   317→    record_confirm_existing_completion(
   318→        stages=stages,
   319→        note=note_text,
   320→        issue_count=len(clusters_with_issues),
   321→        confirmed_text=confirmed_text,
   322→    )
   323→
   324→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   325→        plan,
   326→        "triage_confirm_existing",
   327→        actor="user",
   328→        detail={"confirmed_text": confirmed_text},
   329→    )
   330→
   331→    apply_completion(
   332→        args,
   333→        plan,
   334→        strategy,
   335→        services=resolved_services,
   336→        completion_mode="confirm_existing",
   337→        completion_note=note_text,
   338→    )
   339→    print(
   340→        colorize(
   341→            "  Confirmed existing plan — reused the current enriched cluster plan.",
   342→            "green",
   343→        )
   344→    )
   345→
   346→
   347→def cmd_triage_complete(
   348→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   349→    *,
   350→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   351→) -> None:
   352→    """Public entrypoint for triage completion."""
   353→    _cmd_triage_complete(args, services=services)
   354→
```

```
    86→TriageStages = dict[

    92→def resolve_reusable_report(
    93→    report: str | None,
    94→    existing_stage: dict | ObserveRecord | ReflectRecord | OrganizeRecord | EnrichRecord | SenseCheckRecord | ValueCheckRecord | None,
    95→) -> tuple[str | None, bool]:
    96→    if report:
    97→        return report, False
    98→    if existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
    99→        return existing_stage["report"], True
   100→    return None, False
   101→
   102→
   103→def record_observe_stage(
   104→    stages: TriageStages,
   105→    *,
   106→    report: str,
   107→    issue_count: int,
   108→    cited_ids: list[str],
   109→    existing_stage: ObserveRecord | None,
   110→    is_reuse: bool,
   111→    assessments: list[ObserveAssessmentRecord] | None = None,
   112→    dimension_names: list[str] | None = None,
   113→    dimension_counts: dict[str, int] | None = None,
   114→) -> list[str]:
   115→    observe: ObserveRecord = {
   116→        "stage": "observe",
   117→        "report": report,
   118→        "cited_ids": cited_ids,
   119→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   120→        "issue_count": issue_count,
   121→    }
   122→    if dimension_names is not None:
   123→        observe["dimension_names"] = dimension_names
   124→    if dimension_counts is not None:
   125→        observe["dimension_counts"] = dimension_counts
   126→    if assessments is not None:
   127→        observe["assessments"] = assessments
   128→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
   129→        observe["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
   130→        observe["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
   131→    stages["observe"] = observe
   132→    cleared = cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "observe")
   133→    if not is_reuse:
   134→        observe.pop("confirmed_at", None)
   135→        observe.pop("confirmed_text", None)
   136→    return cleared
   137→
   138→

   140→    stages: TriageStages,

   149→        "report": report,
   150→        "cited_ids": [],
   151→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   152→        "issue_count": issue_count,

   154→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
   155→        organize["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
   156→        organize["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
   157→    stages["organize"] = organize
   158→    return cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "organize")

   176→        enrich["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
   177→        enrich["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
   178→    stages["enrich"] = enrich

   180→

   183→    stages: TriageStages,

   201→def record_value_check_stage(

   204→    report: str,
   205→    existing_stage: ValueCheckRecord | None,
   206→    is_reuse: bool,
   207→) -> list[str]:
   208→    value_check: ValueCheckRecord = {
   209→        "stage": "value-check",
   210→        "report": report,
   211→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   212→    }
   213→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
   214→        value_check["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
   215→        value_check["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
   216→    stages["value-check"] = value_check
   217→    return cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "value-check")
   218→
   219→
   220→def record_confirm_existing_completion(
   221→    *,
   222→    stages: TriageStages,
   223→    note: str,
   224→    issue_count: int,
   225→    confirmed_text: str,
   226→) -> None:
   227→    stages["organize"] = {
   228→        "stage": "organize",
   229→        "report": f"[confirmed-existing] {note}",
   230→        "cited_ids": [],
   231→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   232→        "issue_count": issue_count,
   233→        "confirmed_at": utc_now(),
   234→        "confirmed_text": confirmed_text,
   235→        "reused_existing_plan": True,
   236→        "completion_note": note,
   237→    }
   238→
   239→
   240→__all__ = [
   241→    "ObserveAssessmentRecord",
   242→    "ObserveRecord",
   243→    "ReflectRecord",
   244→    "TriageStages",
   245→    "record_confirm_existing_completion",
   246→    "record_enrich_stage",
   247→    "record_observe_stage",
   248→    "record_organize_stage",
   249→    "record_sense_check_stage",
   250→    "record_value_check_stage",
   251→    "resolve_reusable_report",
   252→]
   253→
```

```
1→"""Shared helpers for triage runner orchestrators."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from datetime import UTC, datetime
     6→
     7→STAGES: tuple[str, ...] = (
     8→    "observe",
     9→    "reflect",
    10→    "organize",
    11→    "enrich",
    12→    "sense-check",
    13→    "value-check",
    14→)
    15→
    16→
    17→def parse_only_stages(raw: str | None) -> list[str]:
    18→    """Parse --only-stages comma-separated string into validated stage list."""
    19→    if not raw:
    20→        return list(STAGES)
    21→    stages = [s.strip().lower() for s in raw.split(",") if s.strip()]
    22→    for stage in stages:
    23→        if stage not in STAGES:
    24→            raise ValueError(f"Unknown stage: {stage!r}. Valid: {', '.join(STAGES)}")
    25→    return stages
    26→
    27→
    28→def run_stamp() -> str:
    29→    return datetime.now(UTC).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    30→
```

```
    30→) -> bool:
```

```
1→"""Stage-gate and coverage helpers for triage command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import is_synthetic_id
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.issue_semantics import is_triage_finding
     8→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import TRIAGE_IDS
     9→
    10→from ..review_coverage import (
    11→    cluster_issue_ids,
    12→    live_active_triage_issue_ids,
    13→    manual_clusters_with_issues,
    14→)
    15→
    16→
    17→def _require_triage_pending(plan: dict, *, action: str) -> bool:
    18→    """Require at least one triage stage ID to be present in queue for an action."""
    19→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    20→    if order & TRIAGE_IDS:
    21→        return True
    22→    print(colorize(f"  No triage stage in the queue — nothing to {action}.", "yellow"))
    23→    return False
    24→
    25→
    26→def _validate_stage_report(
    27→    report: str | None,
    28→    *,
    29→    stage: str,
    30→    min_chars: int,
    31→    missing_guidance: list[str] | None = None,
    32→    short_guidance: list[str] | None = None,
    33→) -> str | None:
    34→    """Validate staged report presence/length and print consistent guidance."""
    35→    if not report:
    36→        print(colorize(f"  --report is required for --stage {stage}.", "red"))
    37→        for line in missing_guidance or []:
    38→            print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
    39→        return None
    40→    cleaned = report.strip()
    41→    if len(cleaned) < min_chars:
    42→        print(
    43→            colorize(
    44→                f"  Report too short: {len(cleaned)} chars (minimum {min_chars}).",
    45→                "red",
    46→            )
    47→        )
    48→        for line in short_guidance or []:
    49→            print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
    50→        return None
    51→    return cleaned
    52→
    53→
    54→def unenriched_clusters(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, list[str]]]:
    55→    """Return clusters with issues that are missing required enrichment.
    56→
    57→    Requirements:
    58→    - Every cluster needs a description and at least one action_step.
    59→    - Small clusters (< 5 issues) need at least 1 action step per issue,
    60→      so each item has a concrete plan. Large clusters (>= 5) just need
    61→      steps overall (cluster-level plan is sufficient).
    62→    """
    63→    gaps: list[tuple[str, list[str]]] = []
    64→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    65→        issue_ids = cluster_issue_ids(cluster)
    66→        if not issue_ids:
    67→            continue
    68→        if cluster.get("auto"):
    69→            continue
    70→        missing: list[str] = []
    71→        if not cluster.get("description"):
    72→            missing.append("description")
    73→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps") or []
    74→        issue_count = len(issue_ids)
    75→        if not steps:
    76→            missing.append("action_steps")
    77→        elif issue_count < 5 and len(steps) < issue_count:
    78→            missing.append(
    79→                f"action_steps (have {len(steps)}, need >= {issue_count} for small cluster)"
    80→            )
    81→        if missing:
    82→            gaps.append((name, missing))
    83→    return gaps
    84→
    85→
    86→def unclustered_review_issues(plan: dict, state: dict | None = None) -> list[str]:
    87→    """Return review issue IDs that aren't in any manual cluster.
    88→
    89→    When *state* is provided, uses open review/concerns issues from state
    90→    (the canonical source). Falls back to scanning queue_order for backwards
    91→    compatibility.
    92→    """
    93→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    94→    clustered_ids: set[str] = set()
    95→    for cluster in clusters.values():
    96→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
    97→            clustered_ids.update(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
    98→    skipped_ids = {
    99→        fid for fid in (plan.get("skipped", {}) or {}).keys() if isinstance(fid, str)
   100→    }
   101→
   102→    if state is not None:
   103→        review_ids = [
   104→            fid for fid, finding in (state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {})).items()
   105→            if finding.get("status") == "open"
   106→            and is_triage_finding(finding)
   107→        ]
   108→        frozen_ids = (plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}) or {}).get("active_triage_issue_ids")
   109→        if isinstance(frozen_ids, list) and frozen_ids:
   110→            frozen_id_set = live_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
   111→            review_ids = [fid for fid in review_ids if fid in frozen_id_set]
   112→    else:
   113→        review_ids = [
   114→            fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   115→            if not is_synthetic_id(fid)
   116→            and (fid.startswith("review::") or fid.startswith("concerns::"))
   117→        ]
   118→
   119→    return [
   120→        fid for fid in review_ids
   121→        if fid not in clustered_ids and fid not in skipped_ids
   122→    ]
   123→
   124→
   125→def value_check_targets(plan: dict, state: dict | None = None) -> list[str]:
   126→    """Return the current execution targets that value-check must judge once each."""
   127→    targets = list(manual_clusters_with_issues(plan))
   128→    targets.extend(unclustered_review_issues(plan, state))
   129→    seen: set[str] = set()
   130→    ordered: list[str] = []
   131→    for target in targets:
   132→        if target in seen:
   133→            continue
   134→        seen.add(target)
   135→        ordered.append(target)
   136→    return ordered
   137→
```
