## Comprehensive Triage Pipeline Analysis

Based on my thorough exploration of the triage pipeline codebase, here's a complete breakdown:

### 1. TRIAGE STAGES AND FLOW

The triage pipeline consists of **6 main stages** (plus a "commit" stage for final completion), defined in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/constants.py`:

**Stage Order (from `stage_queue.py` line 15):**
```
["observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check", "value-check"]
```

**Stage Definitions (from `engine/_plan/triage/playbook.py` lines 7-15):**
```
TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS: (
    ("observe", "Analyse issues & spot contradictions"),
    ("reflect", "Form strategy & present to user"),
    ("organize", "Defer contradictions, cluster, & prioritize"),
    ("enrich", "Make steps executor-ready (detail, refs)"),
    ("sense-check", "Verify accuracy & cross-cluster deps"),
    ("value-check", "Decide item-by-item keep/skip value"),
    ("commit", "Write strategy & confirm"),
)
```

### 2. STAGE PREREQUISITES AND DEPENDENCIES

Each stage has explicit prerequisites defined in `playbook.py` (lines 110-131):

- **observe**: No prerequisites (can start immediately)
- **reflect**: Requires `observe` recorded
- **organize**: Requires `observe` + `reflect` recorded
- **enrich**: Requires `observe` + `reflect` recorded + `organize` **confirmed**
- **sense-check**: Requires `enrich` **confirmed**
- **value-check**: Requires `sense-check` **confirmed**
- **commit**: Requires `value-check` recorded

Key distinction: Some prerequisites require **confirmation** (`confirmed_at` field), not just recording.

### 3. TYPICAL STAGE STRUCTURE

Each stage follows a consistent pattern across files:
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/stages/{stage_name}.py`

**Common Elements:**

1. **Stage Context Resolution** - Load/validate report, resolve reusable reports from previous runs
2. **Prerequisite Checking** - Validate prior stages are complete/confirmed
3. **Validation** - Stage-specific validation logic (evidence parsing, ledger checks, quality checks)
4. **Persistence** - Record the stage in `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["triage_stages"][stage_name]`
5. **Cascade Clearing** - Clear confirmations of later stages if re-recording earlier stages
6. **Logging** - Append log entries with activity tracking

**Stage Record Structure (from `records.py`, lines 12-84):**

Each stage stores a TypedDict with fields like:
- `stage`: Stage name
- `report`: User-provided analysis text
- `timestamp`: UTC timestamp
- `confirmed_at`: When user confirmed (optional)
- `confirmed_text`: User confirmation text (optional)
- Stage-specific fields (e.g., `assess ments`, `issue_count`, `cited_ids`, `disposition_ledger`)

### 4. DETAILED STAGE DESCRIPTIONS

#### **OBSERVE** (`observe.py`)
- **Purpose**: Verify each queued issue against code; spot contradictions
- **Input**: User report with analysis of themes/root causes
- **Validation**: 
  - Evidence parsing with verdict, reasoning, files_read
  - Citation tracking (which issues cited)
  - Dimension breakdown tracking
- **Output**: Issues mapped to verdicts (genuine/false positive/exaggerated/not-worth-it)
- **Special**: Creates `issue_dispositions` metadata with verdicts for downstream stages

#### **REFLECT** (`reflect.py`)
- **Purpose**: Form strategy by comparing current issues to completed work
- **Input**: Report describing how issues relate to previously resolved work
- **Validation**:
  - Requires observe stage complete
  - Detects recurring patterns (dimensions with both resolved and open issues)
  - Parses "Coverage Ledger" section for disposition decisions
- **Output**: Decision ledger (keep/skip/tighten decisions per issue)
- **Special**: Populates `decision`, `target`, `decision_source` in dispositions

#### **ORGANIZE** (`organize.py`)
- **Purpose**: Defer contradictions, cluster issues, and prioritize
- **Input**: Report summarizing organization and priorities
- **Prerequisites**: 
  - Reflect stage must exist
  - Reflect can be auto-confirmed for organize if needed
  - Minimum cluster operations required (3+ or attestation)
- **Validation**:
  - Manual clusters must exist and be enriched
  - All review issues must be clustered or skipped
  - Report must reference all clusters
- **Output**: Marked as "confirmed" stage when ready for enrich
- **Special**: Enforces cluster activity logs to ensure user did the work

#### **ENRICH** (`enrich.py`)
- **Purpose**: Make steps executor-ready with detail and references
- **Input**: Report summarizing enrichment work done
- **Prerequisites**: Organize stage must be confirmed
- **Validation**:
  - Quality checks for underspecified steps (need detail or issue_refs)
  - Cluster update activity required (with attestation override option)
  - Path validation for step files
  - Effort tags (optional warnings)
- **Output**: Records `shallow_count` (steps without detail)
- **Special**: Uses `EnrichStageDeps` dependency injection for testing

#### **SENSE-CHECK** (`sense_check.py`)
- **Purpose**: Verify accuracy and cross-cluster dependencies
- **Input**: Report on what content/structure subagents found and fixed
- **Prerequisites**: Enrich stage must be confirmed
- **Validation**:
  - Re-runs enrich-level quality checks (bad paths, missing effort, missing issue_refs, vague detail)
  - Requires file paths referenced in report
  - Checks cluster references if issues remain
- **Output**: Simple record (no assessments needed)
- **Special**: Can be parallelized with 2 subagents (content + structure)

#### **VALUE-CHECK** (`value_check.py`)
- **Purpose**: Final item-by-item decision on keep/skip/tighten
- **Input**: Report with "Decision Ledger" section (target → keep|tighten|skip)
- **Prerequisites**: Sense-check stage must be confirmed
- **Validation**:
  - Decision Ledger parsing (structured decision lines)
  - All live targets must appear once (no missing, no duplicates, no extra)
  - Quality checks (paths, effort, issue refs)
  - All decisions must be exactly: keep, tighten, or skip
- **Output**: Records value check decisions
- **Special**: Targets = clusters + unclustered review issues

### 5. VALIDATION AND COMPLETION FLOW

**Completion Path** (`completion.py`):

The `--complete` command validates a gate sequence:
1. **Organize gate** - Must exist and be confirmed (or auto-confirm)
2. **Enrich gate** - Must exist and be confirmed, checks underspec steps
3. **Sense-check gate** - Must exist and be confirmed
4. **Value-check gate** - Must exist and be confirmed
5. **Cluster coverage** - Validates all clusters are valid
6. **Strategy** - Resolves execution strategy from meta or new input
7. **Apply completion** - Writes final plan state, logs completion

**Key completion checks (from `completion_policy.py`)**:
- `_completion_clusters_valid()` - All clusters are structured correctly
- `_completion_strategy_valid()` - Strategy meets length/format requirements
- Confirms all previous stages exist and are confirmed

**Alternative Path: Confirm-Existing**
- Fast-track for re-triaging when only new issues added
- Requires prior completed triage (with strategy in meta)
- Skips full 6-stage flow, just confirms cluster plan still valid
- Records "organize" stage as confirmed-existing

### 6. STAGE QUEUE AND LIFECYCLE MANAGEMENT

**Queue Management** (`stage_queue.py`):

- `STAGE_ORDER`: Canonical stage order for cascade operations
- `inject_triage_stages()`: Adds all 6 stage IDs to queue front on start
- `cascade_clear_dispositions()`: On re-record of earlier stages, clears downstream dispositions
  - Observe rerun: Wipes entire disposition map
  - Reflect rerun: Clears decision/target/decision_source fields only
- `cascade_clear_later_confirmations()`: Clears confirmation timestamps on later stages when earlier stage re-recorded
- `purge_triage_stage()`: Remove one stage from queue completely

### 7. STAGE REGISTRATION AND COMMAND DISPATCH

**Command Handlers** (`stages/commands.py`):

```python
STAGE_COMMAND_HANDLERS = {
    "observe": cmd_stage_observe,
    "reflect": cmd_stage_reflect,
    "organize": cmd_stage_organize,
    "enrich": cmd_stage_enrich,
    "sense-check": cmd_stage_sense_check,
    "value-check": cmd_stage_value_check,
}
```

Each handler is a function that:
- Takes `args: argparse.Namespace` and optional `services: TriageServices`
- Calls the stage's validation logic
- Persists the stage record via `record_{stage_name}_stage()`
- Logs the action via `services.append_log_entry()`
- Prints user feedback (success/errors/next steps)

### 8. ORCHESTRATORS

Two runner modes exist:

**Claude Orchestrator** (`orchestrator_claude.py`):
- Manual flow: User launches one subagent per stage
- Instructions for getting prompts, verifying, confirming between stages
- Observe & enrich subagents can use sub-subagents (parallel per dimension/cluster)
- Sense-check launches TWO parallel subagents (content + structure)

**Codex Orchestrator** (complex pipeline):
- Automated/parallel execution mode
- Multiple files for observation, sense-check, pipeline context, etc.
- More advanced parallel execution patterns

### 9. STAGE METADATA STRUCTURE

**Plan Location**: `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["triage_stages"][stage_name]`

**Core Metadata** across all stages:
```python
{
    "stage": str,                  # Stage name
    "report": str,                 # User report text
    "timestamp": str,              # UTC timestamp when recorded
    "confirmed_at": str | None,    # UTC when confirmed
    "confirmed_text": str | None,  # Confirmation note
    # Stage-specific fields below:
}
```

**Observe-specific**:
- `cited_ids`: Issues mentioned in report
- `issue_count`: Total issues analyzed
- `assessments`: List of {hash, verdict, verdict_reasoning, files_read, recommendation}
- `dimension_names`: Detector dimensions tracked
- `dimension_counts`: Count per dimension

**Reflect-specific**:
- `missing_issue_ids`: Issues cited but invalid
- `duplicate_issue_ids`: Issues mentioned multiple times
- `recurring_dims`: Dimensions with both open and resolved issues
- `disposition_ledger`: List of {issue_id, decision, target, ...}

**Organize-specific**:
- `issue_count`: Total review issues
- `reused_existing_plan`: Boolean for confirm-existing path
- `completion_note`: User note (for confirm-existing)

**Enrich-specific**:
- `shallow_count`: Steps lacking detail/refs

**Sense-check/Value-check**: Minimal - just report and timestamps

### 10. KEY FILES SUMMARY

| File | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| `stage_queue.py` | Stage queue mutation, cascade clearing, lifecycle |
| `stages/observe.py` | Observe stage command and validation |
| `stages/reflect.py` | Reflect stage with disposition ledger parsing |
| `stages/organize.py` | Organize validation with cluster activity checks |
| `stages/enrich.py` | Enrich quality validation with DI pattern |
| `stages/sense_check.py` | Sense-check quality re-validation |
| `stages/value_check.py` | Value-check decision ledger validation |
| `stages/completion.py` | Completion flow and confirm-existing |
| `stages/records.py` | TypedDict definitions and persistence helpers |
| `stages/commands.py` | Stage command dispatch registry |
| `stages/helpers.py` | Validation helpers (unclustered issues, targets) |
| `stages/flow_helpers.py` | Report length validation |
| `engine/_plan/triage/playbook.py` | Stage labels, dependencies, progress computation |
| `engine/_plan/constants.py` | TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, specs, order |
| `engine/plan_triage.py` | Public triage API facade |
| `runner/orchestrator_claude.py` | Manual subagent mode instructions |
| `runner/orchestrator_common.py` | Shared runner constants |

This triage pipeline is a carefully orchestrated 6-stage workflow with explicit dependencies, prerequisite gating, cascade clearing, validation at each step, and support for both manual (Claude) and automated (Codex) execution modes.

1→"""Shared helpers for triage runner orchestrators."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from datetime import UTC, datetime
     6→
     7→STAGES: tuple[str, ...] = (
     8→    "observe",
     9→    "reflect",
    10→    "organize",
    11→    "enrich",
    12→    "sense-check",
    13→    "value-check",
    14→)
    15→
    16→
    17→def parse_only_stages(raw: str | None) -> list[str]:
    18→    """Parse --only-stages comma-separated string into validated stage list."""
    19→    if not raw:
    20→        return list(STAGES)
    21→    stages = [s.strip().lower() for s in raw.split(",") if s.strip()]
    22→    for stage in stages:
    23→        if stage not in STAGES:
    24→            raise ValueError(f"Unknown stage: {stage!r}. Valid: {', '.join(STAGES)}")
    25→    return stages
    26→
    27→
    28→def run_stamp() -> str:
    29→    return datetime.now(UTC).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    30→

    11→def _print_observe_report_requirement() -> None:
    12→    print(colorize("  --report is required for --stage observe.", "red"))
    13→    print(colorize("  Verify the queued issues one by one against the code.", "dim"))

    17→
    18→def _print_reflect_report_requirement() -> None:
    19→    print(colorize("  --report is required for --stage reflect.", "red"))
    20→    print(colorize("  Compare current issues against completed work and form a holistic strategy:", "dim"))
    21→    print(colorize("  - What clusters were previously completed? Did fixes hold?", "dim"))
    22→    print(colorize("  - Are any dimensions recurring (resolved before, open again)?", "dim"))
    23→    print(colorize("  - What contradictions did you find? Which direction will you take?", "dim"))
    24→    print(colorize("  - Big picture: what to prioritize, what to defer, what to skip?", "dim"))

    26→
    27→def _print_complete_summary(plan: dict, stages: dict) -> None:
    28→    print(colorize("  Triage summary:", "bold"))
    29→    if "observe" in stages:
    30→        observe_stage = stages["observe"]
    31→        print(colorize(f"    Observe: {observe_stage.get('issue_count', '?')} issues analysed", "dim"))
    32→    if "reflect" in stages:
    33→        reflect_stage = stages["reflect"]
    34→        recurring = reflect_stage.get("recurring_dims", [])
    35→        if recurring:
    36→            print(colorize(f"    Reflect: {len(recurring)} recurring dimension(s)", "dim"))
    37→        else:
    38→            print(colorize("    Reflect: no recurring patterns", "dim"))
    39→    if "organize" not in stages:
    40→        return
    41→    manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
    42→    print(colorize(f"    Organize: {len(manual)} enriched cluster(s)", "dim"))
    43→    for name in manual:
    44→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(name, {})
    45→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
    46→        print(colorize(f"      {name}: {len(steps)} steps", "dim"))
    47→    if "enrich" in stages:
    48→        shallow = stages["enrich"].get("shallow_count", 0)

    51→        else:
    52→            print(colorize("    Enrich: all steps detailed", "dim"))
    53→    if "sense-check" in stages:
    54→        print(colorize("    Sense-check: content & structure verified", "dim"))
    55→    if "value-check" in stages:
    56→        print(colorize("    Value-check: keep/skip value decisions recorded", "dim"))

    59→def _print_new_issues_since_last(si) -> None:

    61→    review_issues = getattr(si, "review_issues", getattr(si, "open_issues", {}))
    62→    for fid in sorted(si.new_since_last):
    63→        issue = review_issues.get(fid, {})
    64→        print(f"    * [{short_issue_id(fid)}] {issue.get('summary', '')}")

    44→def _print_sense_header(total_content: int, *, apply_updates: bool, log: Callable[[str], None]) -> None:

    55→    log(f"sense-check-parallel content_batches={total_content}")

    58→def _sense_modes(*, apply_updates: bool) -> tuple[str, str]:
    59→    mode = "self_record" if apply_updates else "output_only"
    60→    return mode, mode
    61→
    62→
    63→def _content_batch_config(
    64→    *,
    65→    cluster_name: str,

    67→    plan: dict,

    99→    logs_dir: Path,
   100→    mode: str,

   122→def _content_tasks_and_meta(
   123→    *,
   124→    clusters: list[str],
   125→    plan: dict,
   126→    repo_root: Path,
   127→    prompts_dir: Path,
   128→    output_dir: Path,
   129→    logs_dir: Path,
   130→    timeout_seconds: int,
   131→    dry_run: bool,
   132→    policy_text: str,

   135→    log: Callable[[str], None],

   137→    tasks: dict[int, Callable[[], TriageStageRunResult]] = {}
   138→    batch_meta: list[tuple[str, Path]] = []
   139→    for i, cluster_name in enumerate(clusters):
   140→        config = _content_batch_config(
   141→            cluster_name=cluster_name,
   142→            batch_index=i,
   143→            plan=plan,
   144→            repo_root=repo_root,
   145→            prompts_dir=prompts_dir,
   146→            output_dir=output_dir,
   147→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   148→            policy_text=policy_text,
   149→            mode=content_mode,
   150→            cli_command=cli_command,
   151→        )
   152→        _write_batch_prompt(config)
   153→        batch_meta.append((config.label, config.output_file))
   154→        if not dry_run:
   155→            tasks[i] = partial(
   156→                run_triage_stage,
   157→                prompt=config.prompt,
   158→                repo_root=repo_root,
   159→                output_file=config.output_file,
   160→                log_file=config.log_file,
   161→                timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   162→                validate_output_fn=_output_file_has_text,
   163→            )
   164→        print(colorize(f"    Content batch {i + 1}: {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   165→        log(f"sense-check-content batch={i + 1} cluster={cluster_name}")
   166→    return tasks, batch_meta
   167→
   168→
   169→def _run_parallel_or_fail(
   170→    *,
   171→    tasks: dict[int, Callable[[], TriageStageRunResult]],
   172→    stage_label: str,
   173→    batch_label_fn: Callable[[int], str],
   174→    log: Callable[[str], None],
   175→) -> list[int]:
   176→    return run_parallel_batches(
   177→        tasks=tasks,
   178→        stage_label=stage_label,
   179→        batch_label_fn=batch_label_fn,
   180→        append_run_log=log,
   181→        heartbeat_seconds=15.0,
   182→    )
   183→
   184→
   185→def _content_failures(
   186→    *,
   187→    tasks: dict[int, Callable[[], TriageStageRunResult]],
   188→    clusters: list[str],
   189→    log: Callable[[str], None],
   190→) -> list[int]:
   191→    def _content_label(idx: int) -> str:
   192→        if idx < len(clusters):
   193→            return f"content:{clusters[idx]}"
   194→        return f"content:{idx}"
   195→
   196→    return _run_parallel_or_fail(
   197→        tasks=tasks,
   198→        stage_label="Sense-check",
   199→        batch_label_fn=_content_label,
   200→        log=log,
   201→    )
   202→
   203→
   204→def _parallel_failure_result(
   205→    failures: list[int],
   206→    *,
   207→    log: Callable[[str], None],
   208→) -> TriageStageRunResult:
   209→    print(colorize(f"  Sense-check: {len(failures)} batch(es) failed: {failures}", "red"))
   210→    log(f"sense-check-parallel-failed failures={failures}")
   211→    return TriageStageRunResult(
   212→        exit_code=1,
   213→        reason="parallel_execution_failed",
   214→    )
   215→
   216→
   217→def _reload_structure_plan(
   218→    *,
   219→    reload_plan: Callable[[], dict] | None,
   220→    log: Callable[[str], None],
   221→) -> dict | None:
   222→    if reload_plan is None:
   223→        return None
   224→    try:
   225→        reloaded = dict(reload_plan())
   226→        log("sense-check-plan-reloaded phase=structure")
   227→        return reloaded
   228→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:  # pragma: no cover - defensive fallback
   229→        print(colorize("  Sense-check: failed to reload plan after content updates.", "red"))
   230→        log(f"sense-check-plan-reload-failed error={exc}")
   231→        return None
   232→
   233→
   234→def _merge_batch_outputs(batch_meta: list[tuple[str, Path]]) -> str:
   235→    parts: list[str] = []
   236→    for label, output_file in batch_meta:
   237→        content = ""
   238→        if output_file.exists():
   239→            try:
   240→                content = output_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").strip()
   241→            except OSError:
   242→                content = "(output missing)"
   243→        if not content:
   244→            content = "(no output)"
   245→        parts.append(f"## {label}\n\n{content}")
   246→    return "\n\n---\n\n".join(parts)
   247→
   248→
   249→def run_sense_check(
   250→    *,
   251→    plan: dict,
   252→    repo_root: Path,
   253→    prompts_dir: Path,
   254→    output_dir: Path,
   255→    logs_dir: Path,
   256→    timeout_seconds: int,
   257→    dry_run: bool = False,
   258→    cli_command: str = "desloppify",
   259→    apply_updates: bool = False,
   260→    reload_plan: Callable[[], dict] | None = None,
   261→    append_run_log=None,
   262→) -> TriageStageRunResult:
   263→    """Run sense-check via parallel codex subprocess batches."""
   264→    _log = append_run_log or _noop_log
   265→
   266→    clusters = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   267→    total_content = len(clusters)
   268→    total = total_content + 1
   269→    _print_sense_header(total_content, apply_updates=apply_updates, log=_log)
   270→
   271→    policy_result = load_policy_result()
   272→    policy_text = render_policy_block(policy_result.policy)
   273→    if not policy_result.ok:
   274→        print(
   275→            colorize(
   276→                f"  Warning: ignoring malformed project policy ({policy_result.message or 'unknown error'}).",
   277→                "yellow",
   278→            )
   279→        )
   280→    content_mode, structure_mode = _sense_modes(apply_updates=apply_updates)
   281→    content_tasks, batch_meta = _content_tasks_and_meta(
   282→        clusters=clusters,
   283→        plan=plan,
   284→        repo_root=repo_root,
   285→        prompts_dir=prompts_dir,
   286→        output_dir=output_dir,
   287→        logs_dir=logs_dir,
   288→        timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   289→        dry_run=dry_run,
   290→        policy_text=policy_text,
   291→        content_mode=content_mode,
   292→        cli_command=cli_command,
   293→        log=_log,
   294→    )
   295→    structure_plan = dict(plan)
   296→    structure_config = _structure_batch_config(
   297→        plan=structure_plan,
   298→        repo_root=repo_root,
   299→        prompts_dir=prompts_dir,
   300→        output_dir=output_dir,
   301→        logs_dir=logs_dir,
   302→        mode=structure_mode,
   303→        cli_command=cli_command,
   304→    )
   305→    _write_batch_prompt(structure_config)
   306→    batch_meta.append((structure_config.label, structure_config.output_file))
   307→    print(colorize("    Structure batch: global dependency check", "dim"))
   308→    _log("sense-check-structure batch=global")
   309→
   310→    if dry_run:
   311→        if apply_updates:
   312→            print(colorize("  [dry-run] Would execute sequenced sense-check batches.", "dim"))
   313→        else:
   314→            print(colorize("  [dry-run] Would execute parallel sense-check batches.", "dim"))
   315→        return TriageStageRunResult(exit_code=0, reason="dry_run", dry_run=True)
   316→
   317→    if content_tasks:
   318→        content_failures = _content_failures(tasks=content_tasks, clusters=clusters, log=_log)
   319→        if content_failures:
   320→            return _parallel_failure_result(content_failures, log=_log)
   321→
   322→    if apply_updates and reload_plan is not None:
   323→        reloaded_plan = _reload_structure_plan(reload_plan=reload_plan, log=_log)
   324→        if reloaded_plan is None:
   325→            return TriageStageRunResult(exit_code=1, reason="plan_reload_failed")
   326→        structure_plan = reloaded_plan
   327→        structure_config = _structure_batch_config(
   328→            plan=structure_plan,
   329→            repo_root=repo_root,
   330→            prompts_dir=prompts_dir,
   331→            output_dir=output_dir,
   332→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   333→            mode=structure_mode,
   334→            cli_command=cli_command,
   335→        )
   336→        _write_batch_prompt(structure_config)
   337→
   338→    structure_tasks: dict[int, Callable[[], TriageStageRunResult]] = {
   339→        0: partial(
   340→            run_triage_stage,
   341→            prompt=structure_config.prompt,
   342→            repo_root=repo_root,
   343→            output_file=structure_config.output_file,
   344→            log_file=structure_config.log_file,
   345→            timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   346→            validate_output_fn=_output_file_has_text,
   347→        )
   348→    }
   349→    structure_failures = _run_parallel_or_fail(
   350→        tasks=structure_tasks,
   351→        stage_label="Sense-check",
   352→        batch_label_fn=lambda _idx: "structure",
   353→        log=_log,
   354→    )
   355→    if structure_failures:
   356→        return _parallel_failure_result(structure_failures, log=_log)
   357→
   358→    merged = _merge_batch_outputs(batch_meta)
   359→    print(colorize(f"  Sense-check: merged {total} batch outputs ({len(merged)} chars).", "green"))
   360→    _log(f"sense-check-parallel-done merged_chars={len(merged)}")
   361→    return TriageStageRunResult(
   362→        exit_code=0,
   363→        merged_output=merged,
   364→    )
   365→
   366→
   367→__all__ = ["run_sense_check"]

    37→    value_check_targets: Callable[[dict, dict | None], list[str]] = value_check_targets
    38→    manual_clusters_with_issues: Callable[[dict], list[str]] = manual_clusters_with_issues
    39→    colorize: ColorizeFn = colorize
    40→    default_triage_services: Callable[[], TriageServices] = default_triage_services
    41→    print_cascade_clear_feedback: Callable[[list[str], dict], None] = print_cascade_clear_feedback
    42→    get_project_root: Callable[[], Path] | None = None
    43→
    44→
    45→def _report_length_ok(report: str | None, *, colorize_fn: ColorizeFn) -> bool:
    46→    if not report:
    47→        print(colorize_fn("  --report is required for --stage value-check.", "red"))
    48→        print(
    49→            colorize_fn(
    50→                "  Include a Decision Ledger with one line per live queue target: keep, tighten, or skip.",
    51→                "dim",
    52→            )
    53→        )
    54→        return False
    55→    if len(report.strip()) < 100:
    56→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Report too short: {len(report.strip())} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
    57→        return False
    58→    return True
    59→
    60→
    61→def _quality_problems(plan: dict, *, deps: ValueCheckStageDeps) -> list[str]:
    62→    get_project_root = deps.get_project_root
    63→    if get_project_root is None:
    64→        from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    65→
    66→    repo_root = get_project_root()
    67→    quality_report = evaluate_enrich_quality(
    68→        plan,
    69→        repo_root,
    70→        phase_label="sense-check",
    71→        bad_paths_severity="warning",
    72→        missing_effort_severity="failure",
    73→        include_missing_issue_refs=True,
    74→        include_vague_detail=True,
    75→        stale_issue_refs_severity=None,
    76→    )
    77→    return [issue.message for issue in quality_report.failures]
    78→
    79→
    80→def _ledger_failures(report: str, *, targets: list[str]) -> list[str]:
    81→    parsed = parse_value_check_decision_ledger(report)
    82→    failures: list[str] = []
    83→    if not parsed.entries:
    84→        failures.append(
    85→            "Report is missing a `## Decision Ledger` with `- cluster-or-id -> keep|tighten|skip` lines."
    86→        )
    87→        return failures
    88→    if parsed.duplicates:
    89→        dupes = ", ".join(parsed.duplicates[:5])
    90→        failures.append(f"Decision Ledger repeats target(s): {dupes}")
    91→    missing = [target for target in targets if target not in parsed.entries]
    92→    extra = [target for target in parsed.entries if target not in targets]
    93→    if missing:
    94→        failures.append(
    95→            "Decision Ledger is missing target(s): " + ", ".join(missing[:5])
    96→        )
    97→    if extra:
    98→        failures.append(
    99→            "Decision Ledger references non-live target(s): " + ", ".join(extra[:5])
   100→        )
   101→    return failures
   102→
   103→
   104→def run_stage_value_check(
   105→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   106→    *,
   107→    services: TriageServices | None,
   108→    deps: ValueCheckStageDeps | None = None,
   109→) -> None:
   110→    """Record the VALUE-CHECK stage after final keep/tighten/skip review."""
   111→    resolved_deps = deps or ValueCheckStageDeps()
   112→    resolved_services = services or resolved_deps.default_triage_services()
   113→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   114→    state = resolved_services.command_runtime(args).state
   115→
   116→    if not resolved_deps.has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   117→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to value-check.", "yellow"))
   118→        return
   119→
   120→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   121→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   122→    existing_stage = stages.get("value-check")
   123→    report = getattr(args, "report", None)
   124→    report, is_reuse = resolved_deps.resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
   125→
   126→    if not stages.get("sense-check", {}).get("confirmed_at"):
   127→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Cannot value-check: sense-check stage not confirmed.", "red"))
   128→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Run: desloppify plan triage --confirm sense-check", "dim"))
   129→        return
   130→
   131→    problems = _quality_problems(plan, deps=resolved_deps)
   132→    if problems:
   133→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Cannot record value-check — plan still has issues:", "red"))
   134→        for problem in problems:
   135→            print(resolved_deps.colorize(f"    • {problem}", "yellow"))
   136→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Fix those before recording the final keep/skip pass.", "dim"))
   137→        return
   138→
   139→    if not _report_length_ok(report, colorize_fn=resolved_deps.colorize):
   140→        return
   141→
   142→    targets = resolved_deps.value_check_targets(plan, state)
   143→    ledger_failures = _ledger_failures(report, targets=targets)
   144→    if ledger_failures:
   145→        for failure in ledger_failures:
   146→            print(resolved_deps.colorize(f"  {failure}", "red"))
   147→        return
   148→
   149→    evidence_failures = validate_report_has_file_paths(report)
   150→    cluster_names = resolved_deps.manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   151→    evidence_failures.extend(validate_report_references_clusters(report, cluster_names))
   152→    blocking = [failure for failure in evidence_failures if failure.blocking]
   153→    if blocking:
   154→        print(
   155→            resolved_deps.colorize(
   156→                format_evidence_failures(blocking, stage_label="value-check"),
   157→                "red",
   158→            )
   159→        )
   160→        return
   161→
   162→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   163→    cleared = resolved_deps.record_value_check_stage(
   164→        stages,
   165→        report=report,
   166→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   167→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   168→    )
   169→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   170→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   171→        plan,
   172→        "triage_value_check",
   173→        actor="user",
   174→        detail={"target_count": len(targets), "reuse": is_reuse},
   175→    )
   176→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   177→
   178→    print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Value-check stage recorded.", "green"))
   179→    if is_reuse:
   180→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Value-check data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   181→        if cleared:
   182→            resolved_deps.print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   183→    else:
   184→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Now confirm the value-check.", "yellow"))
   185→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm value-check", "dim"))
   186→
   187→
   188→def cmd_stage_value_check(
   189→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   190→    *,
   191→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   192→) -> None:
   193→    """Public entrypoint for value-check stage recording."""
   194→    run_stage_value_check(args, services=services)
   195→
   196→
   197→__all__ = [
   198→    "ValueCheckStageDeps",
   199→    "cmd_stage_value_check",
   200→    "run_stage_value_check",
   201→]
   202→
```

   125→def value_check_targets(plan: dict, state: dict | None = None) -> list[str]:
   126→    """Return the current execution targets that value-check must judge once each."""
   127→    targets = list(manual_clusters_with_issues(plan))
   128→    targets.extend(unclustered_review_issues(plan, state))
   129→    seen: set[str] = set()
   130→    ordered: list[str] = []
   131→    for target in targets:
   132→        if target in seen:
   133→            continue
   134→        seen.add(target)
   135→        ordered.append(target)
   136→    return ordered
   137→

   438→
   439→def parse_value_check_decision_ledger(report: str) -> DecisionLedger:
   440→    """Parse `## Decision Ledger` lines from a value-check report."""
   441→    entries: dict[str, str] = {}
   442→    duplicates: list[str] = []
   443→    for line in report.splitlines():
   444→        match = _VALUE_LEDGER_RE.match(line)
   445→        if match is None:
   446→            continue
   447→        target = match.group("target").strip()
   448→        decision = match.group("decision").strip().lower()
   449→        if target in entries:
   450→            duplicates.append(target)
   451→            continue
   452→        entries[target] = decision
   453→    return DecisionLedger(entries=entries, duplicates=duplicates)
   454→
   455→
   456→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   457→# Shared output helpers
   458→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   459→
   460→def format_evidence_failures(
   461→    failures: list[EvidenceFailure],
   462→    *,
   463→    stage_label: str,
   464→) -> str:
   465→    """Format evidence failures into a single rejection message."""
   466→    blocking = [f for f in failures if f.blocking]
   467→    advisory = [f for f in failures if not f.blocking]
   468→    parts: list[str] = []
   469→
   470→    for f in blocking:
   471→        parts.append(f"  REJECTED: {f.message}")
   472→
   473→    for f in advisory:
   474→        parts.append(f"  Warning: {f.message}")
   475→
   476→    return "\n\n".join(parts)
   477→
   478→
   479→def resolve_short_hash_to_full_id(short_hash: str, valid_ids: set[str]) -> str | None:
   480→    """Resolve a short hash to a full issue ID using collision-aware resolution.
   481→
   482→    Uses ``_build_id_resolution_maps`` from reflect_accounting to handle
   483→    ambiguous short hashes safely (drops collisions rather than silently
   484→    overwriting).
   485→    """
   486→    from ..validation.reflect_accounting import _build_id_resolution_maps
   487→
   488→    maps = _build_id_resolution_maps(valid_ids)
   489→    # Try direct match first
   490→    if short_hash in valid_ids:
   491→        return short_hash
   492→    # Try collision-aware short hex map
   493→    resolved = maps.short_hex_map.get(short_hash)
   494→    if resolved:
   495→        return resolved
   496→    return None
   497→
   498→

   500→    "DecisionLedger",

    59→def is_synthetic_id(issue_id: str) -> bool:
    60→    """Return True when a raw plan ID refers to synthetic queue work."""

    74→@dataclass
    75→class QueueSyncResult:
    76→    """Unified result for all queue sync operations."""
    77→
    78→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    79→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    80→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    81→    deferred: bool = False
    82→
    83→    @property
    84→    def changes(self) -> int:
    85→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned) + len(self.resurfaced)

    88→def _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    89→    """Extract the triage stages dict from meta or a raw stages dict."""
    90→    if not isinstance(meta_or_stages, dict):
    91→        return {}
    92→    if "triage_stages" in meta_or_stages:
    93→        raw = meta_or_stages.get("triage_stages")
    94→        return raw if isinstance(raw, dict) else {}
    95→    candidate_names = {str(name) for name in meta_or_stages.keys()}
    96→    if candidate_names and candidate_names.issubset(_TRIAGE_STAGE_NAMES):
    97→        return meta_or_stages
    98→    return {}
    99→
   100→
   101→def confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> set[str]:
   102→    """Return triage stage names with an explicit ``confirmed_at`` marker."""
   103→    return {
   104→        str(name)
   105→        for name, payload in _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages).items()
   106→        if isinstance(payload, dict) and payload.get("confirmed_at")
   107→    }
   108→
   109→
   110→def recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> set[str]:
   111→    """Return recorded triage stage names that still need confirmation."""
   112→    return {
   113→        str(name)
   114→        for name, payload in _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages).items()
   115→        if isinstance(payload, dict) and payload and not payload.get("confirmed_at")
   116→    }
   117→
   118→
   119→def normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(queue_order: list[str]) -> None:
   120→    """Keep workflow items ahead of triage, then preserve the rest as-is."""
   121→    seen: set[str] = set()
   122→    normalized: list[str] = []
   123→
   124→    for issue_id in WORKFLOW_PRIORITY_ORDER:
   125→        if issue_id in queue_order and issue_id not in seen:
   126→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   127→            seen.add(issue_id)
   128→
   129→    for issue_id in queue_order:
   130→        if issue_id.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX) and issue_id not in seen:
   131→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   132→            seen.add(issue_id)
   133→
   134→    for issue_id in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
   135→        if issue_id in queue_order and issue_id not in seen:
   136→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   137→            seen.add(issue_id)
   138→
   139→    for issue_id in queue_order:
   140→        if issue_id in seen:
   141→            continue
   142→        normalized.append(issue_id)
   143→        seen.add(issue_id)
   144→
   145→    queue_order[:] = normalized
   146→

    74→    "enrich": TRIAGE_CMD_ENRICH,
    75→    "sense-check": TRIAGE_CMD_SENSE_CHECK,
    76→    "value-check": TRIAGE_CMD_VALUE_CHECK,
    77→    "commit": TRIAGE_CMD_COMPLETE,
    78→}
    79→
    80→
    81→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    82→class StagePrerequisite:
    83→    """One required upstream stage for a triage workflow seam."""
    84→
    85→    stage_name: str
    86→    require_confirmation: bool = False
    87→
    88→
    89→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    90→class StageReadiness:
    91→    """Readiness state for one triage stage."""
    92→
    93→    name: str
    94→    recorded: bool
    95→    confirmed: bool
    96→
    97→
    98→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    99→class TriageProgress:
   100→    """Canonical stage progression snapshot for display and validation."""
   101→
   102→    stages: tuple[StageReadiness, ...]
   103→    current_stage: str | None
   104→    blocked_reason: str | None
   105→    next_command: str | None
   106→    completed_count: int
   107→    confirmed_count: int
   108→
   109→
   110→TRIAGE_STAGE_PREREQUISITES: dict[str, tuple[StagePrerequisite, ...]] = {
   111→    "observe": (),
   112→    "reflect": (StagePrerequisite("observe"),),
   113→    "organize": (
   114→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
   115→        StagePrerequisite("reflect"),
   116→    ),
   117→    "enrich": (
   118→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
   119→        StagePrerequisite("reflect"),
   120→        StagePrerequisite("organize", require_confirmation=True),
   121→    ),
   122→    "sense-check": (
   123→        StagePrerequisite("enrich", require_confirmation=True),
   124→    ),
   125→    "value-check": (
   126→        StagePrerequisite("sense-check", require_confirmation=True),
   127→    ),
   128→    "commit": (
   129→        StagePrerequisite("value-check"),
   130→    ),
   131→}
   132→
   133→TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES: dict[str, set[str]] = {
   134→    stage: {prerequisite.stage_name for prerequisite in prerequisites}
   135→    for stage, prerequisites in TRIAGE_STAGE_PREREQUISITES.items()
   136→}
   137→
   138→_STAGE_LABEL_BY_NAME = dict(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   139→
   140→
   141→def _confirm_stage_command(stage_name: str) -> str:
   142→    return f"desloppify plan triage --confirm {stage_name}"
   143→
   144→
   145→def _first_unmet_prerequisite(
   146→    stage_name: str,
   147→    recorded: dict[str, bool],
   148→    confirmed: dict[str, bool],
   149→) -> StagePrerequisite | None:
   150→    for prerequisite in TRIAGE_STAGE_PREREQUISITES[stage_name]:
   151→        if not recorded.get(prerequisite.stage_name, False):
   152→            return prerequisite
   153→        if prerequisite.require_confirmation and not confirmed.get(prerequisite.stage_name, False):
   154→            return prerequisite
   155→    return None
   156→
   157→
   158→def compute_triage_progress(stages_data: dict) -> TriageProgress:
   159→    """Return canonical triage-stage progression for display and validation."""
   160→    stage_map = stages_data if isinstance(stages_data, dict) else {}
   161→    readiness: list[StageReadiness] = []
   162→    recorded: dict[str, bool] = {}
   163→    confirmed: dict[str, bool] = {}
   164→
   165→    for stage_name, _label in TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS:
   166→        payload = stage_map.get(stage_name, {})
   167→        is_recorded = isinstance(payload, dict) and stage_name in stage_map
   168→        is_confirmed = bool(payload.get("confirmed_at")) if isinstance(payload, dict) else False
   169→        readiness.append(
   170→            StageReadiness(
   171→                name=stage_name,
   172→                recorded=is_recorded,
   173→                confirmed=is_confirmed,
   174→            )
   175→        )
   176→        recorded[stage_name] = is_recorded
   177→        confirmed[stage_name] = is_confirmed
   178→
   179→    current_stage: str | None = None
   180→    blocked_reason: str | None = None
   181→    next_command: str | None = None
   182→
   183→    for stage_name, label in TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS:
   184→        if recorded[stage_name]:
   185→            continue
   186→        missing = _first_unmet_prerequisite(stage_name, recorded, confirmed)
   187→        if missing is None:
   188→            current_stage = stage_name
   189→            next_command = triage_manual_stage_command(stage_name)
   190→        else:
   191→            prerequisite_label = _STAGE_LABEL_BY_NAME.get(missing.stage_name, missing.stage_name)
   192→            if missing.require_confirmation and recorded.get(missing.stage_name, False):
   193→                blocked_reason = f"{label} blocked until {prerequisite_label} is confirmed."
   194→                next_command = _confirm_stage_command(missing.stage_name)
   195→            else:
   196→                blocked_reason = f"{label} blocked until {prerequisite_label} is recorded."
   197→                next_command = triage_manual_stage_command(missing.stage_name)
   198→        break
   199→
   200→    return TriageProgress(
   201→        stages=tuple(readiness),
   202→        current_stage=current_stage,
   203→        blocked_reason=blocked_reason,
   204→        next_command=next_command,
   205→        completed_count=sum(1 for stage in readiness if stage.recorded),
   206→        confirmed_count=sum(1 for stage in readiness if stage.confirmed),
   207→    )
   208→
   209→
   210→def triage_run_stages_command(
   211→    *,
   212→    runner: str = "codex",
   213→    only_stages: str | tuple[str, ...] | list[str] | None = None,
   214→) -> str:
   215→    """Return the canonical staged triage runner command."""
   216→    resolved_runner = str(runner).strip().lower()
   217→    if resolved_runner not in TRIAGE_RUNNERS:
   218→        supported = ", ".join(TRIAGE_RUNNERS)
   219→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported triage runner: {runner!r}. Valid: {supported}")
   220→
   221→    command = f"desloppify plan triage --run-stages --runner {resolved_runner}"
   222→    if only_stages is None:
   223→        return command
   224→
   225→    if isinstance(only_stages, str):
   226→        stages = [only_stages]
   227→    else:
   228→        stages = [str(stage).strip().lower() for stage in only_stages if str(stage).strip()]
   229→
   230→    invalid = [stage for stage in stages if stage not in _RUNNER_STAGE_NAMES]
   231→    if invalid:
   232→        supported = ", ".join(sorted(_RUNNER_STAGE_NAMES))
   233→        bad = ", ".join(sorted(set(invalid)))
   234→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported triage stage(s): {bad}. Valid: {supported}")
   235→
   236→    return f"{command} --only-stages {','.join(stages)}"
   237→
   238→
   239→def triage_runner_commands(
   240→    *,
   241→    only_stages: str | tuple[str, ...] | list[str] | None = None,
   242→) -> tuple[tuple[str, str], tuple[str, str]]:
   243→    """Return the preferred staged-runner commands for Codex and Claude."""
   244→    return (
   245→        ("Codex", triage_run_stages_command(runner="codex", only_stages=only_stages)),
   246→        ("Claude", triage_run_stages_command(runner="claude", only_stages=only_stages)),
   247→    )
   248→
   249→
   250→def triage_manual_stage_command(stage: str) -> str:
   251→    """Return the manual fallback command for a triage stage."""
   252→    resolved_stage = str(stage).strip().lower()
   253→    if resolved_stage not in _MANUAL_STAGE_COMMANDS:
   254→        supported = ", ".join(sorted(_MANUAL_STAGE_COMMANDS))
   255→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported triage stage: {stage!r}. Valid: {supported}")
   256→    return _MANUAL_STAGE_COMMANDS[resolved_stage]
   257→
   258→
   259→__all__ = [
   260→    "StagePrerequisite",
   261→    "StageReadiness",
   262→    "TriageProgress",
   263→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES",
   264→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS",
   265→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_PREREQUISITES",
   266→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ADD",
   267→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_CREATE",
   268→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH",
   269→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH_COMPACT",
   270→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_STEPS",
   271→    "TRIAGE_CMD_COMPLETE",
   272→    "TRIAGE_CMD_SENSE_CHECK",
   273→    "TRIAGE_CMD_COMPLETE_VERBOSE",

## SPECIAL NOTE: Sense-Check Orchestrator
The primary sense-check handler is in `orchestrator_codex_sense.py` (367 lines). This file currently handles only sense-check validation (content + structure batches). When folding value-check, you'll need to decide whether to:
- **Extend the sense-check handler** to also parse value-check reports after content/structure validation, OR
- **Create a combined sense-and-value handler** that runs value-check decision validation as a final step

The decision ledger parsing (parse_value_check_decision_ledger) will integrate into the sense-check output processing pipeline.

> DEVELOPER

## Task
Fold the "value-check" stage into "sense-check" as a third subagent, reducing the triage pipeline from 6 stages to 5 (observe → reflect → organize → enrich → sense-check → commit).

The current sense-check has 2 parallel subagents: content (per-cluster) and structure (global). We're adding a third: **value** (global), which walks the execution queue item-by-item applying a YAGNI/KISS rubric to decide keep/tighten/skip.

## Current Architecture

### Sense-check today (2 subagents):
1. **Content** (per cluster): reads source files, checks 8 things (over-engineering, staleness, line numbers, names, counts, vagueness, effort tags, duplicates)
2. **Structure** (global): file-touch graph, shared files, missing cascades, circular deps

### Value-check today (separate stage):
- Prerequisite: sense-check confirmed
- Walks queue targets one by one
- Rubric: keep / tighten / skip
- Requires `## Decision Ledger` in report with one line per live queue target
- Validates: all targets covered, no duplicates, no extras, file paths cited, clusters referenced
- Uses `value_check_targets()` helper to enumerate targets (clusters + unclustered review issues)

## Design Requirements

1. **Value becomes the 3rd subagent of sense-check** — runs in parallel with content and structure
2. **YAGNI and KISS** are the core principles of the value rubric
3. Value subagent report is merged into the combined sense-check report
4. Decision Ledger validation moves into sense-check confirmation gate (not recording gate)
5. The `ValueCheckRecord` TypedDict and `record_value_check_stage()` go away — data folds into `SenseCheckRecord`
6. Completion gates: remove value-check gate, sense-check gate absorbs its checks
7. Stage count: TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS drops from 7 to 6 (observe, reflect, organize, enrich, sense-check, commit)

## Value Subagent Prompt Design

The value subagent prompt should emphasize:

```
## VALUE subagent — YAGNI/KISS pass

Your task: walk the execution queue target by target and decide keep/tighten/skip.

### Core principles
- **YAGNI**: You Aren't Gonna Need It. If the fix addresses a hypothetical 
  future problem rather than a concrete present one, skip it.
- **KISS**: Keep It Simple, Stupid. If the fix adds more complexity than it 
  removes, skip it. The codebase should have fewer moving parts after execution, not more.

### Rubric
For every live queue target, choose exactly one:
1. `keep` — clearly simplifies, clarifies, or corrects
2. `tighten` — worth doing but the plan needs to be simpler or more concrete
3. `skip` — the fix adds churn, indirection, or abstraction for too little gain

### What to skip (YAGNI/KISS failures):
- Wrappers, facades, registries that add a layer for one consumer
- Abstractions that trade simple duplication for complex indirection
- "Improve naming" across files when the current names are understood
- Steps whose implementation is more complex than the code they replace
- Theoretical cleanups that don't fix a real confusion or bug

### What to keep:
- Deletions that remove dead code, unused layers, or unnecessary indirection
- Simplifications that reduce branching, nesting, or state
- Focused unifications where inconsistency causes actual confusion
- Bug fixes and correctness improvements

### Required output
Start with:
## Decision Ledger
- target-name -> keep
- target-name -> tighten  
- target-name -> skip

Then short sections with reasoning and file references.
```

1. First: engine/_plan/constants.py and playbook.py (foundation)
2. Then: stage_queue.py, orchestrator_common.py (queue management)

    68→    stage_name for stage_name, _label in TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS if stage_name != "commit"
    69→)
    70→_MANUAL_STAGE_COMMANDS: dict[str, str] = {
    71→    "observe": TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE,
    72→    "reflect": TRIAGE_CMD_REFLECT,
    73→    "organize": TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE,
    74→    "enrich": TRIAGE_CMD_ENRICH,
    75→    "sense-check": TRIAGE_CMD_SENSE_CHECK,
    76→    "value-check": TRIAGE_CMD_VALUE_CHECK,
    77→    "commit": TRIAGE_CMD_COMPLETE,
    78→}
    79→
    80→
    81→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    82→class StagePrerequisite:
    83→    """One required upstream stage for a triage workflow seam."""
    84→
    85→    stage_name: str
    86→    require_confirmation: bool = False
    87→
    88→
    89→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    90→class StageReadiness:
    91→    """Readiness state for one triage stage."""
    92→
    93→    name: str
    94→    recorded: bool
    95→    confirmed: bool
    96→
    97→
    98→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    99→class TriageProgress:
   100→    """Canonical stage progression snapshot for display and validation."""
   101→
   102→    stages: tuple[StageReadiness, ...]
   103→    current_stage: str | None
   104→    blocked_reason: str | None
   105→    next_command: str | None
   106→    completed_count: int
   107→    confirmed_count: int
   108→
   109→
   110→TRIAGE_STAGE_PREREQUISITES: dict[str, tuple[StagePrerequisite, ...]] = {
   111→    "observe": (),
   112→    "reflect": (StagePrerequisite("observe"),),
   113→    "organize": (
   114→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
   115→        StagePrerequisite("reflect"),
   116→    ),
   117→    "enrich": (
   118→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
   119→        StagePrerequisite("reflect"),
   120→        StagePrerequisite("organize", require_confirmation=True),
   121→    ),
   122→    "sense-check": (
   123→        StagePrerequisite("enrich", require_confirmation=True),
   124→    ),
   125→    "value-check": (
   126→        StagePrerequisite("sense-check", require_confirmation=True),
   127→    ),
   128→    "commit": (
   129→        StagePrerequisite("value-check"),
   130→    ),
   131→}
   132→
   133→TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES: dict[str, set[str]] = {
   134→    stage: {prerequisite.stage_name for prerequisite in prerequisites}
   135→    for stage, prerequisites in TRIAGE_STAGE_PREREQUISITES.items()
   136→}
   137→
   138→_STAGE_LABEL_BY_NAME = dict(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   139→
   140→
   141→def _confirm_stage_command(stage_name: str) -> str:
   142→    return f"desloppify plan triage --confirm {stage_name}"
   143→
   144→
   145→def _first_unmet_prerequisite(
   146→    stage_name: str,
   147→    recorded: dict[str, bool],
   148→    confirmed: dict[str, bool],
   149→) -> StagePrerequisite | None:
   150→    for prerequisite in TRIAGE_STAGE_PREREQUISITES[stage_name]:
   151→        if not recorded.get(prerequisite.stage_name, False):
   152→            return prerequisite
   153→        if prerequisite.require_confirmation and not confirmed.get(prerequisite.stage_name, False):
   154→            return prerequisite
   155→    return None
   156→
   157→
   158→def compute_triage_progress(stages_data: dict) -> TriageProgress:
   159→    """Return canonical triage-stage progression for display and validation."""
   160→    stage_map = stages_data if isinstance(stages_data, dict) else {}
   161→    readiness: list[StageReadiness] = []
   162→    recorded: dict[str, bool] = {}
   163→    confirmed: dict[str, bool] = {}
   164→
   165→    for stage_name, _label in TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS:
   166→        payload = stage_map.get(stage_name, {})
   167→        is_recorded = isinstance(payload, dict) and stage_name in stage_map
   168→        is_confirmed = bool(payload.get("confirmed_at")) if isinstance(payload, dict) else False
   169→        readiness.append(
   170→            StageReadiness(
   171→                name=stage_name,
   172→                recorded=is_recorded,
   173→                confirmed=is_confirmed,
   174→            )
   175→        )
   176→        recorded[stage_name] = is_recorded
   177→        confirmed[stage_name] = is_confirmed
   178→
   179→    current_stage: str | None = None
   180→    blocked_reason: str | None = None
   181→    next_command: str | None = None
   182→
   183→    for stage_name, label in TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS:
   184→        if recorded[stage_name]:
   185→            continue
   186→        missing = _first_unmet_prerequisite(stage_name, recorded, confirmed)
   187→        if missing is None:
   188→            current_stage = stage_name
   189→            next_command = triage_manual_stage_command(stage_name)
   190→        else:
   191→            prerequisite_label = _STAGE_LABEL_BY_NAME.get(missing.stage_name, missing.stage_name)
   192→            if missing.require_confirmation and recorded.get(missing.stage_name, False):
   193→                blocked_reason = f"{label} blocked until {prerequisite_label} is confirmed."
   194→                next_command = _confirm_stage_command(missing.stage_name)
   195→            else:
   196→                blocked_reason = f"{label} blocked until {prerequisite_label} is recorded."
   197→                next_command = triage_manual_stage_command(missing.stage_name)
   198→        break
   199→
   200→    return TriageProgress(
   201→        stages=tuple(readiness),
   202→        current_stage=current_stage,
   203→        blocked_reason=blocked_reason,
   204→        next_command=next_command,
   205→        completed_count=sum(1 for stage in readiness if stage.recorded),
   206→        confirmed_count=sum(1 for stage in readiness if stage.confirmed),
   207→    )
   208→
   209→
   210→def triage_run_stages_command(
   211→    *,
   212→    runner: str = "codex",
   213→    only_stages: str | tuple[str, ...] | list[str] | None = None,
   214→) -> str:
   215→    """Return the canonical staged triage runner command."""
   216→    resolved_runner = str(runner).strip().lower()
   217→    if resolved_runner not in TRIAGE_RUNNERS:
   218→        supported = ", ".join(TRIAGE_RUNNERS)
   219→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported triage runner: {runner!r}. Valid: {supported}")
   220→
   221→    command = f"desloppify plan triage --run-stages --runner {resolved_runner}"
   222→    if only_stages is None:
   223→        return command
   224→
   225→    if isinstance(only_stages, str):
   226→        stages = [only_stages]
   227→    else:
   228→        stages = [str(stage).strip().lower() for stage in only_stages if str(stage).strip()]
   229→
   230→    invalid = [stage for stage in stages if stage not in _RUNNER_STAGE_NAMES]
   231→    if invalid:
   232→        supported = ", ".join(sorted(_RUNNER_STAGE_NAMES))
   233→        bad = ", ".join(sorted(set(invalid)))
   234→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported triage stage(s): {bad}. Valid: {supported}")
   235→
   236→    return f"{command} --only-stages {','.join(stages)}"
   237→
   238→
   239→def triage_runner_commands(
   240→    *,
   241→    only_stages: str | tuple[str, ...] | list[str] | None = None,
   242→) -> tuple[tuple[str, str], tuple[str, str]]:
   243→    """Return the preferred staged-runner commands for Codex and Claude."""
   244→    return (
   245→        ("Codex", triage_run_stages_command(runner="codex", only_stages=only_stages)),
   246→        ("Claude", triage_run_stages_command(runner="claude", only_stages=only_stages)),
   247→    )
   248→
   249→
   250→def triage_manual_stage_command(stage: str) -> str:
   251→    """Return the manual fallback command for a triage stage."""
   252→    resolved_stage = str(stage).strip().lower()
   253→    if resolved_stage not in _MANUAL_STAGE_COMMANDS:
   254→        supported = ", ".join(sorted(_MANUAL_STAGE_COMMANDS))
   255→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported triage stage: {stage!r}. Valid: {supported}")
   256→    return _MANUAL_STAGE_COMMANDS[resolved_stage]
   257→
   258→
   259→__all__ = [
   260→    "StagePrerequisite",
   261→    "StageReadiness",
   262→    "TriageProgress",
   263→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES",
   264→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS",
   265→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_PREREQUISITES",
   266→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ADD",
   267→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_CREATE",
   268→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH",
   269→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH_COMPACT",
   270→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_STEPS",
   271→    "TRIAGE_CMD_COMPLETE",
   272→    "TRIAGE_CMD_SENSE_CHECK",
   273→    "TRIAGE_CMD_COMPLETE_VERBOSE",
   274→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CONFIRM_EXISTING",
   275→    "TRIAGE_CMD_ENRICH",
   276→    "TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE",
   277→    "TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE",

   279→    "TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE",
   280→    "TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX",

   283→    "triage_manual_stage_command",

1→"""Queue/stage mutation helpers for triage workflow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix
     9→from desloppify.engine.plan_ops import purge_ids
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import PlanModel
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import TRIAGE_IDS, TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS
    12→
    13→from .plan_state_access import ensure_queue_order, ensure_skipped_map
    14→
    15→STAGE_ORDER = ["observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check", "value-check"]
    16→
    17→
    18→def has_triage_in_queue(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
    19→    """Return True when any triage stage IDs are currently queued."""
    20→    order = set(ensure_queue_order(plan))
    21→    return bool(order & TRIAGE_IDS)
    22→
    23→
    24→def clear_triage_stage_skips(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
    25→    """Remove skipped markers for triage stages before reinjection."""
    26→    skipped = ensure_skipped_map(plan)
    27→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    28→        skipped.pop(sid, None)
    29→
    30→
    31→def inject_triage_stages(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
    32→    """Inject the canonical triage stage IDs at the queue front."""
    33→    order = ensure_queue_order(plan)
    34→    clear_triage_stage_skips(plan)
    35→    remaining = [issue_id for issue_id in order if issue_id not in TRIAGE_IDS]
    36→    order[:] = [*remaining, *TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS]
    37→    normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(order)
    38→
    39→
    40→def purge_triage_stage(plan: PlanModel, stage_name: str) -> None:
    41→    """Remove one triage stage workflow item from the plan."""
    42→    purge_ids(plan, [f"triage::{stage_name}"])
    43→
    44→
    45→def cascade_clear_dispositions(meta: dict[str, Any], from_stage: str) -> None:
    46→    """Reset issue_dispositions when an earlier stage reruns.
    47→
    48→    - observe rerun: wipe the entire disposition map (verdicts change)
    49→    - reflect rerun: clear decision/target/decision_source from all entries
    50→      (observe verdicts remain, but reflect decisions are outdated)
    51→    """
    52→    dispositions = meta.get("issue_dispositions")
    53→    if not dispositions:
    54→        return
    55→    if from_stage == "observe":
    56→        meta["issue_dispositions"] = {}
    57→    elif from_stage == "reflect":
    58→        for entry in dispositions.values():
    59→            entry.pop("decision", None)
    60→            entry.pop("target", None)
    61→            entry.pop("decision_source", None)
    62→
    63→
    64→def cascade_clear_later_confirmations(
    65→    stages: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
    66→    from_stage: str,
    67→) -> list[str]:
    68→    """Clear later-stage confirmations after mutating an earlier stage."""
    69→    try:
    70→        idx = STAGE_ORDER.index(from_stage)
    71→    except ValueError:
    72→        return []
    73→    cleared: list[str] = []
    74→    for later in STAGE_ORDER[idx + 1:]:
    75→        if later in stages and stages[later].get("confirmed_at"):
    76→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_at", None)
    77→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_text", None)
    78→            cleared.append(later)
    79→    return cleared
    80→
    81→
    82→def print_cascade_clear_feedback(
    83→    cleared: list[str],
    84→    stages: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
    85→) -> None:
    86→    """Render confirmation-clearing feedback after stage rewrites."""
    87→    if not cleared:
    88→        return
    89→    print(colorize(f"  Cleared confirmations on: {', '.join(cleared)}", "yellow"))
    90→    next_unconfirmed = next(
    91→        (
    92→            stage
    93→            for stage in STAGE_ORDER
    94→            if stage in stages and not stages[stage].get("confirmed_at")
    95→        ),
    96→        None,
    97→    )
    98→    if next_unconfirmed:
    99→        print(
   100→            colorize(
   101→                f"  Re-confirm with: desloppify plan triage --confirm {next_unconfirmed}",
   102→                "dim",
   103→            )
   104→        )
   105→
   106→
   107→__all__ = [
   108→    "STAGE_ORDER",
   109→    "cascade_clear_dispositions",
   110→    "cascade_clear_later_confirmations",
   111→    "has_triage_in_queue",
   112→    "inject_triage_stages",
   113→    "print_cascade_clear_feedback",
   114→    "purge_triage_stage",
   115→]
   116→
```

1→"""State mutation helpers for triage stage records."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import TypedDict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.state_io import utc_now
     8→
     9→from ..stage_queue import cascade_clear_later_confirmations
    10→
    11→
    12→class ObserveAssessmentRecord(TypedDict):
    13→    """Persisted observe-stage verdict for one cited issue hash."""
    14→
    15→    hash: str
    16→    verdict: str
    17→    verdict_reasoning: str
    18→    files_read: list[str]
    19→    recommendation: str
    20→
    21→
    22→class ObserveRecord(TypedDict, total=False):
    23→    stage: str
    24→    report: str
    25→    cited_ids: list[str]
    26→    timestamp: str
    27→    issue_count: int
    28→    dimension_names: list[str]
    29→    dimension_counts: dict[str, int]
    30→    assessments: list[ObserveAssessmentRecord]
    31→    confirmed_at: str
    32→    confirmed_text: str
    33→
    34→
    35→class ReflectRecord(TypedDict, total=False):
    36→    stage: str
    37→    report: str
    38→    cited_ids: list[str]
    39→    timestamp: str
    40→    issue_count: int
    41→    missing_issue_ids: list[str]
    42→    duplicate_issue_ids: list[str]
    43→    recurring_dims: list[str]
    44→    disposition_ledger: list[dict[str, str]]
    45→    confirmed_at: str
    46→    confirmed_text: str
    47→
    48→
    49→class OrganizeRecord(TypedDict, total=False):
    50→    stage: str
    51→    report: str
    52→    cited_ids: list[str]
    53→    timestamp: str
    54→    issue_count: int
    55→    confirmed_at: str
    56→    confirmed_text: str
    57→    reused_existing_plan: bool
    58→    completion_note: str
    59→
    60→
    61→class EnrichRecord(TypedDict, total=False):
    62→    stage: str
    63→    report: str
    64→    timestamp: str
    65→    shallow_count: int
    66→    confirmed_at: str
    67→    confirmed_text: str
    68→
    69→
    70→class SenseCheckRecord(TypedDict, total=False):
    71→    stage: str
    72→    report: str
    73→    timestamp: str
    74→    confirmed_at: str
    75→    confirmed_text: str
    76→
    77→
    78→class ValueCheckRecord(TypedDict, total=False):
    79→    stage: str
    80→    report: str
    81→    timestamp: str
    82→    confirmed_at: str
    83→    confirmed_text: str
    84→
    85→
    86→TriageStages = dict[
    87→    str,
    88→    ObserveRecord | ReflectRecord | OrganizeRecord | EnrichRecord | SenseCheckRecord | ValueCheckRecord,
    89→]
    90→
    91→
    92→def resolve_reusable_report(
    93→    report: str | None,
    94→    existing_stage: dict | ObserveRecord | ReflectRecord | OrganizeRecord | EnrichRecord | SenseCheckRecord | ValueCheckRecord | None,
    95→) -> tuple[str | None, bool]:
    96→    if report:
    97→        return report, False
    98→    if existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
    99→        return existing_stage["report"], True
   100→    return None, False
   101→
   102→
   103→def record_observe_stage(
   104→    stages: TriageStages,
   105→    *,
   106→    report: str,
   107→    issue_count: int,
   108→    cited_ids: list[str],
   109→    existing_stage: ObserveRecord | None,
   110→    is_reuse: bool,
   111→    assessments: list[ObserveAssessmentRecord] | None = None,
   112→    dimension_names: list[str] | None = None,
   113→    dimension_counts: dict[str, int] | None = None,
   114→) -> list[str]:
   115→    observe: ObserveRecord = {
   116→        "stage": "observe",
   117→        "report": report,
   118→        "cited_ids": cited_ids,
   119→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   120→        "issue_count": issue_count,
   121→    }
   122→    if dimension_names is not None:
   123→        observe["dimension_names"] = dimension_names
   124→    if dimension_counts is not None:
   125→        observe["dimension_counts"] = dimension_counts
   126→    if assessments is not None:
   127→        observe["assessments"] = assessments
   128→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
   129→        observe["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
   130→        observe["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
   131→    stages["observe"] = observe
   132→    cleared = cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "observe")
   133→    if not is_reuse:
   134→        observe.pop("confirmed_at", None)
   135→        observe.pop("confirmed_text", None)
   136→    return cleared

     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from collections.abc import Callable
     7→from dataclasses import dataclass

    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    11→
    12→from .records import record_value_check_stage, resolve_reusable_report

    14→    format_evidence_failures,
    15→    parse_value_check_decision_ledger,
    16→    validate_report_has_file_paths,
    17→    validate_report_references_clusters,
    18→)

    21→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    22→from ..review_coverage import manual_clusters_with_issues

    25→ColorizeFn = Callable[[str, str], str]
    26→
    27→
    28→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    29→class ValueCheckStageDeps:
    30→    """Dependency container for focused value-check stage testing."""
    31→
    32→    has_triage_in_queue: Callable[[dict], bool] = has_triage_in_queue
    33→    resolve_reusable_report: Callable[[str | None, dict | None], tuple[str | None, bool]] = (
    34→        resolve_reusable_report
    35→    )
    36→    record_value_check_stage: Callable[..., list[str]] = record_value_check_stage
    37→    value_check_targets: Callable[[dict, dict | None], list[str]] = value_check_targets
    38→    manual_clusters_with_issues: Callable[[dict], list[str]] = manual_clusters_with_issues
    39→    colorize: ColorizeFn = colorize
    40→    default_triage_services: Callable[[], TriageServices] = default_triage_services
    41→    print_cascade_clear_feedback: Callable[[list[str], dict], None] = print_cascade_clear_feedback
    42→    get_project_root: Callable[[], Path] | None = None
    43→
    44→
    45→def _report_length_ok(report: str | None, *, colorize_fn: ColorizeFn) -> bool:
    46→    if not report:
    47→        print(colorize_fn("  --report is required for --stage value-check.", "red"))
    48→        print(
    49→            colorize_fn(
    50→                "  Include a Decision Ledger with one line per live queue target: keep, tighten, or skip.",
    51→                "dim",
    52→            )
    53→        )
    54→        return False
    55→    if len(report.strip()) < 100:
    56→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Report too short: {len(report.strip())} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
    57→        return False
    58→    return True
    59→
    60→
    61→def _quality_problems(plan: dict, *, deps: ValueCheckStageDeps) -> list[str]:
    62→    get_project_root = deps.get_project_root
    63→    if get_project_root is None:
    64→        from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    65→
    66→    repo_root = get_project_root()
    67→    quality_report = evaluate_enrich_quality(
    68→        plan,
    69→        repo_root,
    70→        phase_label="sense-check",
    71→        bad_paths_severity="warning",
    72→        missing_effort_severity="failure",
    73→        include_missing_issue_refs=True,
    74→        include_vague_detail=True,
    75→        stale_issue_refs_severity=None,
    76→    )
    77→    return [issue.message for issue in quality_report.failures]
    78→
    79→
    80→def _ledger_failures(report: str, *, targets: list[str]) -> list[str]:
    81→    parsed = parse_value_check_decision_ledger(report)
    82→    failures: list[str] = []
    83→    if not parsed.entries:
    84→        failures.append(
    85→            "Report is missing a `## Decision Ledger` with `- cluster-or-id -> keep|tighten|skip` lines."
    86→        )
    87→        return failures
    88→    if parsed.duplicates:
    89→        dupes = ", ".join(parsed.duplicates[:5])
    90→        failures.append(f"Decision Ledger repeats target(s): {dupes}")
    91→    missing = [target for target in targets if target not in parsed.entries]
    92→    extra = [target for target in parsed.entries if target not in targets]
    93→    if missing:
    94→        failures.append(
    95→            "Decision Ledger is missing target(s): " + ", ".join(missing[:5])
    96→        )
    97→    if extra:
    98→        failures.append(
    99→            "Decision Ledger references non-live target(s): " + ", ".join(extra[:5])
   100→        )
   101→    return failures
   102→
   103→
   104→def run_stage_value_check(
   105→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   106→    *,
   107→    services: TriageServices | None,
   108→    deps: ValueCheckStageDeps | None = None,
   109→) -> None:
   110→    """Record the VALUE-CHECK stage after final keep/tighten/skip review."""
   111→    resolved_deps = deps or ValueCheckStageDeps()
   112→    resolved_services = services or resolved_deps.default_triage_services()
   113→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   114→    state = resolved_services.command_runtime(args).state
   115→
   116→    if not resolved_deps.has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   117→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to value-check.", "yellow"))
   118→        return
   119→
   120→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   121→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   122→    existing_stage = stages.get("value-check")
   123→    report = getattr(args, "report", None)
   124→    report, is_reuse = resolved_deps.resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
   125→
   126→    if not stages.get("sense-check", {}).get("confirmed_at"):
   127→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Cannot value-check: sense-check stage not confirmed.", "red"))
   128→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Run: desloppify plan triage --confirm sense-check", "dim"))
   129→        return
   130→
   131→    problems = _quality_problems(plan, deps=resolved_deps)
   132→    if problems:
   133→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Cannot record value-check — plan still has issues:", "red"))
   134→        for problem in problems:
   135→            print(resolved_deps.colorize(f"    • {problem}", "yellow"))
   136→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Fix those before recording the final keep/skip pass.", "dim"))
   137→        return
   138→
   139→    if not _report_length_ok(report, colorize_fn=resolved_deps.colorize):
   140→        return
   141→
   142→    targets = resolved_deps.value_check_targets(plan, state)
   143→    ledger_failures = _ledger_failures(report, targets=targets)
   144→    if ledger_failures:
   145→        for failure in ledger_failures:
   146→            print(resolved_deps.colorize(f"  {failure}", "red"))
   147→        return
   148→
   149→    evidence_failures = validate_report_has_file_paths(report)
   150→    cluster_names = resolved_deps.manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   151→    evidence_failures.extend(validate_report_references_clusters(report, cluster_names))
   152→    blocking = [failure for failure in evidence_failures if failure.blocking]
   153→    if blocking:
   154→        print(
   155→            resolved_deps.colorize(
   156→                format_evidence_failures(blocking, stage_label="value-check"),
   157→                "red",
   158→            )
   159→        )
   160→        return
   161→
   162→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   163→    cleared = resolved_deps.record_value_check_stage(
   164→        stages,
   165→        report=report,
   166→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   167→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   168→    )
   169→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   170→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   171→        plan,
   172→        "triage_value_check",
   173→        actor="user",
   174→        detail={"target_count": len(targets), "reuse": is_reuse},
   175→    )
   176→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   177→
   178→    print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Value-check stage recorded.", "green"))
   179→    if is_reuse:
   180→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Value-check data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   181→        if cleared:
   182→            resolved_deps.print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   183→    else:
   184→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Now confirm the value-check.", "yellow"))
   185→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm value-check", "dim"))
   186→
   187→
   188→def cmd_stage_value_check(
   189→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   190→    *,
   191→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   192→) -> None:
   193→    """Public entrypoint for value-check stage recording."""
   194→    run_stage_value_check(args, services=services)
   195→
   196→
   197→__all__ = [
   198→    "ValueCheckStageDeps",
   199→    "cmd_stage_value_check",
   200→    "run_stage_value_check",
   201→]
   202→
