1→"""Stage-gate and coverage helpers for triage command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import is_synthetic_id
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.issue_semantics import is_triage_finding
     8→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import TRIAGE_IDS
     9→
    10→from ..review_coverage import (
    11→    cluster_issue_ids,
    12→    live_active_triage_issue_ids,
    13→    manual_clusters_with_issues,
    14→)
    15→
    16→
    17→def _require_triage_pending(plan: dict, *, action: str) -> bool:
    18→    """Require at least one triage stage ID to be present in queue for an action."""
    19→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    20→    if order & TRIAGE_IDS:
    21→        return True
    22→    print(colorize(f"  No triage stage in the queue — nothing to {action}.", "yellow"))
    23→    return False
    24→
    25→
    26→def _validate_stage_report(
    27→    report: str | None,
    28→    *,
    29→    stage: str,
    30→    min_chars: int,
    31→    missing_guidance: list[str] | None = None,
    32→    short_guidance: list[str] | None = None,
    33→) -> str | None:
    34→    """Validate staged report presence/length and print consistent guidance."""
    35→    if not report:
    36→        print(colorize(f"  --report is required for --stage {stage}.", "red"))
    37→        for line in missing_guidance or []:
    38→            print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
    39→        return None
    40→    cleaned = report.strip()
    41→    if len(cleaned) < min_chars:
    42→        print(
    43→            colorize(
    44→                f"  Report too short: {len(cleaned)} chars (minimum {min_chars}).",
    45→                "red",
    46→            )
    47→        )
    48→        for line in short_guidance or []:
    49→            print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
    50→        return None
    51→    return cleaned
    52→
    53→
    54→def unenriched_clusters(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, list[str]]]:
    55→    """Return clusters with issues that are missing required enrichment.
    56→
    57→    Requirements:
    58→    - Every cluster needs a description and at least one action_step.
    59→    - Small clusters (< 5 issues) need at least 1 action step per issue,
    60→      so each item has a concrete plan. Large clusters (>= 5) just need
    61→      steps overall (cluster-level plan is sufficient).
    62→    """
    63→    gaps: list[tuple[str, list[str]]] = []
    64→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    65→        issue_ids = cluster_issue_ids(cluster)
    66→        if not issue_ids:
    67→            continue
    68→        if cluster.get("auto"):
    69→            continue
    70→        missing: list[str] = []
    71→        if not cluster.get("description"):
    72→            missing.append("description")
    73→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps") or []
    74→        issue_count = len(issue_ids)
    75→        if not steps:
    76→            missing.append("action_steps")
    77→        elif issue_count < 5 and len(steps) < issue_count:
    78→            missing.append(
    79→                f"action_steps (have {len(steps)}, need >= {issue_count} for small cluster)"
    80→            )
    81→        if missing:
    82→            gaps.append((name, missing))
    83→    return gaps
    84→
    85→
    86→def unclustered_review_issues(plan: dict, state: dict | None = None) -> list[str]:
    87→    """Return review issue IDs that aren't in any manual cluster.
    88→
    89→    When *state* is provided, uses open review/concerns issues from state
    90→    (the canonical source). Falls back to scanning queue_order for backwards
    91→    compatibility.
    92→    """
    93→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    94→    clustered_ids: set[str] = set()
    95→    for cluster in clusters.values():
    96→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
    97→            clustered_ids.update(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
    98→    skipped_ids = {
    99→        fid for fid in (plan.get("skipped", {}) or {}).keys() if isinstance(fid, str)
   100→    }
   101→
   102→    if state is not None:
   103→        review_ids = [
   104→            fid for fid, finding in (state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {})).items()
   105→            if finding.get("status") == "open"
   106→            and is_triage_finding(finding)
   107→        ]
   108→        frozen_ids = (plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}) or {}).get("active_triage_issue_ids")
   109→        if isinstance(frozen_ids, list) and frozen_ids:
   110→            frozen_id_set = live_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
   111→            review_ids = [fid for fid in review_ids if fid in frozen_id_set]
   112→    else:
   113→        review_ids = [
   114→            fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   115→            if not is_synthetic_id(fid)
   116→            and (fid.startswith("review::") or fid.startswith("concerns::"))
   117→        ]
   118→
   119→    return [
   120→        fid for fid in review_ids
   121→        if fid not in clustered_ids and fid not in skipped_ids
   122→    ]
   123→
   124→
   125→def value_check_targets(plan: dict, state: dict | None = None) -> list[str]:
   126→    """Return the current execution targets that value-check must judge once each."""
   127→    targets = list(manual_clusters_with_issues(plan))
   128→    targets.extend(unclustered_review_issues(plan, state))
   129→    seen: set[str] = set()
   130→    ordered: list[str] = []
   131→    for target in targets:
   132→        if target in seen:
   133→            continue
   134→        seen.add(target)
   135→        ordered.append(target)
   136→    return ordered
   137→
```

    27→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    28→    *,
    29→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
    30→) -> bool:

1→"""Shared helpers for triage runner orchestrators."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from datetime import UTC, datetime
     6→
     7→STAGES: tuple[str, ...] = (
     8→    "observe",
     9→    "reflect",
    10→    "organize",
    11→    "enrich",
    12→    "sense-check",

    16→
    17→def parse_only_stages(raw: str | None) -> list[str]:
    18→    """Parse --only-stages comma-separated string into validated stage list."""
    19→    if not raw:
    20→        return list(STAGES)
    21→    stages = [s.strip().lower() for s in raw.split(",") if s.strip()]
    22→    for stage in stages:
    23→        if stage not in STAGES:
    24→            raise ValueError(f"Unknown stage: {stage!r}. Valid: {', '.join(STAGES)}")
    25→    return stages
    26→
    27→
    28→def run_stamp() -> str:
    29→    return datetime.now(UTC).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    30→
```

   122→def _content_tasks_and_meta(
   123→    *,
   124→    clusters: list[str],
   125→    plan: dict,
   126→    repo_root: Path,
   127→    prompts_dir: Path,
   128→    output_dir: Path,
   129→    logs_dir: Path,
   130→    timeout_seconds: int,

   134→    cli_command: str,
   135→    log: Callable[[str], None],
   136→) -> tuple[dict[int, Callable[[], TriageStageRunResult]], list[tuple[str, Path]]]:
   137→    tasks: dict[int, Callable[[], TriageStageRunResult]] = {}
   138→    batch_meta: list[tuple[str, Path]] = []
   139→    for i, cluster_name in enumerate(clusters):
   140→        config = _content_batch_config(
   141→            cluster_name=cluster_name,
   142→            batch_index=i,
   143→            plan=plan,
   144→            repo_root=repo_root,
   145→            prompts_dir=prompts_dir,
   146→            output_dir=output_dir,
   147→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   148→            policy_text=policy_text,
   149→            mode=content_mode,
   150→            cli_command=cli_command,
   151→        )
   152→        _write_batch_prompt(config)
   153→        batch_meta.append((config.label, config.output_file))
   154→        if not dry_run:
   155→            tasks[i] = partial(
   156→                run_triage_stage,
   157→                prompt=config.prompt,
   158→                repo_root=repo_root,
   159→                output_file=config.output_file,
   160→                log_file=config.log_file,
   161→                timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   162→                validate_output_fn=_output_file_has_text,
   163→            )
   164→        print(colorize(f"    Content batch {i + 1}: {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   165→        log(f"sense-check-content batch={i + 1} cluster={cluster_name}")
   166→    return tasks, batch_meta
   167→
   168→
   169→def _run_parallel_or_fail(
   170→    *,
   171→    tasks: dict[int, Callable[[], TriageStageRunResult]],
   172→    stage_label: str,
   173→    batch_label_fn: Callable[[int], str],
   174→    log: Callable[[str], None],
   175→) -> list[int]:
   176→    return run_parallel_batches(
   177→        tasks=tasks,
   178→        stage_label=stage_label,
   179→        batch_label_fn=batch_label_fn,
   180→        append_run_log=log,
   181→        heartbeat_seconds=15.0,
   182→    )
   183→
   184→
   185→def _content_failures(
   186→    *,
   187→    tasks: dict[int, Callable[[], TriageStageRunResult]],
   188→    clusters: list[str],
   189→    log: Callable[[str], None],
   190→) -> list[int]:
   191→    def _content_label(idx: int) -> str:
   192→        if idx < len(clusters):
   193→            return f"content:{clusters[idx]}"
   194→        return f"content:{idx}"
   195→
   196→    return _run_parallel_or_fail(
   197→        tasks=tasks,
   198→        stage_label="Sense-check",
   199→        batch_label_fn=_content_label,
   200→        log=log,
   201→    )
   202→
   203→
   204→def _parallel_failure_result(
   205→    failures: list[int],
   206→    *,
   207→    log: Callable[[str], None],
   208→) -> TriageStageRunResult:
   209→    print(colorize(f"  Sense-check: {len(failures)} batch(es) failed: {failures}", "red"))
   210→    log(f"sense-check-parallel-failed failures={failures}")
   211→    return TriageStageRunResult(
   212→        exit_code=1,
   213→        reason="parallel_execution_failed",
   214→    )
   215→
   216→
   217→def _reload_structure_plan(
   218→    *,
   219→    reload_plan: Callable[[], dict] | None,
   220→    log: Callable[[str], None],
   221→) -> dict | None:
   222→    if reload_plan is None:
   223→        return None
   224→    try:
   225→        reloaded = dict(reload_plan())
   226→        log("sense-check-plan-reloaded phase=structure")
   227→        return reloaded
   228→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:  # pragma: no cover - defensive fallback
   229→        print(colorize("  Sense-check: failed to reload plan after content updates.", "red"))
   230→        log(f"sense-check-plan-reload-failed error={exc}")
   231→        return None
   232→
   233→
   234→def _merge_batch_outputs(batch_meta: list[tuple[str, Path]]) -> str:
   235→    parts: list[str] = []
   236→    for label, output_file in batch_meta:
   237→        content = ""
   238→        if output_file.exists():
   239→            try:
   240→                content = output_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").strip()
   241→            except OSError:
   242→                content = "(output missing)"
   243→        if not content:
   244→            content = "(no output)"
   245→        parts.append(f"## {label}\n\n{content}")
   246→    return "\n\n---\n\n".join(parts)
   247→
   248→
   249→def run_sense_check(
   250→    *,
   251→    plan: dict,
   252→    repo_root: Path,
   253→    prompts_dir: Path,
   254→    output_dir: Path,
   255→    logs_dir: Path,
   256→    timeout_seconds: int,
   257→    dry_run: bool = False,
   258→    cli_command: str = "desloppify",
   259→    apply_updates: bool = False,
   260→    reload_plan: Callable[[], dict] | None = None,
   261→    append_run_log=None,
   262→) -> TriageStageRunResult:
   263→    """Run sense-check via parallel codex subprocess batches."""
   264→    _log = append_run_log or _noop_log
   265→
   266→    clusters = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   267→    total_content = len(clusters)
   268→    total = total_content + 1
   269→    _print_sense_header(total_content, apply_updates=apply_updates, log=_log)
   270→
   271→    policy_result = load_policy_result()
   272→    policy_text = render_policy_block(policy_result.policy)
   273→    if not policy_result.ok:
   274→        print(
   275→            colorize(
   276→                f"  Warning: ignoring malformed project policy ({policy_result.message or 'unknown error'}).",
   277→                "yellow",
   278→            )
   279→        )
   280→    content_mode, structure_mode = _sense_modes(apply_updates=apply_updates)
   281→    content_tasks, batch_meta = _content_tasks_and_meta(
   282→        clusters=clusters,
   283→        plan=plan,
   284→        repo_root=repo_root,
   285→        prompts_dir=prompts_dir,
   286→        output_dir=output_dir,
   287→        logs_dir=logs_dir,
   288→        timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   289→        dry_run=dry_run,
   290→        policy_text=policy_text,
   291→        content_mode=content_mode,
   292→        cli_command=cli_command,
   293→        log=_log,
   294→    )
   295→    structure_plan = dict(plan)
   296→    structure_config = _structure_batch_config(
   297→        plan=structure_plan,
   298→        repo_root=repo_root,
   299→        prompts_dir=prompts_dir,
   300→        output_dir=output_dir,
   301→        logs_dir=logs_dir,
   302→        mode=structure_mode,
   303→        cli_command=cli_command,
   304→    )
   305→    _write_batch_prompt(structure_config)
   306→    batch_meta.append((structure_config.label, structure_config.output_file))
   307→    print(colorize("    Structure batch: global dependency check", "dim"))
   308→    _log("sense-check-structure batch=global")
   309→
   310→    if dry_run:
   311→        if apply_updates:
   312→            print(colorize("  [dry-run] Would execute sequenced sense-check batches.", "dim"))
   313→        else:
   314→            print(colorize("  [dry-run] Would execute parallel sense-check batches.", "dim"))
   315→        return TriageStageRunResult(exit_code=0, reason="dry_run", dry_run=True)
   316→
   317→    if content_tasks:
   318→        content_failures = _content_failures(tasks=content_tasks, clusters=clusters, log=_log)
   319→        if content_failures:
   320→            return _parallel_failure_result(content_failures, log=_log)
   321→
   322→    if apply_updates and reload_plan is not None:
   323→        reloaded_plan = _reload_structure_plan(reload_plan=reload_plan, log=_log)
   324→        if reloaded_plan is None:
   325→            return TriageStageRunResult(exit_code=1, reason="plan_reload_failed")
   326→        structure_plan = reloaded_plan
   327→        structure_config = _structure_batch_config(
   328→            plan=structure_plan,
   329→            repo_root=repo_root,
   330→            prompts_dir=prompts_dir,
   331→            output_dir=output_dir,
   332→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   333→            mode=structure_mode,
   334→            cli_command=cli_command,
   335→        )
   336→        _write_batch_prompt(structure_config)
   337→
   338→    structure_tasks: dict[int, Callable[[], TriageStageRunResult]] = {
   339→        0: partial(
   340→            run_triage_stage,
   341→            prompt=structure_config.prompt,
   342→            repo_root=repo_root,
   343→            output_file=structure_config.output_file,
   344→            log_file=structure_config.log_file,
   345→            timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   346→            validate_output_fn=_output_file_has_text,
   347→        )
   348→    }
   349→    structure_failures = _run_parallel_or_fail(
   350→        tasks=structure_tasks,
   351→        stage_label="Sense-check",
   352→        batch_label_fn=lambda _idx: "structure",
   353→        log=_log,
   354→    )
   355→    if structure_failures:
   356→        return _parallel_failure_result(structure_failures, log=_log)
   357→
   358→    merged = _merge_batch_outputs(batch_meta)
   359→    print(colorize(f"  Sense-check: merged {total} batch outputs ({len(merged)} chars).", "green"))
   360→    _log(f"sense-check-parallel-done merged_chars={len(merged)}")
   361→    return TriageStageRunResult(
   362→        exit_code=0,
   363→        merged_output=merged,
   364→    )
   365→
   366→
   367→__all__ = ["run_sense_check"]
   368→

   207→    validate_reflect_issue_accounting: Callable[
   208→        ...,
   209→        tuple[bool, set[str], list[str], list[str]],
   210→    ],
   211→) -> tuple[bool, str | None]:
   212→    """Fail fast when a requested stage has invalid upstream prerequisites."""
   213→    # Dry-run previews do not persist upstream stage reports, so preflight checks that
   214→    # require recorded reflect/enrich state would otherwise fail by construction.
   215→    if dry_run and stage in {"organize", "sense-check", "value-check"}:
   216→        return True, None
   217→
   218→    if stage == "sense-check":
   219→        stages = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_stages", {})
   220→        progress = compute_triage_progress(stages)
   221→        if "sense-check" in stages or progress.current_stage == "sense-check":
   222→            return True, None
   223→        reason = "enrich_not_confirmed"
   224→        append_run_log(f"stage-preflight-failed stage={stage} reason={reason}")
   225→        return False, reason
   226→
   227→    if stage == "value-check":
   228→        stages = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_stages", {})
   229→        progress = compute_triage_progress(stages)
   230→        if "value-check" in stages or progress.current_stage == "value-check":
   231→            return True, None
   232→        reason = "sense_check_not_confirmed"
   233→        append_run_log(f"stage-preflight-failed stage={stage} reason={reason}")
   234→        return False, reason
   235→
   236→    if stage != "organize":
   237→        return True, None
   238→    stages = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_stages", {})
   239→    reflect_report = str(stages.get("reflect", {}).get("report", ""))
   240→    accounting_ok, _cited, missing_ids, duplicate_ids = validate_reflect_issue_accounting(
   241→        report=reflect_report,
   242→        valid_ids=set(
   243→            getattr(triage_input, "review_issues", getattr(triage_input, "open_issues", {})).keys()
   244→        ),
   245→    )
   246→    if not accounting_ok:
   247→        reason_parts: list[str] = []
   248→        if missing_ids:
   249→            reason_parts.append(f"missing={len(missing_ids)}")
   250→        if duplicate_ids:
   251→            reason_parts.append(f"duplicates={len(duplicate_ids)}")
   252→        reason = "reflect_accounting_invalid"
   253→        if reason_parts:
   254→            reason = f"{reason}({' '.join(reason_parts)})"
   255→        append_run_log(f"stage-preflight-failed stage={stage} reason={reason}")
   256→        return False, reason
   257→
   258→    # Self-record stages mutate plan state from within the subagent, so their
   259→    # postconditions cannot be required before launch. The organize command
   260→    # itself validates ledger/disposition alignment when it records the stage.
   261→    if prompt_mode == "self_record":
   262→        return True, None
   263→
   264→    # Output-only organize paths may rely on pre-applied plan mutations.
   265→    ledger_mismatches = validate_organize_against_reflect_ledger(
   266→        plan=dict(plan), stages=stages,
   267→    )
   268→    if ledger_mismatches:
   269→        mismatch_types = set()
   270→        for m in ledger_mismatches:
   271→            mismatch_types.add(m.expected_decision)
   272→        reason = f"reflect_ledger_mismatch(count={len(ledger_mismatches)} types={','.join(sorted(mismatch_types))})"
   273→        append_run_log(f"stage-preflight-failed stage={stage} reason={reason}")
   274→        return False, reason
   275→
   276→    return True, None
   277→
   278→
   279→def build_reflect_repair_prompt(
   280→    *,
   281→    triage_input: Any,
   282→    prior_reports: Mapping[str, str],
   283→    repo_root: Path,
   284→    cli_command: str,
   285→    original_report: str,
   286→    missing_ids: list[str],
   287→    duplicate_ids: list[str],
   288→    build_stage_prompt_fn: Callable[..., str],
   289→    stages_data: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
   290→) -> str:
   291→    """Build a targeted retry prompt for a reflect report that failed accounting."""
   292→    missing_short = ", ".join(issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1] for issue_id in missing_ids) or "none"
   293→    duplicate_short = (
   294→        ", ".join(issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1] for issue_id in duplicate_ids) or "none"
   295→    )
   296→    base_prompt = build_stage_prompt_fn(
   297→        "reflect",
   298→        triage_input,
   299→        dict(prior_reports),
   300→        repo_root=repo_root,
   301→        mode="output_only",
   302→        cli_command=cli_command,
   303→        stages_data=stages_data,
   304→    )
   305→    return "\n\n".join(
   306→        [
   307→            base_prompt,
   308→            "## Repair Pass",
   309→            "Your previous reflect report failed the exact-hash accounting check.",
   310→            f"Missing hashes: {missing_short}",
   311→            f"Duplicated hashes: {duplicate_short}",
   312→            "Rewrite the FULL reflect report so it passes validation.",
   313→            "Requirements for this repair:",
   314→            "- Start with a `## Coverage Ledger` section.",
   315→            '- Use one ledger line per issue hash: `- abcd1234 -> cluster "name"` or `- abcd1234 -> skip "reason"`.',
   316→            "- Mention every required hash exactly once in that ledger.",
   317→            "- Do not mention hashes anywhere else in the report.",
   318→            "- Preserve the same strategy unless fixing the missing/duplicate hashes forces a small adjustment.",
   319→            "- Output only the corrected reflect report.",
   320→            "## Previous Reflect Report",
   321→            original_report,
   322→        ]
   323→    )
   324→
   325→
   326→def repair_reflect_report_if_needed(
   327→    *,
   328→    report: str,
   329→    triage_input: Any,
   330→    prior_reports: Mapping[str, str],
   331→    repo_root: Path,
   332→    prompts_dir: Path,
   333→    output_dir: Path,
   334→    logs_dir: Path,
   335→    cli_command: str,
   336→    timeout_seconds: int,
   337→    append_run_log: Callable[[str], None],
   338→    dependencies: StageExecutionDependencies,
   339→    stages_data: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
   340→) -> tuple[str | None, str | None]:
   341→    """Retry reflect once with a targeted repair prompt when accounting is invalid."""
   342→    _cited, missing_ids, duplicate_ids = dependencies.analyze_reflect_issue_accounting(
   343→        report=report,
   344→        valid_ids=set(
   345→            getattr(triage_input, "review_issues", getattr(triage_input, "open_issues", {})).keys()
   346→        ),
   347→    )
   348→    if not missing_ids and not duplicate_ids:
   349→        return report, None
   350→
   351→    print(colorize("  Reflect: repairing missing/duplicate hash accounting...", "yellow"))
   352→    append_run_log(
   353→        "stage-reflect-repair-start "
   354→        f"missing={len(missing_ids)} duplicates={len(duplicate_ids)}"
   355→    )
   356→
   357→    repair_prompt = build_reflect_repair_prompt(
   358→        triage_input=triage_input,
   359→        prior_reports=prior_reports,
   360→        repo_root=repo_root,
   361→        cli_command=cli_command,
   362→        original_report=report,
   363→        missing_ids=missing_ids,
   364→        duplicate_ids=duplicate_ids,
   365→        build_stage_prompt_fn=dependencies.build_stage_prompt,
   366→        stages_data=stages_data,
   367→    )
   368→    repair_prompt_file = prompts_dir / "reflect.repair.md"
   369→    repair_output_file = output_dir / "reflect.repair.raw.txt"
   370→    repair_log_file = logs_dir / "reflect.repair.log"
   371→    safe_write_text(repair_prompt_file, repair_prompt)
   372→    stage_result = dependencies.run_triage_stage(
   373→        prompt=repair_prompt,
   374→        repo_root=repo_root,
   375→        output_file=repair_output_file,
   376→        log_file=repair_log_file,
   377→        timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   378→    )
   379→    append_run_log(f"stage-reflect-repair-done code={stage_result.exit_code}")
   380→    if not stage_result.ok:
   381→        return None, f"reflect_repair_failed_exit_{stage_result.exit_code}"
   382→
   383→    repaired_report = dependencies.read_stage_output(repair_output_file)
   384→    if not repaired_report:
   385→        return None, "reflect_repair_empty_output"
   386→
   387→    _cited, missing_after, duplicates_after = dependencies.analyze_reflect_issue_accounting(
   388→        report=repaired_report,
   389→        valid_ids=set(
   390→            getattr(triage_input, "review_issues", getattr(triage_input, "open_issues", {})).keys()
   391→        ),
   392→    )
   393→    if missing_after or duplicates_after:
   394→        return None, "reflect_repair_invalid"
   395→
   396→    print(colorize("  Reflect: repair pass fixed issue accounting.", "green"))
   397→    append_run_log("stage-reflect-repair-success")
   398→    return repaired_report, None
   399→
   400→
   401→def _execute_parallel_stage(
   402→    *,
   403→    context: StageRunContext,
   404→    stage: str,
   405→    handler: StageHandler | None,
   406→) -> StageExecutionResult:
   407→    """Execute optional parallel stage path."""
   408→    if handler is None or handler.run_parallel is None:
   409→        return StageExecutionResult(status="ready", payload={})
   410→
   411→    parallel_result = handler.run_parallel(context)
   412→    if parallel_result.status == "dry_run":
   413→        return StageExecutionResult(
   414→            status="dry_run",
   415→            payload={"status": "dry_run"},
   416→            used_parallel=True,
   417→        )
   418→
   419→    if parallel_result.ok and parallel_result.merged_output:
   420→        if handler.record_report is not None:
   421→            handler.record_report(parallel_result.merged_output, context.args, context.services)
   422→            return StageExecutionResult(status="ready", payload={}, used_parallel=True)
   423→        return StageExecutionResult(status="ready", payload={})
   424→
   425→    if parallel_result.ok:
   426→        return StageExecutionResult(status="ready", payload={})
   427→
   428→    elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - context.stage_start)
   429→    error_reason = parallel_result.reason or "parallel_execution_failed"
   430→    print(colorize(f"  {stage.capitalize()}: parallel execution failed. Aborting.", "red"))
   431→    context.append_run_log(
   432→        f"stage-failed stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s reason={error_reason}"
   433→    )
   434→    return StageExecutionResult(
   435→        status="failed",
   436→        payload={
   437→            "status": "failed",
   438→            "elapsed_seconds": elapsed,
   439→            "error": error_reason,
   440→        },
   441→        used_parallel=True,
   442→        elapsed_seconds=elapsed,
   443→    )
   444→
   445→
   446→def _failure_result(
   447→    *,
   448→    stage: str,
   449→    elapsed: int | None,
   450→    error: str,
   451→    append_run_log: Callable[[str], None] | None = None,
   452→    log_event: str = "stage-failed",
   453→    log_reason: str | None = None,
   454→    printed_message: str | None = None,
   455→) -> StageExecutionResult:
   456→    if printed_message is not None:
   457→        print(colorize(printed_message, "red"))
   458→    if append_run_log is not None:
   459→        log_parts = [f"{log_event} stage={stage}"]
   460→        if elapsed is not None:
   461→            log_parts.append(f"elapsed={elapsed}s")
   462→        log_parts.append(f"reason={log_reason or error}")
   463→        append_run_log(" ".join(log_parts))
   464→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
   465→        "status": "failed",
   466→        "error": error,
   467→    }
   468→    if elapsed is not None:
   469→        payload["elapsed_seconds"] = elapsed
   470→    return StageExecutionResult(
   471→        status="failed",
   472→        payload=payload,
   473→        elapsed_seconds=elapsed,
   474→    )
   475→
   476→
   477→def _build_subprocess_prompt(
   478→    *,
   479→    context: StageRunContext,
   480→    stage: str,
   481→    prompt_mode: PromptMode,
   482→    dependencies: StageExecutionDependencies,
   483→) -> tuple[str, Mapping[str, Any]]:
   484→    """Build and persist one stage prompt for subprocess execution."""
   485→    stages_data = context.plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_stages", {})
   486→    prompt = dependencies.build_stage_prompt(
   487→        stage,
   488→        context.triage_input,
   489→        dict(context.prior_reports),
   490→        repo_root=context.repo_root,
   491→        mode=prompt_mode,
   492→        cli_command=context.cli_command,
   493→        stages_data=stages_data,
   494→    )
   495→    prompt_file = context.prompts_dir / f"{stage}.md"
   496→    safe_write_text(prompt_file, prompt)
   497→    return prompt, stages_data
   498→
   499→
   500→def _run_subprocess_stage(
   501→    *,
   502→    context: StageRunContext,
   503→    stage: str,
   504→    prompt: str,
   505→    dependencies: StageExecutionDependencies,
   506→) -> StageExecutionResult:
   507→    """Run codex subprocess for one stage (or emit dry-run status)."""
   508→    prompt_file = context.prompts_dir / f"{stage}.md"
   509→    if context.dry_run:
   510→        print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: prompt written to {prompt_file}", "cyan"))
   511→        print(colorize("  [dry-run] Would execute codex subprocess.", "dim"))
   512→        return StageExecutionResult(
   513→            status="dry_run",
   514→            payload={"status": "dry_run"},
   515→        )
   516→
   517→    print(colorize(f"\n  Stage {stage}: launching codex subprocess...", "bold"))
   518→    context.append_run_log(f"stage-subprocess-start stage={stage}")
   519→
   520→    output_file = context.output_dir / f"{stage}.raw.txt"
   521→    log_file = context.logs_dir / f"{stage}.log"
   522→    stage_result = dependencies.run_triage_stage(
   523→        prompt=prompt,
   524→        repo_root=context.repo_root,
   525→        output_file=output_file,
   526→        log_file=log_file,
   527→        timeout_seconds=context.timeout_seconds,
   528→    )
   529→
   530→    elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - context.stage_start)
   531→    context.append_run_log(
   532→        "stage-subprocess-done "
   533→        f"stage={stage} code={stage_result.exit_code} elapsed={elapsed}s"
   534→    )
   535→
   536→    if stage_result.ok:
   537→        return StageExecutionResult(
   538→            status="ready",
   539→            payload={},
   540→            output_file=output_file,
   541→            elapsed_seconds=elapsed,
   542→        )
   543→
   544→    print(
   545→        colorize(
   546→            "  Stage "
   547→            f"{stage}: codex subprocess failed (exit {stage_result.exit_code}).",
   548→            "red",
   549→        )
   550→    )
   551→    print(colorize(f"  Check log: {log_file}", "dim"))
   552→    print(colorize("  Re-run to resume (confirmed stages are skipped).", "dim"))
   553→    context.append_run_log(
   554→        "stage-failed "
   555→        f"stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s code={stage_result.exit_code}"
   556→    )
   557→    return StageExecutionResult(
   558→        status="failed",
   559→        payload={
   560→            "status": "failed",
   561→            "exit_code": stage_result.exit_code,
   562→            "elapsed_seconds": elapsed,
   563→        },
   564→        elapsed_seconds=elapsed,
   565→    )
   566→
   567→
   568→def _resolve_subprocess_artifacts(
   569→    subprocess_result: StageExecutionResult,
   570→) -> tuple[Path, int] | None:
   571→    output_file = subprocess_result.output_file
   572→    elapsed = subprocess_result.elapsed_seconds
   573→    if output_file is None or elapsed is None:
   574→        return None
   575→    return output_file, elapsed
   576→
   577→
   578→def _record_stage_report_if_needed(
   579→    *,
   580→    context: StageRunContext,
   581→    stage: str,
   582→    handler: StageHandler | None,
   583→    dependencies: StageExecutionDependencies,
   584→    output_file: Path,
   585→    elapsed: int,
   586→    stages_data: Mapping[str, Any],
   587→) -> StageExecutionResult:
   588→    """Record subprocess output for stages that require orchestrator persistence."""
   589→    if handler is None or handler.record_report is None:
   590→        return StageExecutionResult(status="ready", payload={})
   591→
   592→    report = dependencies.read_stage_output(output_file)
   593→    if not report:
   594→        return _failure_result(
   595→            stage=stage,
   596→            elapsed=elapsed,
   597→            error="empty_stage_output",
   598→            append_run_log=context.append_run_log,
   599→            printed_message=f"  Stage {stage}: output file was empty after subprocess.",
   600→        )
   601→
   602→    if stage == "reflect":
   603→        report, repair_error = repair_reflect_report_if_needed(
   604→            report=report,
   605→            triage_input=context.triage_input,
   606→            prior_reports=context.prior_reports,
   607→            repo_root=context.repo_root,
   608→            prompts_dir=context.prompts_dir,
   609→            output_dir=context.output_dir,
   610→            logs_dir=context.logs_dir,
   611→            cli_command=context.cli_command,
   612→            timeout_seconds=context.timeout_seconds,
   613→            append_run_log=context.append_run_log,
   614→            dependencies=dependencies,
   615→            stages_data=stages_data,
   616→        )
   617→        if repair_error:
   618→            return _failure_result(
   619→                stage=stage,
   620→                elapsed=elapsed,
   621→                error=repair_error,
   622→                append_run_log=context.append_run_log,
   623→                printed_message=f"  Stage {stage}: repair failed ({repair_error}).",
   624→            )
   625→        if not report:
   626→            return _failure_result(
   627→                stage=stage,
   628→                elapsed=elapsed,
   629→                error="reflect_repair_no_report",
   630→                append_run_log=context.append_run_log,
   631→            )
   632→
   633→    handler.record_report(report, context.args, context.services)
   634→    plan_after_record = context.services.load_plan()
   635→    if not stage_report_recorded(plan_after_record, stage):
   636→        return _failure_result(
   637→            stage=stage,
   638→            elapsed=elapsed,
   639→            error="stage_not_recorded",
   640→            append_run_log=context.append_run_log,
   641→            log_event="stage-record-failed",
   642→            printed_message=f"  Stage {stage}: handler completed but did not persist the stage.",
   643→        )
   644→    context.append_run_log(
   645→        f"stage-recorded stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s mode=orchestrator"
   646→    )
   647→    return StageExecutionResult(status="ready", payload={})
   648→
   649→
   650→def _run_stage_subprocess_path(
   651→    *,
   652→    context: StageRunContext,
   653→    stage: str,
   654→    handler: StageHandler | None,
   655→    prompt_mode: PromptMode,
   656→    dependencies: StageExecutionDependencies,
   657→) -> StageExecutionResult:
   658→    prompt, stages_data = _build_subprocess_prompt(
   659→        context=context,
   660→        stage=stage,
   661→        prompt_mode=prompt_mode,
   662→        dependencies=dependencies,
   663→    )
   664→    subprocess_result = _run_subprocess_stage(
   665→        context=context,
   666→        stage=stage,
   667→        prompt=prompt,
   668→        dependencies=dependencies,
   669→    )
   670→    if subprocess_result.status != "ready":
   671→        return subprocess_result
   672→
   673→    subprocess_artifacts = _resolve_subprocess_artifacts(subprocess_result)
   674→    if subprocess_artifacts is None:
   675→        return _failure_result(
   676→            stage=stage,
   677→            elapsed=None,
   678→            error="subprocess_output_missing",
   679→        )
   680→    output_file, elapsed = subprocess_artifacts
   681→
   682→    return _record_stage_report_if_needed(
   683→        context=context,
   684→        stage=stage,
   685→        handler=handler,
   686→        dependencies=dependencies,
   687→        output_file=output_file,
   688→        elapsed=elapsed,
   689→        stages_data=stages_data,
   690→    )
   691→
   692→
   693→def execute_stage(
   694→    context: StageRunContext,
   695→    *,
   696→    handlers: Mapping[str, StageHandler],
   697→    dependencies: StageExecutionDependencies,
   698→) -> StageExecutionResult:
   699→    """Execute one stage and return an explicit stage outcome."""
   700→    stage = context.stage
   701→    handler = handlers.get(stage)
   702→    prompt_mode = handler.prompt_mode if handler is not None else "output_only"
   703→
   704→    preflight_ok, preflight_reason = preflight_stage(
   705→        stage=stage,
   706→        prompt_mode=prompt_mode,
   707→        plan=context.plan,
   708→        triage_input=context.triage_input,
   709→        dry_run=context.dry_run,
   710→        append_run_log=context.append_run_log,
   711→        validate_reflect_issue_accounting=dependencies.validate_reflect_issue_accounting,
   712→    )
   713→    if not preflight_ok:
   714→        elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - context.stage_start)
   715→        return _failure_result(
   716→            stage=stage,
   717→            elapsed=elapsed,
   718→            error=preflight_reason or "stage_preflight_failed",
   719→            printed_message=f"  Stage {stage}: blocked before launch ({preflight_reason}).",
   720→        )
   721→
   722→    parallel_result = _execute_parallel_stage(
   723→        context=context,
   724→        stage=stage,
   725→        handler=handler,
   726→    )
   727→    if parallel_result.status != "ready":
   728→        return parallel_result
   729→    if parallel_result.used_parallel:
   730→        return StageExecutionResult(status="ready", payload={})
   731→    return _run_stage_subprocess_path(
   732→        context=context,
   733→        stage=stage,
   734→        handler=handler,
   735→        prompt_mode=prompt_mode,
   736→        dependencies=dependencies,
   737→    )
   738→
   739→
   740→__all__ = [
   741→    "DEFAULT_STAGE_HANDLERS",
   742→    "StageExecutionResult",
   743→    "StageExecutionDependencies",
   744→    "StageHandler",
   745→    "build_reflect_repair_prompt",
   746→    "default_stage_execution_dependencies",
   747→    "execute_stage",
   748→    "preflight_stage",
   749→    "read_stage_output",
   750→    "repair_reflect_report_if_needed",
   751→    "stage_report_recorded",
   752→]
   753→
```

    20→    EnrichQualityReport as _ConfirmationCheckReport,
    21→    evaluate_enrich_quality,
    22→)
    23→
    24→
    25→def _print_confirmation_failure(
    26→    *,
    27→    issue: _ConfirmationCheckIssue | None,
    28→    header: str,
    29→    row_printer,
    30→    hints: tuple[str, ...] = (),
    31→) -> bool:
    32→    if issue is None:
    33→        return False
    34→    print(colorize(header.format(total=issue.total), "red"))
    35→    row_printer(issue)
    36→    for hint in hints:
    37→        print(colorize(hint, "dim"))
    38→    return True
    39→
    40→
    41→def _collect_enrich_level_confirmation_checks(
    42→    plan: dict,
    43→    *,
    44→    include_stale_issue_ref_warning: bool,
    45→) -> _ConfirmationCheckReport:
    46→    from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    47→
    48→    return evaluate_enrich_quality(
    49→        plan,
    50→        get_project_root(),
    51→        phase_label="sense-check" if not include_stale_issue_ref_warning else "enrich",
    52→        bad_paths_severity="failure",
    53→        missing_effort_severity="failure",
    54→        include_missing_issue_refs=True,
    55→        include_vague_detail=True,
    56→        stale_issue_refs_severity="warning" if include_stale_issue_ref_warning else None,
    57→    )
    58→
    59→
    60→def _print_underspecified_rows(issue: _ConfirmationCheckIssue) -> None:
    61→    for name, bare, total in issue.rows[:5]:
    62→        print(colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps", "yellow"))
    63→    print()
    64→
    65→
    66→def _print_bad_path_rows(issue: _ConfirmationCheckIssue) -> None:
    67→    for name, step_num, paths in issue.rows:
    68→        for path_str in paths:
    69→            print(colorize(f"    {name} step {step_num}: {path_str}", "yellow"))
    70→
    71→
    72→def _print_missing_ratio_rows(issue: _ConfirmationCheckIssue, *, suffix: str) -> None:
    73→    for name, missing, total in issue.rows[:5]:
    74→        print(colorize(f"    {name}: {missing}/{total} steps {suffix}", "yellow"))
    75→
    76→
    77→def _print_vague_detail_rows(issue: _ConfirmationCheckIssue) -> None:
    78→    for name, step_num, title in issue.rows[:5]:
    79→        print(colorize(f"    {name} step {step_num}: {title}", "yellow"))
    80→
    81→
    82→def _handle_enrich_failures(checks: _ConfirmationCheckReport) -> bool:
    83→    if _print_confirmation_failure(
    84→        issue=checks.failure("underspecified"),
    85→        header="\n  Cannot confirm: {total} step(s) still lack detail or issue_refs.",
    86→        row_printer=_print_underspecified_rows,
    87→        hints=(
    88→            "  Every step needs --detail (sub-points) or --issue-refs (for auto-completion).",
    89→            '  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"',
    90→        ),
    91→    ):
    92→        return True
    93→    print(colorize("  All steps have detail or issue_refs.", "green"))
    94→    if _print_confirmation_failure(
    95→        issue=checks.failure("bad_paths"),
    96→        header="\n  Cannot confirm: {total} file path(s) in step details don't exist on disk.",
    97→        row_printer=_print_bad_path_rows,
    98→        hints=("  Fix paths with: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail '...'",),
    99→    ):
   100→        return True
   101→    if _print_confirmation_failure(
   102→        issue=checks.failure("missing_effort"),
   103→        header="\n  Cannot confirm: {total} step(s) have no effort tag.",
   104→        row_printer=lambda issue: _print_missing_ratio_rows(issue, suffix="missing effort"),
   105→        hints=(
   106→            "  Every step needs --effort (trivial/small/medium/large).",
   107→            "  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --effort small",
   108→        ),
   109→    ):
   110→        return True
   111→    if _print_confirmation_failure(
   112→        issue=checks.failure("missing_issue_refs"),
   113→        header="\n  Cannot confirm: {total} step(s) have no issue_refs.",
   114→        row_printer=lambda issue: _print_missing_ratio_rows(issue, suffix="missing refs"),
   115→        hints=(
   116→            "  Every step needs --issue-refs linking it to the review issue(s) it addresses.",
   117→            "  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --issue-refs <hash1> <hash2>",
   118→        ),
   119→    ):
   120→        return True
   121→    return _print_confirmation_failure(
   122→        issue=checks.failure("vague_detail"),
   123→        header="\n  Cannot confirm: {total} step(s) have vague detail (< 80 chars, no file paths).",
   124→        row_printer=_print_vague_detail_rows,
   125→        hints=(
   126→            "  Executor-ready means: someone with zero context knows which file to open and what to change.",
   127→            "  Add file paths and specific instructions to each step's --detail.",
   128→        ),
   129→    )
   130→
   131→
   132→def _print_stale_ref_warning(issue: _ConfirmationCheckIssue | None) -> None:
   133→    if issue is None:
   134→        return
   135→    print(colorize(f"\n  Warning: {issue.total} step issue_ref(s) point to skipped/wontfixed issues.", "yellow"))
   136→    for name, step_num, ids in issue.rows[:5]:
   137→        print(colorize(f"    {name} step {step_num}: {', '.join(ids[:3])}", "yellow"))
   138→    print(colorize("  Consider removing stale refs or removing the step if it's no longer needed.", "dim"))
   139→
   140→
   141→def _handle_sense_check_failures(checks: _ConfirmationCheckReport) -> bool:
   142→    failure_messages = {
   143→        "underspecified": "still lack detail or issue_refs.",
   144→        "bad_paths": "file path(s) in step details don't exist on disk.",
   145→        "missing_effort": "step(s) have no effort tag.",
   146→        "missing_issue_refs": "step(s) have no issue_refs.",
   147→        "vague_detail": "step(s) have vague detail.",
   148→    }
   149→    for code, suffix in failure_messages.items():
   150→        issue = checks.failure(code)
   151→        if issue is None:
   152→            continue
   153→        print(colorize(f"\n  Cannot confirm: {issue.total} {suffix}", "red"))
   154→        if code == "underspecified":
   155→            _print_underspecified_rows(issue)
   156→        elif code == "bad_paths":
   157→            _print_bad_path_rows(issue)
   158→        return True
   159→    return False
   160→
   161→
   162→def confirm_enrich(
   163→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   164→    plan: dict,
   165→    stages: dict,
   166→    attestation: str | None,
   167→    *,
   168→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   169→) -> None:
   170→    """Show enrich summary and record confirmation if attestation is valid."""
   171→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   172→    if not ensure_stage_is_confirmable(stages, stage="enrich"):
   173→        return
   174→
   175→    checks = _collect_enrich_level_confirmation_checks(
   176→        plan,
   177→        include_stale_issue_ref_warning=True,
   178→    )
   179→
   180→    print(colorize("  Stage: ENRICH — Make steps executor-ready (detail, refs)", "bold"))
   181→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 54, "dim"))
   182→
   183→    if _handle_enrich_failures(checks):
   184→        return
   185→
   186→    _print_stale_ref_warning(checks.warning("stale_issue_refs"))
   187→
   188→    enrich_clusters = [n for n in plan.get("clusters", {}) if not plan["clusters"][n].get("auto")]
   189→
   190→    if not finalize_stage_confirmation(
   191→        plan=plan,
   192→        stages=stages,
   193→        request=StageConfirmationRequest(
   194→            stage="enrich",
   195→            attestation=attestation,
   196→            min_attestation_len=MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN,
   197→            command_hint='desloppify plan triage --confirm enrich --attestation "Steps are executor-ready..."',
   198→            validation_stage="enrich",
   199→            validate_attestation_fn=validate_attestation,
   200→            validation_kwargs={"cluster_names": enrich_clusters},
   201→            log_action="triage_confirm_enrich",
   202→        ),
   203→        services=resolved_services,
   204→    ):
   205→        return
   206→    print_user_message(
   207→        "Hey — enrich is confirmed. Run `desloppify plan triage"
   208→        " --stage sense-check --report \"...\"` to verify step"
   209→        " accuracy and cross-cluster dependencies."
   210→    )
   211→
   212→
   213→def confirm_sense_check(
   214→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   215→    plan: dict,
   216→    stages: dict,
   217→    attestation: str | None,
   218→    *,
   219→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   220→) -> None:
   221→    """Show sense-check summary and record confirmation if attestation is valid."""
   222→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   223→    if not ensure_stage_is_confirmable(stages, stage="sense-check"):
   224→        return
   225→
   226→    checks = _collect_enrich_level_confirmation_checks(
   227→        plan,
   228→        include_stale_issue_ref_warning=False,
   229→    )
   230→
   231→    print(colorize("  Stage: SENSE-CHECK — Verify accuracy & cross-cluster deps", "bold"))
   232→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 57, "dim"))
   233→
   234→    if _handle_sense_check_failures(checks):
   235→        return
   236→
   237→    print(colorize("  All enrich-level checks pass.", "green"))
   238→
   239→    sense_check_clusters = [n for n in plan.get("clusters", {}) if not plan["clusters"][n].get("auto")]
   240→
   241→    if not finalize_stage_confirmation(
   242→        plan=plan,
   243→        stages=stages,
   244→        request=StageConfirmationRequest(
   245→            stage="sense-check",
   246→            attestation=attestation,
   247→            min_attestation_len=MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN,
   248→            command_hint='desloppify plan triage --confirm sense-check --attestation "Content and structure verified..."',
   249→            validation_stage="sense-check",
   250→            validate_attestation_fn=validate_attestation,
   251→            validation_kwargs={"cluster_names": sense_check_clusters},
   252→            log_action="triage_confirm_sense_check",
   253→        ),
   254→        services=resolved_services,
   255→    ):
   256→        return
   257→    print_user_message(
   258→        "Hey — sense-check is confirmed. Run `desloppify plan triage"
   259→        " --stage value-check --report \"...\"` for the final keep/skip pass."
   260→    )
   261→
   262→
   263→def confirm_value_check(
   264→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   265→    plan: dict,
   266→    stages: dict,
   267→    attestation: str | None,
   268→    *,
   269→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   270→) -> None:
   271→    """Show value-check summary and record confirmation if attestation is valid."""
   272→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   273→    if not ensure_stage_is_confirmable(stages, stage="value-check"):
   274→        return
   275→
   276→    print(colorize("  Stage: VALUE-CHECK — Decide item-by-item keep/skip value", "bold"))
   277→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 62, "dim"))
   278→
   279→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   280→    targets = value_check_targets(plan, runtime.state)
   281→    if targets:
   282→        print(colorize(f"  Reviewed {len(targets)} live queue target(s).", "green"))
   283→    else:
   284→        print(colorize("  No live queue targets remained in this value-check batch.", "green"))
   285→
   286→    cluster_names = [n for n in plan.get("clusters", {}) if not plan["clusters"][n].get("auto")]
   287→    if not finalize_stage_confirmation(
   288→        plan=plan,
   289→        stages=stages,
   290→        request=StageConfirmationRequest(
   291→            stage="value-check",
   292→            attestation=attestation,
   293→            min_attestation_len=MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN,
   294→            command_hint='desloppify plan triage --confirm value-check --attestation "I reviewed each queued item for value and elegance..."',
   295→            validation_stage="value-check",
   296→            validate_attestation_fn=validate_attestation,
   297→            validation_kwargs={"cluster_names": cluster_names},
   298→            log_action="triage_confirm_value_check",
   299→        ),
   300→        services=resolved_services,
   301→    ):
   302→        return
   303→    print_user_message(

1→"""Canonical confirmation-stage router for triage."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from .basic import (
     8→    MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN,
     9→    confirm_observe,
    10→    confirm_reflect,
    11→    validate_attestation,
    12→)
    13→from .enrich import confirm_enrich, confirm_sense_check, confirm_value_check
    14→from .organize import confirm_organize
    15→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    16→
    17→
    18→def cmd_confirm_stage(
    19→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    20→    *,
    21→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
    22→) -> None:
    23→    """Route ``--confirm <stage>`` to the stage-specific confirmation handler."""
    24→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
    25→    confirm_stage = getattr(args, "confirm", None)
    26→    attestation = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
    27→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
    28→    stages = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_stages", {})
    29→
    30→    handlers = {
    31→        "observe": confirm_observe,
    32→        "reflect": confirm_reflect,
    33→        "organize": confirm_organize,
    34→        "enrich": confirm_enrich,
    35→        "sense-check": confirm_sense_check,
    36→        "value-check": confirm_value_check,
    37→    }
    38→    handler = handlers.get(confirm_stage)
    39→    if handler is None:
    40→        return
    41→    handler(args, plan, stages, attestation, services=resolved_services)
    42→
    43→
    44→__all__ = ["MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN", "cmd_confirm_stage", "validate_attestation"]
    45→

1→"""Shared helpers for triage stage confirmation flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     9→from desloppify.state_io import utc_now
    10→
    11→from ..stage_queue import purge_triage_stage
    12→
    13→_STAGE_LABELS = {
    14→    "observe": "Observe",
    15→    "reflect": "Reflect",
    16→    "organize": "Organize",
    17→    "enrich": "Enrich",
    18→    "sense-check": "Sense-check",
    19→    "value-check": "Value-check",
    20→}
    21→
    22→
    23→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    24→class StageConfirmationRequest:
    25→    """Input contract for shared stage-confirmation behavior."""
    26→
    27→    stage: str
    28→    attestation: str | None
    29→    min_attestation_len: int
    30→    command_hint: str
    31→    validation_stage: str
    32→    validate_attestation_fn: Callable[..., str | None]
    33→    log_action: str
    34→    validation_kwargs: dict[str, object] | None = None
    35→    log_detail: dict[str, object] | None = None
    36→    not_satisfied_hint: str | None = None
    37→
    38→
    39→def ensure_stage_is_confirmable(stages: dict, *, stage: str) -> bool:
    40→    """Validate stage presence/confirmation status before confirm flow runs."""
    41→    if stage not in stages:
    42→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot confirm: {stage} stage not recorded.", "red"))
    43→        print(colorize(f'  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage {stage} --report "..."', "dim"))
    44→        return False
    45→    if stages[stage].get("confirmed_at"):
    46→        label = _STAGE_LABELS.get(stage, stage.title())
    47→        print(colorize(f"  {label} stage already confirmed.", "green"))
    48→        return False
    49→    return True
    50→
    51→
    52→def finalize_stage_confirmation(
    53→    *,
    54→    plan: dict,
    55→    stages: dict,
    56→    request: StageConfirmationRequest,
    57→    services,
    58→) -> bool:
    59→    """Apply shared attestation validation + state mutation + log/save flow."""
    60→    attestation_text = (request.attestation or "").strip()
    61→    if len(attestation_text) < request.min_attestation_len:
    62→        if attestation_text:
    63→            print(
    64→                colorize(
    65→                    (
    66→                        "\n  Attestation too short "
    67→                        f"({len(attestation_text)} chars, min {request.min_attestation_len})."
    68→                    ),
    69→                    "red",
    70→                )
    71→            )
    72→        print(colorize("\n  If satisfied, confirm:", "dim"))
    73→        print(colorize(f"    {request.command_hint}", "dim"))
    74→        if request.not_satisfied_hint:
    75→            print(colorize(f"  {request.not_satisfied_hint}", "dim"))
    76→        return False
    77→
    78→    err = request.validate_attestation_fn(
    79→        attestation_text,
    80→        request.validation_stage,
    81→        **(request.validation_kwargs or {}),
    82→    )
    83→    if err:
    84→        print(colorize(f"\n  {err}", "red"))
    85→        return False
    86→
    87→    stages[request.stage]["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
    88→    stages[request.stage]["confirmed_text"] = attestation_text
    89→    purge_triage_stage(plan, request.stage)
    90→
    91→    detail = {"attestation": attestation_text}
    92→    if request.log_detail:
    93→        detail.update(request.log_detail)
    94→    services.append_log_entry(
    95→        plan,
    96→        request.log_action,
    97→        actor="user",
    98→        detail=detail,
    99→    )
   100→    services.save_plan(plan)
   101→    label = _STAGE_LABELS.get(request.stage, request.stage.title())
   102→    print(colorize(f'  ✓ {label} confirmed: "{attestation_text}"', "green"))
   103→    return True
   104→
   105→
   106→__all__ = [
   107→    "StageConfirmationRequest",
   108→    "ensure_stage_is_confirmable",
   109→    "finalize_stage_confirmation",
   110→]
   111→

    34→def _required_issue_hashes(triage_input: TriageInput) -> list[str]:
    35→    """Return sorted short hashes for open review issues."""
    36→    review_issues = getattr(triage_input, "review_issues", getattr(triage_input, "open_issues", {}))
    37→    return sorted(issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1] for issue_id in review_issues)
    38→
    39→
    40→def _compact_issue_summary(triage_input: TriageInput) -> str:
    41→    """Return a compact issue summary for later triage stages."""
    42→    review_issues = getattr(triage_input, "review_issues", getattr(triage_input, "open_issues", {}))
    43→    by_dim: Counter[str] = Counter()
    44→    for issue in review_issues.values():
    45→        detail = issue.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(issue.get("detail"), dict) else {}
    46→        by_dim[str(detail.get("dimension", "unknown"))] += 1
    47→    dims = ", ".join(f"{name} ({count})" for name, count in sorted(by_dim.items()))
    48→    parts = [
    49→        "## Issue Summary",
    50→        f"Open review issues: {len(review_issues)}",
    51→    ]
    52→    if dims:
    53→        parts.append(f"Open dimensions: {dims}")
    54→    if triage_input.new_since_last:
    55→        parts.append(f"New since last triage: {len(triage_input.new_since_last)}")
    56→    if triage_input.resolved_since_last:
    57→        parts.append(f"Resolved since last triage: {len(triage_input.resolved_since_last)}")
    58→
    59→    # Cluster summary if clusters exist
    60→    existing = triage_input.existing_clusters or {}

    86→
    87→def _issue_context_for_stage(
    88→    stage: str,
    89→    triage_input: TriageInput,
    90→    mode: PromptMode = "self_record",
    91→) -> str:
    92→    """Return the amount of issue context appropriate for a stage."""
    93→    if stage in {"observe", "reflect"}:
    94→        parts = ["## Issue Data\n\n" + build_triage_prompt(triage_input)]
    95→        if stage == "reflect":
    96→            short_ids = _required_issue_hashes(triage_input)
    97→            parts.append(
    98→                "## Required Issue Hashes\n"
    99→                f"Total open review issues: {len(short_ids)}\n"
   100→                "Every one of these hashes must appear exactly once in your cluster/skip blueprint.\n"
   101→                "Do not repeat hashes outside that blueprint.\n"
   102→                + ", ".join(short_ids)
   103→            )
   104→            parts.append(
   105→                "## Coverage Ledger Template\n"
   106→                "Your final report MUST contain a `## Coverage Ledger` section with one line per issue.\n"
   107→                "Allowed forms:\n"
   108→                '- `- abcd1234 -> cluster "cluster-name"`\n'
   109→                '- `- abcd1234 -> skip "specific-reason-tag"`\n'
   110→                "Do not mention hashes outside the `## Coverage Ledger` section.\n"
   111→                + "\n".join(f"- {short_id} -> TODO" for short_id in short_ids)
   112→            )
   113→        return "\n\n".join(parts)
   114→    summary = _compact_issue_summary(triage_input)
   115→    if mode == "self_record":
   116→        summary += _INVESTIGATION_COMMANDS
   117→    return summary
   118→
   119→
   120→def _format_assessments_table(assessments: list[dict]) -> str:
   121→    """Format structured observe assessments as a readable table for downstream stages."""
   122→    if not assessments:
   123→        return ""
   124→    lines = ["### Structured Observe Assessments\n"]
   125→    lines.append("| Hash | Verdict | Recommendation |")
   126→    lines.append("|------|---------|----------------|")
   127→    for a in assessments:
   128→        h = a.get("hash", "?")
   129→        v = a.get("verdict", "?")
   130→        r = a.get("recommendation", "")
   131→        # Truncate recommendation for table readability
   132→        if len(r) > 80:
   133→            r = r[:77] + "..."
   134→        lines.append(f"| {h} | {v} | {r} |")
   135→    return "\n".join(lines)
   136→
   137→
   138→def _format_disposition_table(
   139→    dispositions: dict[str, dict],
   140→    assessments: list[dict],
   141→) -> str:
   142→    """Format the unified disposition map for downstream stages.
   143→
   144→    Partitions issues into:
   145→    - Auto-skipped by observe (false-positive/exaggerated)
   146→    - Genuine issues requiring disposition
   147→    - Actionability-flagged issues (over-engineering/not-worth-it)
   148→    """
   149→    if not dispositions and not assessments:
   150→        return ""
   151→
   152→    auto_skipped: list[dict] = []
   153→    genuine: list[dict] = []
   154→    actionability: list[dict] = []
   155→
   156→    # Build from assessments for hash display, enriched by dispositions
   157→    for a in assessments:
   158→        h = a.get("hash", "?")
   159→        v = a.get("verdict", "?")
   160→        r = a.get("recommendation", "")
   161→        if len(r) > 80:
   162→            r = r[:77] + "..."
   163→        entry = {"hash": h, "verdict": v, "recommendation": r}
   164→
   165→        if v in ("false positive", "exaggerated"):
   166→            auto_skipped.append(entry)
   167→        elif v in ("over engineering", "not worth it"):
   168→            actionability.append(entry)
   169→        else:
   170→            genuine.append(entry)
   171→
   172→    lines = ["### Issue Disposition Summary\n"]
   173→
   174→    if auto_skipped:
   175→        lines.append(f"**Auto-skipped by observe ({len(auto_skipped)} issues)** — no action needed:\n")
   176→        lines.append("| Hash | Verdict | Recommendation |")
   177→        lines.append("|------|---------|----------------|")
   178→        for e in auto_skipped:
   179→            lines.append(f"| {e['hash']} | {e['verdict']} | {e['recommendation']} |")
   180→        lines.append("")
   181→
   182→    if genuine:
   183→        lines.append(f"**Genuine issues requiring disposition ({len(genuine)} issues):**\n")
   184→        lines.append("| Hash | Verdict | Recommendation |")
   185→        lines.append("|------|---------|----------------|")
   186→        for e in genuine:
   187→            lines.append(f"| {e['hash']} | {e['verdict']} | {e['recommendation']} |")
   188→        lines.append("")
   189→
   190→    if actionability:
   191→        lines.append(
   192→            f"**Actionability-flagged issues ({len(actionability)} issues)** "
   193→            "— observe thinks these aren't worth it, reflect decides:\n"
   194→        )
   195→        lines.append("| Hash | Verdict | Recommendation |")
   196→        lines.append("|------|---------|----------------|")
   197→        for e in actionability:
   198→            lines.append(f"| {e['hash']} | {e['verdict']} | {e['recommendation']} |")
   199→        lines.append("")
   200→
   201→    return "\n".join(lines)
   202→
   203→
   204→def _format_assessment_file_evidence(assessments: list[dict]) -> str:
   205→    """Format file-level observe evidence for clustering-heavy downstream stages."""
   206→    if not assessments:
   207→        return ""
   208→    lines = ["### Observe File Evidence\n"]
   209→    for assessment in assessments:
   210→        issue_hash = str(assessment.get("hash", "?"))
   211→        files_read = [
   212→            str(path).strip()
   213→            for path in assessment.get("files_read", [])
   214→            if str(path).strip()
   215→        ]
   216→        verdict = str(assessment.get("verdict", "?")).strip() or "?"
   217→        reasoning = str(assessment.get("verdict_reasoning", "")).strip()
   218→        recommendation = str(assessment.get("recommendation", "")).strip()
   219→        lines.append(f"- {issue_hash}: {verdict}")
   220→        if files_read:
   221→            lines.append(f"  files_read: {', '.join(files_read)}")
   222→        if reasoning:
   223→            lines.append(f"  verdict_reasoning: {reasoning}")
   224→        if recommendation:
   225→            lines.append(f"  recommendation: {recommendation}")
   226→    return "\n".join(lines)
   227→
   228→
   229→def _relevant_prior_reports(
   230→    stage: str,
   231→    prior_reports: dict[str, str],
   232→    stages_data: dict | None = None,
   233→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   234→    """Return the stage reports that matter for the current stage."""
   235→    wanted = {
   236→        "reflect": ("observe",),
   237→        "organize": ("reflect",),
   238→        "enrich": ("organize",),
   239→        "sense-check": ("organize", "enrich"),
   240→        "value-check": ("organize", "enrich", "sense-check"),
   241→    }.get(stage, tuple(prior_reports))
   242→    result = [(name, prior_reports[name]) for name in wanted if name in prior_reports]
   243→
   244→    # Append structured observe assessments / disposition table for downstream stages
   245→    if stage in {"reflect", "organize", "sense-check", "value-check"} and stages_data:
   246→        observe_data = stages_data.get("observe", {})
   247→        assessments = observe_data.get("assessments", [])
   248→
   249→        # Use disposition table when dispositions exist, fall back to assessments table.
   250→        # Note: stages_data is the triage_stages dict; dispositions are on
   251→        # epic_triage_meta which we don't have here. Use assessments to build
   252→        # the partitioned view since the verdict info is the same.
   253→        if assessments:
   254→            disp_table = _format_disposition_table({}, assessments)
   255→            if disp_table:
   256→                result.append(("observe-dispositions", disp_table))
   257→            else:
   258→                table = _format_assessments_table(assessments)
   259→                if table:
   260→                    result.append(("observe-assessments", table))
   261→        if stage == "organize":
   262→            evidence = _format_assessment_file_evidence(assessments)
   263→            if evidence:
   264→                result.append(("observe-evidence", evidence))
   265→
   266→    return result
   267→
   268→
   269→def build_stage_prompt(
   270→    stage: str,
   271→    triage_input: TriageInput,
   272→    prior_reports: dict[str, str],
   273→    *,
   274→    repo_root: Path,
   275→    mode: PromptMode = "self_record",
   276→    cli_command: str = "desloppify",
   277→    stages_data: dict | None = None,
   278→) -> str:
   279→    """Build a complete subagent prompt for a triage stage."""
   280→    parts: list[str] = []
   281→
   282→    # Preamble
   283→    parts.append(
   284→        triage_prompt_preamble(mode).format(
   285→            stage=stage.upper(),
   286→            repo_root=repo_root,
   287→            cli_command=cli_command,
   288→        )
   289→    )
   290→
   291→    # Prior stage reports
   292→    relevant_prior_reports = _relevant_prior_reports(stage, prior_reports, stages_data)
   293→    if relevant_prior_reports:
   294→        parts.append("## Prior Stage Reports\n")
   295→        for prior_stage, report in relevant_prior_reports:
   296→            parts.append(f"### {prior_stage.upper()} Report\n{report}\n")
   297→
   298→    # Issue data / summary
   299→    parts.append(_issue_context_for_stage(stage, triage_input, mode))
   300→
   301→    # Stage-specific instructions
   302→    instruction_fn = _STAGE_INSTRUCTIONS.get(stage)
   303→    if instruction_fn:
   304→        parts.append(instruction_fn(mode))
   305→
   306→    # CLI reference
   307→    if mode == "self_record":
   308→        parts.append(render_cli_reference(cli_command))
   309→
   310→    # Validation requirements
   311→    parts.append(_validation_requirements(stage))
   312→
   313→    return "\n\n".join(parts)
   314→
   315→
   316→def cmd_stage_prompt(
   317→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   318→    *,
   319→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   320→) -> None:
   321→    """Print the current prompt for a triage stage, built from live plan data."""
   322→    stage = args.stage_prompt
   323→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   324→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   325→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   326→    state = runtime.state
   327→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   328→    repo_root = get_project_root()
   329→
   330→    # Extract real prior reports from plan.json
   331→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   332→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   333→    prior_reports: dict[str, str] = {}
   334→    for prior_stage in _STAGES:
   335→        if prior_stage == stage:
   336→            break
   337→        report = stages.get(prior_stage, {}).get("report", "")
   338→        if report:
   339→            prior_reports[prior_stage] = report
   340→
   341→    prompt = build_stage_prompt(stage, si, prior_reports, repo_root=repo_root, stages_data=stages)
   342→    print(prompt)
   343→
   344→
   345→__all__ = [
   346→    "build_observe_batch_prompt",
   347→    "build_sense_check_content_prompt",
   348→    "build_sense_check_structure_prompt",
   349→    "build_stage_prompt",
   350→    "cmd_stage_prompt",
   351→    "_observe_batch_instructions",
   352→    "_validation_requirements",
   353→]
   354→

    36→        "   asking a single question? If not:\n"

    42→        "7. DUPLICATES: If you notice this step does the same thing as a step in another\n"

   102→def _format_cluster_step(index: int, step: object) -> str:
   103→    title = step.get("title", str(step)) if isinstance(step, dict) else str(step)
   104→    detail = step.get("detail", "") if isinstance(step, dict) else ""
   105→    effort = step.get("effort", "") if isinstance(step, dict) else ""
   106→    refs = step.get("issue_refs", []) if isinstance(step, dict) else []
   107→    line = f"{index}. **{title}**"
   108→    if effort:
   109→        line += f" [{effort}]"
   110→    if refs:
   111→        line += f" (refs: {', '.join(refs[:3])})"
   112→    if detail:
   113→        line += f"\n   {detail[:300]}"
   114→    return line
   115→

   122→        )

   125→        "Write a plain-text report of your findings. The orchestrator records the stage."
   126→    )

   161→def _format_structure_cluster(name: str, cluster: dict) -> list[str]:

   168→    lines = [header]
   169→    for i, step in enumerate(steps, 1):

   171→        detail = step.get("detail", "") if isinstance(step, dict) else ""
   172→        line = f"  {i}. {title}"
   173→        if detail:
   174→            line += f"\n     {detail[:200]}"
   175→        lines.append(line)
   176→    return lines

   191→def build_sense_check_content_prompt(
   192→    *,
   193→    cluster_name: str,

   197→    mode: str = "output_only",

   199→) -> str:

   201→    cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name, {})

   203→    issue_ids = cluster_issue_ids(cluster)

   209→        f"Repo root: {repo_root}"
   210→    )
   211→    parts.append(_sense_check_job_block(mode=mode))
   212→    parts.append(_sense_check_fix_list())
   213→
   214→    if policy_block:

   216→
   217→    parts.append(

   258→    parts.append(
   259→        "## What to check and fix\n"
   260→        "1. SHARED FILES: If cluster A and cluster B both have steps touching the same file,\n"
   261→        "   and neither depends on the other → report which dependency edge should be added.\n"
   262→        "2. MISSING CASCADE: If a step renames/removes a function or export, check whether\n"
   263→        "   any other file imports it. If those importers aren't covered by any step in any\n"
   264→        "   cluster → report the cascade step that should be added.\n"
   265→        "   Include the cluster name, affected importers, and issue hash in your report.\n"
   266→        "3. CIRCULAR DEPS: If adding a dependency would create a cycle, flag it in your report\n"
   267→        "   instead of adding it.\n"
   268→    )
   269→
   270→    structure_apply = _sense_check_structure_apply_block(
   271→        mode=mode,
   272→        cli_command=cli_command,
   273→    )
   274→    if structure_apply:
   275→        parts.append(structure_apply)
   276→    parts.append(_sense_check_structure_not_to_do(mode))
   277→
   278→    # Include all clusters with their steps and dependencies
   279→    parts.append("## Clusters\n")
   280→    for name, c in sorted(clusters.items()):
   281→        if c.get("auto"):
   282→            continue
   283→        parts.extend(_format_structure_cluster(name, c))
   284→
   285→    parts.append(_sense_check_structure_output_block(mode))
   286→
   287→    return "\n\n".join(parts)
   288→
   289→
   290→__all__ = [
   291→    "build_sense_check_content_prompt",
   292→    "build_sense_check_structure_prompt",
   293→]
   294→
```
