   102→    return f"""\
   103→## REFLECT Stage Instructions
   104→
   105→Your task: using the verdicts from observe, design the cluster structure.
   106→
   107→**A strategy is NOT a restatement of observe.** Observe says "here's what I found." Reflect
   108→says "here's what we should DO about it, and here's what we should NOT do, and here's WHY."
   109→
   110→**The Structured Observe Assessments table (provided below) is your primary input.** It contains
   111→a per-issue verdict (genuine/false-positive/exaggerated/over-engineering/not-worth-it) with reasoning. Use
   112→these verdicts as authoritative — do not second-guess observe unless you have specific evidence.
   113→
   114→**Important: Issues with verdict `false-positive` or `exaggerated` have already been auto-skipped
   115→by observe confirmation.** They are NOT in your issue set and do NOT need ledger entries.
   116→
   117→For issues with verdict `over-engineering` or `not-worth-it`: observe flagged these as
   118→questionable, but YOU decide. If you agree they're not worth fixing, skip them. If you disagree
   119→and think the fix has value, cluster them. These are judgment calls, not factual determinations.
   120→
   121→### What you must do:
   122→
   123→1. **Filter:** which issues are genuine (from the observe assessments table)?
   124→2. **Map:** for each genuine issue, what file/directory does it touch?
   125→3. **Group:** which issues share files or directories? These become clusters.
   126→4. **Skip:** which issues should be skipped? Apply YAGNI: if the fix is more complex than the
   127→   problem, skip it. Valid skip reasons:
   128→   - "the fix would add a 50-line abstraction to save 3 lines of duplication"
   129→   - "the current code is clear and simple despite being theoretically suboptimal"
   130→   - "observe verdict: not-worth-it — the improvement is marginal"
   131→   - "fixing this requires touching 8 files for a naming consistency issue nobody notices"
   132→   Invalid: "low priority" (everything is low priority compared to something)
   133→5. **Order:** which clusters depend on others? What's the execution sequence?
   134→6. **Check recurring patterns** — compare current issues against resolved history. If the same
   135→   dimension keeps producing issues, that's a root cause that needs addressing, not just
   136→   another round of fixes.
   137→7. **Consider mechanical backlog** — the backlog section shows auto-clusters
   138→   (pre-grouped detector findings) and unclustered items. For each auto-cluster:
   139→   - **promote**: name it in a `## Backlog Promotions` section. Prefer clusters with
   140→     `[autofix: ...]` hints because they are lower-risk.
   141→   - **leave**: say nothing. Silence means it stays in backlog.
   142→   - **supersede**: absorb the underlying work into a review cluster when the same files
   143→     or root cause already belong together.
   144→   For unclustered items: promote individually or group related ones into a manual cluster.
   145→   Mechanical items are NOT part of the Coverage Ledger — that ledger remains review-issues only.
   146→8. **Account for every issue exactly once** — every open issue hash must appear in exactly one
   147→   cluster line or one skip line. Do not drop hashes, and do not repeat a hash in multiple
   148→   clusters or in both a cluster and a skip.
   149→
   150→### Your report MUST include both a coverage ledger and a concrete cluster blueprint
   151→
   152→This blueprint is what the organize stage will execute. Be specific:
   153→```
   154→## Coverage Ledger
   155→- a5996373 -> cluster "travel-structure-contract-unification"
   156→- fb113678 -> skip "false-positive-current-code"
   157→
   158→## Cluster Blueprint
   159→Cluster "media-lightbox-hooks" (all in src/domains/media-lightbox/)
   160→Cluster "task-typing" (both touch src/types/database.ts)
   161→
   162→## Backlog Promotions
   163→- Promote auto/unused-imports (overlaps with the files in cluster "task-typing")
   164→
   165→## Skip Decisions
   166→Skip "false-positive-current-code" (false positive per observe)
   167→```
   168→
   169→### Hard accounting rule
   170→
   171→- Start your report with a `## Coverage Ledger` section.
   172→- In that section, mention each issue hash **once and only once** on its own ledger line.
   173→- Do **not** mention issue hashes again in cluster rationale paragraphs, recurring-pattern notes,
   174→  or ordering explanations. After the ledger, refer to clusters by name.
   175→- Before finishing, do a self-check: the ledger must cover all open issue hashes exactly once.
   176→
   177→### What a LAZY reflect looks like (will be rejected):
   178→- Restating observe findings in slightly different words
   179→- "We should prioritize high-impact items and defer low-priority ones"
   180→- A bulleted list of dimensions without any strategic thinking
   181→- Ignoring recurring patterns
   182→- No `## Coverage Ledger`
   183→- No cluster blueprint (just vague grouping ideas)
   184→- Missing or duplicated issue hashes
   185→
   186→### What a GOOD reflect looks like:
   187→- "50% false positive rate. Of 34 issues, 17 are genuine. 10 of those are batch-scriptable
   188→  convention fixes (zero risk, 30 min) — cluster 'convention-batch'. The remaining 7 split into
   189→  3 clusters by file proximity: 'media-lightbox-hooks' (issues X,Y,Z — all in src/domains/media-lightbox/),
   190→  'timeline-cleanup' (issues A,B,C — touching Timeline components), 'task-typing' (issues D,E).
   191→  Skip: issue W (false positive), issue V (over-engineering), issue U (not-worth-it:
   192→  adds ErrorBoundary wrapper around 3 components that already handle errors inline —
   193→  technically cleaner but doubles the JSX nesting depth for no behavioral change).
   194→  design_coherence recurs (2 resolved, 5 open) but only 1 of the 5 actually warrants work."
   195→
   196→{tail}
   197→"""

   241→        process_block = """\
   242→2. **False-positive and exaggerated issues have already been auto-skipped** by observe confirmation.
   243→   If reflect skipped additional issues (over-engineering/not-worth-it), include those skip decisions.
   244→3. Define the clusters exactly as they should be created.
   245→4. Assign every kept issue to a cluster.

   248→7. Call out cross-cluster dependencies when clusters touch overlapping files.
   249→"""
   250→        tail = """\
   251→When done, write a plain-text organize report that names the clusters, their issue membership,

   253→"""
   254→    return f"""\
   255→## ORGANIZE Stage Instructions
   256→
   257→Your task: execute the cluster blueprint from the reflect stage.
   258→
   259→{intro}
   260→
   261→This stage should be largely mechanical. If you find yourself making major strategic
   262→decisions, something went wrong in reflect — the strategy should already be decided.
   263→
   264→### Process
   265→
   266→1. Review the reflect report's cluster blueprint AND the observe assessments table (both provided below)
   267→{process_block}
   268→
   269→### Quality gates (the confirmation will check these)
   270→
   271→Before recording, verify:
   272→- [ ] Every issue that reflect marked as skip has been skipped (false-positive/exaggerated are already auto-skipped)
   273→- [ ] No cluster where the total fix effort exceeds the problem severity (KISS: prefer
   274→      simple code with a known smell over complex code with a hidden abstraction)
   275→- [ ] Every cluster name describes an area or specific change, not a problem type
   276→- [ ] No cluster has issues from 5+ unrelated directories (theme-group smell)
   277→- [ ] Step count < issue count (consolidation happened)
   278→- [ ] Every skip has a specific per-issue reason (not "low priority")
   279→- [ ] Overlapping clusters have --depends-on set
   280→- [ ] Cluster descriptions describe the WORK, not the PROBLEMS
   281→
   282→Every review issue must end up in a cluster OR be skipped.

   288→def _enrich_instructions(mode: PromptMode = "self_record") -> str:
   289→    subagent_block = """\
   290→**USE SUBAGENTS — one per cluster.** Each subagent MUST:
   291→
   292→1. Run `desloppify plan cluster show <name>` to get steps, members, and reviewer suggestions
   293→2. For deeper analysis, run `desloppify show <issue-id> --no-budget` to see full evidence
   294→3. **Read the actual source file for every step** — not just the issue description.
   295→   The issue says what's wrong; you need to see the code to say what to DO.
   296→4. Write detail that includes: the file path, the specific location (line range or
   297→   function name), and the exact change to make
   298→5. Set effort based on the ACTUAL complexity you see in the code, not a guess
   299→"""
   300→    tail = """\
   301→When done, run:
   302→```
   303→desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "<enrichment summary>" --attestation "<80+ chars mentioning cluster names>"
   304→```
   305→"""
   306→    if mode == "output_only":
   307→        subagent_block = """\
   308→**USE SUBAGENTS — one per cluster.** Each subagent MUST:
   309→
   310→1. Inspect the cluster definition provided in the prompt context
   311→2. **Read the actual source file for every step** — not just the issue description.
   312→   The issue says what's wrong; you need to see the code to say what to DO.
   313→3. Write detail that includes: the file path, the specific location (line range or
   314→   function name), and the exact change to make
   315→4. Set effort based on the ACTUAL complexity you see in the code, not a guess
   316→"""
   317→        tail = """\
   318→When done, write a plain-text enrichment report describing the corrected step details,
   319→effort tags, and issue refs for each cluster. The orchestrator records the stage.
   320→"""
   321→    return f"""\
   322→## ENRICH Stage Instructions
   323→
   324→Your task: make EVERY step executor-ready. The test: could a developer who has never seen
   325→this codebase read your step detail and make the change without asking a single question?
   326→
   327→If the answer is "they'd need to figure out which file" or "they'd need to understand the
   328→context" — your step is not ready. Be specific enough that the work is mechanical.
   329→Use the organize report included above as the authoritative cluster plan; do not re-derive
   330→strategy from old triage runs unless you find a concrete mismatch you need to explain.
   331→
   332→### Requirements (ALL BLOCKING — confirmation will reject if not met)
   333→
   334→1. Every step MUST have `--detail` with 80+ chars INCLUDING at least one file path (src/... or supabase/...)
   335→2. Every step MUST have `--issue-refs` linking it to specific review issue hash(es)
   336→3. Every step MUST have `--effort` tag (trivial/small/medium/large) — set INDIVIDUALLY, not bulk
   337→4. File paths in detail MUST exist on disk (validator checks this)
   338→5. No step may reference a skipped/wontfixed issue in its issue_refs
   339→
   340→### How to enrich
   341→
   342→{subagent_block}
   343→
   344→### Common lazy patterns to avoid
   345→
   346→**Copying the issue description as step detail.** The issue says "useGenerationsPaneController
   347→returns 60+ values mixing concerns." That's a PROBLEM description. The step detail should say
   348→"In src/shared/components/GenerationsPane/hooks/useGenerationsPaneController.ts (283 lines),
   349→extract lines 45-89 (filter state: activeFilter, setActiveFilter, filterOptions, applyFilter)
   350→into a new useGenerationFilters hook. The controller imports and spreads the sub-hook's return."
   351→
   352→**Vague action verbs.** "Refactor", "clean up", "improve", "fix" are not actions.
   353→"Extract lines 45-89 into useGenerationFilters", "delete lines 12-18", "rename the file
   354→from X to Y and update the 3 imports in A.tsx, B.tsx, C.tsx" are actions.
   355→
   356→**Guessing file paths.** If you write `src/shared/lib/jsonNarrowing.ts` and it doesn't exist,
   357→confirmation will block. READ the file system. Only reference files you've verified exist.
   358→
   359→**Bulk effort tags.** Don't mark everything "small". A file rename with 2 imports is "trivial".
   360→Decomposing a 400-line hook into 3 sub-hooks is "medium" or "large". Think about each one.
   361→
   362→**Over-engineering the fix.** KISS: the fix should be simpler than the problem. If you're
   363→adding interfaces, registries, factory methods, or configuration to fix a code smell, the
   364→cure is worse than the disease. Prefer: delete code > inline code > extract a function >
   365→add an abstraction. If the simplest fix is "delete these 10 lines," say that — don't
   366→propose a refactoring framework.
   367→
   368→### Examples
   369→
   370→**GOOD step detail:**
   371→```
   372→--detail "In src/shared/hooks/billing/useAutoTopup.ts lines 118-129, add onMutate handler
   373→to capture previous queryClient state before optimistic update. In onError callback, restore
   374→previous state and change showToast from false to true."
   375→--issue-refs 79baeebf --effort small
   376→```
   377→
   378→**BAD step detail (will be rejected):**
   379→```
   380→--detail "Fix silent error swallowing"  # No file, no location, no action
   381→--detail "Decompose god-hooks"  # What file? What hooks? Into what?
   382→--detail "Address the issues identified in the observe stage"  # This says nothing
   383→```
   384→
   385→### Do NOT mark steps as done
   386→
   387→Use `--update-step N` to add detail, effort, and issue-refs.
   388→Do NOT use `--done-step` — steps are only marked done when actual code changes are made.
   389→
   390→### File path rules
   391→
   392→Only reference files that exist RIGHT NOW. Do not reference files that a step will create
   393→(e.g., a new shared module) or rename targets (the new filename after a rename). Reference
   394→the current source file and describe what will change. The path validator will block
   395→confirmation if paths don't exist on disk.
   396→
   397→{tail}
   398→"""
   399→
   400→
   401→def _sense_check_instructions(mode: PromptMode = "self_record") -> str:
   402→    content_fix_block = (
   403→        'Fix with: `desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "..." --effort <tag>`'
   404→    )
   405→    structure_fix_block = """\
   406→Fix with: `desloppify plan cluster update <name> --depends-on <other>`
   407→Fix with: `desloppify plan cluster update <name> --add-step "..." --detail "..." --effort trivial --issue-refs <hash>`
   408→"""
   409→    tail = """\
   410→When done, run:
   411→```
   412→desloppify plan triage --stage sense-check --report "<findings summary>"
   413→```
   414→"""
   415→    if mode == "output_only":
   416→        content_fix_block = (
   417→            "Report the exact step corrections that need to be made; the orchestrator will apply them."
   418→        )
   419→        structure_fix_block = (
   420→            "Report the exact dependency additions or cascade steps that need to be made; "
   421→            "the orchestrator will apply them.\n"
   422→        )
   423→        tail = """\
   424→When done, write a plain-text sense-check report with concrete content and structure fixes.
   425→The orchestrator records and confirms the stage.
   426→"""
   427→    investigation_hint = (
   428→        "Use `desloppify show <issue-hash> --no-budget` to verify step details "
   429→        "match the original reviewer analysis.\n\n"
   430→        if mode == "self_record"
   431→        else ""
   432→    )
   433→    constraints = render_constraints(header="   Also flag steps that:", bullet="   - ")
   434→    return f"""\
   435→## SENSE-CHECK Stage Instructions
   436→
   437→This stage is handled by two parallel subagents. If you are being run as a
   438→single-subprocess fallback, perform BOTH the content and structure checks below.
   439→
   440→### Content Check (per cluster)
   441→{investigation_hint}For EVERY step in every cluster, read the actual source file and verify:
   442→1. OVER-ENGINEERING (check FIRST — if the fix fails this, skip the rest):
   443→   Would this change make the codebase *worse*? Flag steps that:
   444→   - Add abstractions, wrappers, or indirection for a one-time operation
   445→   - Introduce unnecessary config/feature-flags/generalization
   446→   - Make simple code harder to read for marginal benefit
   447→   - Gold-plate beyond what the issue actually requires
   448→   - Trade one smell for a worse one (e.g. fix duplication by adding a fragile base class)
   449→   - Add more lines of code than they remove (net complexity increase)
   450→{constraints}
   451→   Remove or simplify over-engineered steps. If the whole cluster is net-negative,
   452→   recommend removing it entirely — don't just flag it, say "skip this cluster."
   453→2. STALENESS: Is the problem still present, or already fixed?
   454→3. LINE NUMBERS: Does the code at the claimed lines match the step description?
   455→4. NAMES: Do function/variable/type names in the step exist in the file?
   456→5. COUNTS: Are counts ("update the 3 imports") accurate?
   457→6. VAGUENESS: Could a developer with zero context execute this step?
   458→7. EFFORT TAGS: Does the tag match actual scope?
   459→8. DUPLICATES: Flag steps that duplicate work in another cluster.
   460→
   461→{content_fix_block}
   462→
   463→### Structure Check (global)
   464→Build a file-touch graph and check:
   465→1. SHARED FILES: Two clusters touching same file without --depends-on → add dependency
   466→2. MISSING CASCADE: Rename/remove without importer updates → add cascade step
   467→3. CIRCULAR DEPS: Flag cycles, don't add them
   468→
   469→{structure_fix_block}
   470→
   471→{tail}
   472→"""
   473→
   474→
   475→def _value_check_instructions(mode: PromptMode = "self_record") -> str:
   476→    tail = """\
   477→When done, run:
   478→```
   479→desloppify plan triage --stage value-check --report "<keep/skip/tighten summary>"
   480→```
   481→"""
   482→    queue_commands = """\
   483→Use these commands aggressively:
   484→- `desloppify next --count 100` to inspect the current execution queue
   485→- `desloppify plan cluster show <name>` to inspect cluster members and steps
   486→- `desloppify show <issue-id-or-hash> --no-budget` to re-read the underlying finding
   487→- `desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "..." --update-step N --detail "..." --effort small`
   488→- `desloppify plan skip --permanent <pattern> --note "<why>" --attest "I have reviewed this triage skip against the code and I am not gaming the score by suppressing a real defect."`
   489→- `desloppify plan cluster delete <name>` if you skipped everything it contained
   490→"""
   491→    if mode == "output_only":
   492→        queue_commands = (
   493→            "State the exact queue items to keep, tighten, or skip, plus any cluster-step "
   494→            "updates or deletions needed. The orchestrator will apply them."
   495→        )
   496→        tail = """\
   497→When done, write a plain-text value-check report with an item-by-item keep/skip/tighten ledger.
   498→The orchestrator records and confirms the stage.
   499→"""
   500→    return f"""\
   501→## VALUE-CHECK Stage Instructions
   502→
   503→Your task: walk the CURRENT planned work item by item and make the final judgment about value.
   504→
   505→This is not a detector-confidence pass. It is a beauty-and-elegance pass with discipline.
   506→Ask: does doing this make the codebase genuinely better? Beauty is a valid reason to keep work,
   507→but not if it buys that beauty with new indirection, wrappers, abstraction layers, or confusion.
   508→
   509→### Rubric
   510→
   511→For EVERY live queue target, choose exactly one:
   512→1. `keep` — clearly improves correctness, clarity, cohesion, simplicity, or elegance
   513→2. `tighten` — worth doing, but the plan must be simplified or made more concrete first
   514→3. `skip` — the fix would add churn, indirection, coordination, or abstraction for too little gain
   515→
   516→What should usually be skipped:
   517→- facade pruning that just spreads imports
   518→- abstraction-for-abstraction's-sake
   519→- tiny theoretical cleanups that make the code harder to follow
   520→- fixes whose implementation is more complicated than the current code
   521→
   522→What can still be worth keeping:
   523→- simplifications that delete layers or reduce branching
   524→- aesthetic cleanups that genuinely improve readability without adding machinery
   525→- focused unifications that make naming or flow more coherent with less confusion
   526→
   527→{queue_commands}
   528→
   529→### Required process
   530→
   531→1. Re-read the actual code behind each queue target.
   532→2. Apply the rubric above, not the raw issue title.
   533→3. Tighten any keeper whose steps are too vague, too broad, or too complicated.
   534→4. Permanently skip anything that fails the value test.
   535→5. Delete dead clusters after skipping all their members.
   536→
   537→### Required report structure
   538→
   539→Start with:
   540→```
   541→## Decision Ledger
   542→- cluster-or-id -> keep
   543→- cluster-or-id -> tighten
   544→- cluster-or-id -> skip
   545→```
   546→
   547→Then add short sections explaining the reasoning, with concrete file references.
   548→Cover every live queue target exactly once in the ledger.
   549→
   550→Optimize for:
   551→- simplicity over cleverness
   552→- directness over indirection
   553→- deletion over abstraction when deletion solves it
   554→- concrete executor-ready detail for everything you keep
   555→
   556→{tail}
   557→"""
   558→
   559→
   560→_STAGE_INSTRUCTIONS = {
   561→    "observe": _observe_instructions,
   562→    "reflect": _reflect_instructions,
   563→    "organize": _organize_instructions,
   564→    "enrich": _enrich_instructions,
   565→    "sense-check": _sense_check_instructions,
   566→    "value-check": _value_check_instructions,
   567→}
   568→
   569→
   570→__all__ = [
   571→    "_STAGE_INSTRUCTIONS",
   572→    "_enrich_instructions",
   573→    "_observe_instructions",
   574→    "_organize_instructions",
   575→    "_reflect_instructions",
   576→    "_sense_check_instructions",
   577→    "_value_check_instructions",
   578→]
   579→

    34→            "- A cluster whose issues span 5+ unrelated directories will be flagged\n"

    36→            "- Stage must be recorded and confirmed\n"

    38→    if stage == "enrich":

    10→_OBSERVE_FALSE_POSITIVE_GUIDANCE = """\
    11→**The review system has a high false-positive rate.** Issues frequently:
    12→- Claim "12 unsafe casts" when there are actually 2
    13→- Describe code that was already refactored
    14→- Propose over-engineering that would make things worse
    15→- Count props/returns/args wrong

    17→  to eliminate 5 lines of duplication)
    18→- Flag issues where the current code is "good enough" — imperfect but clear, simple,
    19→  and not causing real problems
    20→
    21→Your job is to catch these. A report that just restates issue titles is **worthless**.
    22→The value you add is reading the actual code and forming an independent judgment.
    23→
    24→Use the `not-worth-it` verdict when: the issue is technically real but the fix would add
    25→more complexity than it removes, or the current code is simple and readable despite being
    26→theoretically suboptimal. YAGNI — if nobody is actually confused or blocked by this code,

1→"""Stage-gate and fold-confirm helpers for triage completion."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     6→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import (
     7→    TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE,
     8→    TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS,
     9→    compute_triage_progress,
    10→)
    11→
    12→from .enrich_checks import _underspecified_steps
    13→from ..review_coverage import manual_clusters_with_issues
    14→from ..stages.helpers import unenriched_clusters
    15→from .stage_policy import (
    16→    AutoConfirmStageRequest,
    17→    confirm_stage,
    18→)
    19→
    20→
    21→_COMPLETE_AUTO_CONFIRM_STAGE_CONFIG = {
    22→    "organize": {
    23→        "label": "Organize",
    24→        "blocked_heading": "Cannot complete: organize stage not confirmed.",
    25→        "confirm_cmd": "desloppify plan triage --confirm organize",
    26→        "inline_hint": "Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm organize inline.",
    27→    },
    28→    "enrich": {
    29→        "label": "Enrich",
    30→        "blocked_heading": "Cannot complete: enrich stage not confirmed.",
    31→        "confirm_cmd": "desloppify plan triage --confirm enrich",
    32→        "inline_hint": "Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm enrich inline.",
    33→    },
    34→    "sense-check": {
    35→        "label": "Sense-check",
    36→        "blocked_heading": "Cannot complete: sense-check stage not confirmed.",
    37→        "confirm_cmd": "desloppify plan triage --confirm sense-check",
    38→        "inline_hint": "Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm sense-check inline.",
    39→    },
    40→    "value-check": {
    41→        "label": "Value-check",
    42→        "blocked_heading": "Cannot complete: value-check stage not confirmed.",
    43→        "confirm_cmd": "desloppify plan triage --confirm value-check",
    44→        "inline_hint": "Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm value-check inline.",
    45→    },
    46→}
    47→
    48→
    49→def _manual_cluster_names(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
    50→    return [name for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items() if not cluster.get("auto")]
    51→
    52→
    53→def _first_missing_recorded_stage(stages: dict, *, through_stage: str) -> str | None:
    54→    progress = compute_triage_progress(stages)
    55→    recorded = {stage.name: stage.recorded for stage in progress.stages}
    56→    for stage_name, _label in TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS:
    57→        if not recorded.get(stage_name, False):
    58→            return stage_name
    59→        if stage_name == through_stage:
    60→            return None
    61→    return None
    62→
    63→
    64→def _auto_confirm_stage_for_complete(
    65→    *,
    66→    plan: dict,
    67→    stages: dict,
    68→    stage: str,
    69→    attestation: str | None,
    70→    save_plan_fn=None,
    71→) -> bool:
    72→    stage_record = stages.get(stage)
    73→    if stage_record is None:
    74→        return False
    75→
    76→    config = _COMPLETE_AUTO_CONFIRM_STAGE_CONFIG[stage]
    77→    return confirm_stage(
    78→        plan=plan,
    79→        stage_record=stage_record,
    80→        attestation=attestation,
    81→        request=AutoConfirmStageRequest(
    82→            stage_name=stage,
    83→            stage_label=config["label"],
    84→            blocked_heading=config["blocked_heading"],
    85→            confirm_cmd=config["confirm_cmd"],
    86→            inline_hint=config["inline_hint"],
    87→            cluster_names=_manual_cluster_names(plan),
    88→        ),
    89→        save_plan_fn=save_plan_fn,
    90→    )
    91→
    92→
    93→def _require_enrich_stage_for_complete(
    94→    *,
    95→    plan: dict,
    96→    meta: dict,
    97→    stages: dict,
    98→    underspec: list[tuple[str, int, int]] | None = None,
    99→) -> bool:
   100→    missing = _first_missing_recorded_stage(stages, through_stage="enrich")
   101→    if missing is None:
   102→        return True
   103→    if missing != "enrich":
   104→        return _require_organize_stage_for_complete(plan=plan, meta=meta, stages=stages)
   105→
   106→    if underspec is None:
   107→        underspec = _underspecified_steps(plan)
   108→    if underspec:
   109→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: enrich stage not done.", "red"))
   110→        print(colorize(f"  {len(underspec)} cluster(s) have underspecified steps (missing detail or issue_refs):", "yellow"))
   111→        for name, bare, total in underspec[:5]:
   112→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps need enrichment", "yellow"))
   113→        print(colorize('  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"', "dim"))
   114→        print(colorize('  Then: desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "..."', "dim"))
   115→    else:
   116→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: enrich stage not recorded.", "red"))
   117→        print(colorize("  Steps look enriched. Record the stage:", "dim"))
   118→        print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "..."', "dim"))
   119→    return False
   120→
   121→
   122→def _auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete(
   123→    *,
   124→    plan: dict,
   125→    stages: dict,
   126→    attestation: str | None,
   127→    save_plan_fn=None,
   128→    underspec: list[tuple[str, int, int]] | None = None,
   129→) -> bool:
   130→    if "enrich" not in stages:
   131→        return False
   132→
   133→    if underspec is None:
   134→        underspec = _underspecified_steps(plan)
   135→    if underspec:
   136→        total_bare = sum(n for _, n, _ in underspec)
   137→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot auto-confirm enrich: {total_bare} step(s) still lack detail or issue_refs.", "red"))
   138→        for name, bare, total in underspec[:5]:
   139→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps", "yellow"))
   140→        print(colorize('  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"', "dim"))
   141→        return False
   142→
   143→    return _auto_confirm_stage_for_complete(
   144→        plan=plan,
   145→        stages=stages,
   146→        stage="enrich",
   147→        attestation=attestation,
   148→        save_plan_fn=save_plan_fn,
   149→    )
   150→
   151→

   153→    *,
   154→    plan: dict,

   157→) -> bool:
   158→    missing = _first_missing_recorded_stage(stages, through_stage="sense-check")
   159→    if missing is None:
   160→        return True
   161→    if missing != "sense-check":
   162→        return _require_enrich_stage_for_complete(plan=plan, meta=meta, stages=stages)
   163→
   164→    print(colorize("  Cannot complete: sense-check stage not recorded.", "red"))
   165→    print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage sense-check --report "..."', "dim"))
   166→    return False
   167→
   168→

   170→    *,
   171→    plan: dict,

   174→) -> bool:
   175→    missing = _first_missing_recorded_stage(stages, through_stage="value-check")
   176→    if missing is None:
   177→        return True
   178→    if missing != "value-check":
   179→        return _require_sense_check_stage_for_complete(plan=plan, meta=meta, stages=stages)
   180→
   181→    print(colorize("  Cannot complete: value-check stage not recorded.", "red"))
   182→    print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage value-check --report "..."', "dim"))
   183→    return False
   184→
   185→
   186→def _require_organize_stage_for_complete(
   187→    *,
   188→    plan: dict,
   189→    meta: dict,
   190→    stages: dict,
   191→) -> bool:
   192→    missing = _first_missing_recorded_stage(stages, through_stage="organize")
   193→    if missing is None:
   194→        return True
   195→    if missing == "observe":
   196→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: no stages done yet.", "red"))
   197→        print(colorize('  Start with: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   198→        return False
   199→
   200→    print(colorize("  Cannot complete: organize stage not done.", "red"))
   201→    gaps = unenriched_clusters(plan)
   202→    if gaps:
   203→        print(colorize(f"  {len(gaps)} cluster(s) still need enrichment:", "yellow"))
   204→        for name, missing in gaps:
   205→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   206→        print(colorize('  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "..." --steps "step1" "step2"', "dim"))
   207→        print(colorize(f"  Then: {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   208→    else:
   209→        manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   210→        if manual:

   212→            print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   213→        else:
   214→            print(colorize("  Create enriched clusters first, then record organize:", "dim"))
   215→            print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   216→    if meta.get("strategy_summary"):
   217→        print(colorize('  Or fast-track: --confirm-existing --note "why plan is still valid" --strategy "..."', "dim"))
   218→    return False
   219→

    43→def run_enrich_quality_checks(
    44→    plan: dict,
    45→    repo_root: Path,
    46→    *,
    47→    phase_label: str,
    48→) -> list[EnrichQualityFailure]:
    49→    """Run enrich-level executor-readiness checks for a phase."""
    50→    report = evaluate_enrich_quality(
    51→        plan,
    52→        repo_root,
    53→        phase_label=phase_label,
    54→        bad_paths_severity="failure",
    55→        missing_effort_severity="failure",
    56→        include_missing_issue_refs=True,
    57→        include_vague_detail=True,
    58→        stale_issue_refs_severity="failure",
    59→    )
    60→    code_map = {"underspecified": "underspecified_steps", "bad_paths": "missing_paths"}
    61→    return [
    62→        EnrichQualityFailure(
    63→            code=code_map.get(issue.code, issue.code),
    64→            message=issue.message,
    65→        )
    66→        for issue in report.failures
    67→    ]

    70→def _validate_observe_stage(
    71→    *,
    72→    stages: dict,
    73→    triage_input: TriageInput | None,
    74→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    75→    """Validate recorded observe-stage content."""
    76→    if "observe" not in stages:
    77→        return False, "Observe stage not recorded."
    78→    report = stages["observe"].get("report", "")
    79→    if len(report) < 100:
    80→        return False, f"Observe report too short ({len(report)} chars, need 100+)."
    81→    cited = stages["observe"].get("cited_ids", [])
    82→    issue_count = stages["observe"].get("issue_count", 0)
    83→    if issue_count <= 0:
    84→        return True, ""
    85→    min_citations = min(5, max(1, issue_count // 10))
    86→    if len(cited) < min_citations:
    87→        return False, (
    88→            f"Observe report cites only {len(cited)} issue(s) "
    89→            f"(need {min_citations}+). Reference specific issue "
    90→            f"hashes to prove you read them."
    91→        )
    92→    review_issues = (
    93→        getattr(triage_input, "review_issues", getattr(triage_input, "open_issues", {}))
    94→        if triage_input
    95→        else {}
    96→    )
    97→    valid_ids = set(review_issues.keys())
    98→    evidence = parse_observe_evidence(report, valid_ids)
    99→    ev_failures = validate_observe_evidence(evidence, issue_count)
   100→    blocking = [failure for failure in ev_failures if failure.blocking]
   101→    if blocking:
   102→        return False, blocking[0].message
   103→    return True, ""
   104→

   106→def _validate_reflect_stage(stages: dict) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   107→    """Validate recorded reflect-stage content."""
   108→    if "reflect" not in stages:
   109→        return False, "Reflect stage not recorded."
   110→    report = stages["reflect"].get("report", "")
   111→    if len(report) < 100:
   112→        return False, f"Reflect report too short ({len(report)} chars, need 100+)."
   113→    for field_name, label in (
   114→        ("missing_issue_ids", "unaccounted for"),
   115→        ("duplicate_issue_ids", "duplicates"),
   116→    ):
   117→        ids = stages["reflect"].get(field_name, [])
   118→        if ids:
   119→            return False, f"Reflect report {label} {len(ids)} issue(s)."
   120→    issue_count = int(stages["reflect"].get("issue_count", 0) or 0)
   121→    cited = stages["reflect"].get("cited_ids", [])
   122→    if issue_count > 0 and len(cited) < issue_count:
   123→        return False, (
   124→            f"Reflect report cites only {len(cited)}/{issue_count} issue(s). "
   125→            "A reflect blueprint must account for every open review issue."
   126→        )
   127→    skip_failures = validate_reflect_skip_evidence(report)
   128→    blocking_skips = [failure for failure in skip_failures if failure.blocking]
   129→    if blocking_skips:
   130→        return False, blocking_skips[0].message
   131→    return True, ""

   134→def _organize_stage_warnings(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   135→    """Collect advisory organize-stage warnings."""
   136→    warnings: list[str] = []
   137→    overlaps = _cluster_file_overlaps(plan)
   138→    if overlaps:
   139→        warnings.append(f"{len(overlaps)} cluster pair(s) share files without dependencies")
   140→    scattered = _clusters_with_directory_scatter(plan)
   141→    if scattered:
   142→        names = ", ".join(name for name, _, _ in scattered)
   143→        warnings.append(f"Theme-grouped clusters (5+ dirs): {names}")
   144→    high_ratio = _clusters_with_high_step_ratio(plan)
   145→    if high_ratio:
   146→        names = ", ".join(name for name, _, _, _ in high_ratio)
   147→        warnings.append(f"1:1 step-to-issue ratio: {names}")
   148→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   149→    orphaned = [
   150→        name for name, cluster in clusters.items()
   151→        if not cluster.get("auto") and not cluster_issue_ids(cluster) and cluster.get("action_steps")
   152→    ]
   153→    if orphaned:
   154→        warnings.append(f"Orphaned clusters (steps, no issues): {', '.join(orphaned)}")
   155→    return warnings

   158→def _validate_organize_stage(plan: dict, state: dict, stages: dict) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   159→    """Validate recorded organize-stage content."""
   160→    if "organize" not in stages:

   162→    open_review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
   163→    manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   164→    if not open_review_ids and not manual:
   165→        report = stages["organize"].get("report", "")

   168→        return True, ""

   171→    gaps = unenriched_clusters(plan)
   172→    if gaps:

   174→        return False, f"Unenriched clusters: {names}"
   175→    unclustered = unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
   176→    if unclustered:

   178→    report = stages["organize"].get("report", "")
   179→    cluster_ref_failures = validate_report_references_clusters(report, manual)
   180→    blocking_refs = [failure for failure in cluster_ref_failures if failure.blocking]
   181→    if blocking_refs:
   182→        return False, blocking_refs[0].message
   183→    reflect_ts = stages.get("reflect", {}).get("timestamp", "")
   184→    if reflect_ts:
   185→        activity = count_log_activity_since(plan, reflect_ts)
   186→        cluster_ops = sum(
   187→            activity.get(key, 0)
   188→            for key in ("cluster_create", "cluster_add", "cluster_update", "cluster_remove")
   189→        )
   190→        min_ops = max(3, len(manual))
   191→        if cluster_ops < min_ops:
   192→            return False, (
   193→                f"Only {cluster_ops} cluster op(s) logged since reflect "
   194→                f"(need {min_ops}+). Run cluster create/add/update commands."
   195→            )
   196→    warnings = _organize_stage_warnings(plan)
   197→    if warnings:
   198→        return True, "Advisory: " + "; ".join(warnings)
   199→    return True, ""

   245→def _validate_value_check_stage(
   246→    plan: dict,
   247→    state: dict,
   248→    repo_root: Path,
   249→    stages: dict,
   250→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   251→    """Validate recorded value-check content and ledger coverage."""
   252→    if "value-check" not in stages:
   253→        return False, "Value-check stage not recorded."
   254→    report = stages["value-check"].get("report", "")
   255→    if len(report) < 100:
   256→        return False, f"Value-check report too short ({len(report)} chars, need 100+)."
   257→    failures = run_enrich_quality_checks(plan, repo_root, phase_label="value-check")
   258→    if failures:
   259→        return False, failures[0].message
   260→    path_failures = validate_report_has_file_paths(report)
   261→    blocking_pf = [failure for failure in path_failures if failure.blocking]
   262→    if blocking_pf:
   263→        return False, blocking_pf[0].message
   264→    targets = value_check_targets(plan, state)
   265→    parsed = parse_value_check_decision_ledger(report)
   266→    if not parsed.entries:
   267→        return False, "Value-check report missing `## Decision Ledger` entries."
   268→    if parsed.duplicates:
   269→        return False, f"Value-check report duplicates decision targets: {', '.join(parsed.duplicates[:5])}"
   270→    missing = [target for target in targets if target not in parsed.entries]
   271→    if missing:
   272→        return False, f"Value-check report missing decision(s) for: {', '.join(missing[:5])}"
   273→    extras = [target for target in parsed.entries if target not in targets]
   274→    if extras:
   275→        return False, f"Value-check report references non-live target(s): {', '.join(extras[:5])}"
   276→    vc_clusters = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   277→    cluster_failures = validate_report_references_clusters(report, vc_clusters)
   278→    blocking_cf = [failure for failure in cluster_failures if failure.blocking]
   279→    if blocking_cf:
   280→        return False, blocking_cf[0].message
   281→    return True, ""

   284→def validate_stage(
   285→    stage: str,
   286→    plan: dict,
   287→    state: dict,
   288→    repo_root: Path,
   289→    *,
   290→    triage_input: TriageInput | None = None,
   291→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   292→    """Check subagent completed stage correctly. Returns (ok, error_msg)."""
   293→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   294→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   295→    validators = {
   296→        "observe": lambda: _validate_observe_stage(
   297→            stages=stages,
   298→            triage_input=triage_input,
   299→        ),
   300→        "reflect": lambda: _validate_reflect_stage(stages),
   301→        "organize": lambda: _validate_organize_stage(plan, state, stages),
   302→        "enrich": lambda: _validate_enrich_stage(plan, repo_root, stages),
   303→        "sense-check": lambda: _validate_sense_check_stage(plan, state, repo_root, stages),
   304→        "value-check": lambda: _validate_value_check_stage(plan, state, repo_root, stages),
   305→    }
   306→    validator = validators.get(stage)
   307→    if validator is None:
   308→        return False, f"Unknown stage: {stage}"
   309→    return validator()
   310→
   311→
   312→def _validate_required_stages(stages: dict) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   313→    """Validate that all required triage stages are present and confirmed."""
   314→    for required in ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check", "value-check"):
   315→        if required not in stages:
   316→            return False, f"Stage {required} not recorded."
   317→        if not stages[required].get("confirmed_at"):
   318→            return False, f"Stage {required} not confirmed."
   319→    return True, ""
   320→
   321→
   322→def _validate_cluster_dependency_cycles(clusters: dict) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   323→    """Reject self-referential cluster dependencies."""
   324→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   325→        deps = cluster.get("depends_on_clusters", [])
   326→        if name in deps:
   327→            return False, f"Cluster {name} depends on itself."
   328→    return True, ""
   329→
   330→
   331→def _find_all_trivial_clusters(clusters: dict) -> list[str]:
   332→    """Return manual clusters whose action steps are all marked trivial."""
   333→    trivial_clusters: list[str] = []
   334→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   335→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster_issue_ids(cluster):
   336→            continue
   337→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps") or []
   338→        if steps and all(
   339→            isinstance(step, dict) and step.get("effort") == "trivial"
   340→            for step in steps
   341→        ):
   342→            trivial_clusters.append(name)
   343→    return trivial_clusters
   344→
   345→
   346→def validate_completion(
   347→    plan: dict,
   348→    state: dict,
   349→    repo_root: Path,
   350→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   351→    """Validate plan is ready for triage completion. Returns (ok, error_msg)."""
   352→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   353→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   354→
   355→    ok, message = _validate_required_stages(stages)
   356→    if not ok:
   357→        return ok, message
   358→
   359→    open_review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
   360→    manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   361→    if not open_review_ids and not manual:
   362→        return True, ""
   363→    if not manual:
   364→        return False, "No manual clusters with issues."
   365→
   366→    gaps = unenriched_clusters(plan)
   367→    if gaps:
   368→        return False, f"{len(gaps)} cluster(s) still need enrichment."
   369→
   370→    unclustered = unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
   371→    if unclustered:
   372→        return False, f"{len(unclustered)} review issue(s) not in any cluster."
   373→
   374→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   375→    ok, message = _validate_cluster_dependency_cycles(clusters)
   376→    if not ok:
   377→        return ok, message
   378→
   379→    all_trivial_clusters = _find_all_trivial_clusters(clusters)
   380→    if all_trivial_clusters:
   381→        names = ", ".join(sorted(all_trivial_clusters))
   382→        return True, f"Advisory: all action steps are marked trivial in cluster(s): {names}"
   383→
   384→    return True, ""
   385→
   386→
   387→def build_auto_attestation(
   388→    stage: str,
   389→    plan: dict,
   390→    triage_input: TriageInput,
   391→) -> str:
   392→    """Generate valid 80+ char attestation referencing real dimensions/cluster names."""
   393→    review_issues = getattr(triage_input, "review_issues", getattr(triage_input, "open_issues", {}))
   394→    if stage == "observe":
   395→        _by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
   396→        top_dims = dim_names[:3]
   397→        dims_str = ", ".join(top_dims)
   398→        return (
   399→            f"I have thoroughly analysed {len(review_issues)} issues "
   400→            f"across dimensions including {dims_str}, identifying themes, "
   401→            f"root causes, and contradictions across the codebase."
   402→        )
   403→
   404→    if stage == "reflect":
   405→        _by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
   406→        top_dims = dim_names[:3]
   407→        dims_str = ", ".join(top_dims)
   408→        return (
   409→            f"My strategy accounts for {len(review_issues)} issues "
   410→            f"across dimensions including {dims_str}, comparing against "
   411→            f"resolved history and forming priorities for execution."
   412→        )
   413→
   414→    if stage == "organize":
   415→        cluster_names = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   416→        if not cluster_names:
   417→            return (
   418→                "I verified there are zero open review issues in this organize batch, "
   419→                "so no manual clusters or issue assignments were needed before continuing."
   420→            )
   421→        names_str = ", ".join(cluster_names[:3])
   422→        return (
   423→            f"I have organized all review issues into clusters including "
   424→            f"{names_str}, with descriptions, action steps, and clear "
   425→            f"priority ordering based on root cause analysis."
   426→        )
   427→
   428→    if stage == "enrich":
   429→        cluster_names = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   430→        if not cluster_names:
   431→            return (
   432→                "I verified there are zero open review issues in this enrich batch, "
   433→                "so there were no action steps to elaborate before advancing."
   434→            )
   435→        names_str = ", ".join(cluster_names[:3])
   436→        return (
   437→            f"Steps in clusters including {names_str} are executor-ready with "
   438→            f"detail, file paths, issue refs, and effort tags, verified "
   439→            f"against the actual codebase."
   440→        )
   441→
   442→    if stage == "sense-check":
   443→        cluster_names = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   444→        if not cluster_names:
   445→            return (
   446→                "I verified there are zero open review issues in this sense-check batch, "
   447→                "so no cluster content or file-path evidence needed additional review."
   448→            )
   449→        names_str = ", ".join(cluster_names[:3])
   450→        return (
   451→            f"Content and structure verified for clusters including {names_str}. "
   452→            f"All step details are factually accurate, cross-cluster dependencies "
   453→            f"are safe, and enrich-level checks pass."
   454→        )
   455→
   456→    if stage == "value-check":
   457→        targets = value_check_targets(plan, getattr(triage_input, "state", None))
   458→        cluster_names = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   459→        if not targets:
   460→            return (
   461→                "I verified there were zero live queue targets in this value-check batch, "
   462→                "so no additional keep, tighten, or skip decisions were required."
   463→            )
   464→        names_str = ", ".join((cluster_names or targets)[:3])
   465→        return (
   466→            f"I reviewed each queued item for value, elegance, and avoidable indirection, "
   467→            f"including {names_str}, keeping only work that genuinely improves the codebase."
   468→        )
   469→
   470→    return f"Stage {stage} completed with thorough analysis of all available data and verified against codebase."
   471→
   472→

   475→    "build_auto_attestation",
   476→    "run_enrich_quality_checks",

    38→        print(colorize('  Use --strategy "same" to keep it, or provide a new summary.', "dim"))
    39→    else:
    40→        print(
    41→            colorize(

    47→
    48→
    49→def _strategy_valid_or_error(
    50→    strategy: str,
    51→    *,
    52→    include_guidance: bool,
    53→) -> bool:
    54→    if strategy.strip().lower() == "same":
    55→        return True
    56→    if len(strategy.strip()) >= 200:
    57→        return True
    58→    print(colorize(f"  Strategy too short: {len(strategy.strip())} chars (minimum 200).", "red"))
    59→    if include_guidance:
    60→        print(colorize("  The strategy should describe:", "dim"))
    61→        print(colorize("    - Execution order and priorities", "dim"))
    62→        print(colorize("    - What each cluster accomplishes", "dim"))

    67→def _completion_clusters_valid(plan: dict, state: dict | None = None) -> bool:

    70→

    77→        if not any_clusters:

    79→            print(colorize('  Create clusters: desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."', "dim"))
    80→            return False
    81→
    82→    gaps = unenriched_clusters(plan)
    83→    if gaps:

    85→        for name, missing in gaps:

    87→        print(colorize("  Small clusters (<5 issues) need at least 1 action step per issue.", "dim"))
    88→        print(colorize('  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "..." --steps "step1" "step2"', "dim"))

    97→        if len(unclustered) > 5:
    98→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 5} more", "yellow"))
    99→        print(colorize("  Add to a cluster or wontfix each unclustered issue.", "dim"))
   100→        return False
   101→
   102→    return True
   103→

     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

    16→
    17→def is_full_stage_run(stages_to_run: list[str]) -> bool:
    18→    """True when the pipeline was asked to run the full triage stage set."""
    19→    return set(stages_to_run) == set(STAGES)
    20→
    21→
    22→def all_stage_results_successful(
    23→    *,
    24→    stages_to_run: list[str],
    25→    stage_results: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]],
    26→) -> bool:
    27→    """True when each requested stage is confirmed or already confirmed."""
    28→    for stage in stages_to_run:
    29→        status = str(stage_results.get(stage, {}).get("status", ""))
    30→        if status not in {"confirmed", "skipped"}:
    31→            return False
    32→    return True
    33→
    34→
    35→def print_not_finalized_message(reason: str) -> None:
    36→    """Emit a consistent next-step message when auto-completion is skipped/blocked."""
    37→    print(colorize(f"\n  Stages complete, triage not finalized ({reason}).", "yellow"))
    38→    print(
    39→        colorize(
    40→            '  Finalize manually: desloppify plan triage --complete --strategy "<execution plan>"',
    41→            "dim",
    42→        )
    43→    )
    44→
    45→
    46→def validate_and_confirm_stage(
    47→    *,
    48→    stage: str,
    49→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    50→    services: TriageServices,
    51→    triage_input: Any,
    52→    state: Any,
    53→    repo_root,
    54→    stage_start: float,
    55→    append_run_log,
    56→) -> tuple[bool, dict, str]:
    57→    """Run shared stage validation + confirmation flow."""
    58→    plan = services.load_plan()
    59→
    60→    ok, error_msg = validate_stage(
    61→        stage,
    62→        plan,
    63→        state,
    64→        repo_root,
    65→        triage_input=triage_input,
    66→    )
    67→    if not ok:
    68→        elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - stage_start)
    69→        print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: validation failed: {error_msg}", "red"))
    70→        print(colorize("  Re-run to resume.", "dim"))
    71→        append_run_log(
    72→            f"stage-validation-failed stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s error={error_msg}"
    73→        )
    74→        return (
    75→            False,
    76→            {
    77→                "status": "validation_failed",
    78→                "elapsed_seconds": elapsed,
    79→                "error": error_msg,
    80→            },
    81→            "",
    82→        )
    83→
    84→    attestation = build_auto_attestation(stage, plan, triage_input)
    85→    confirm_args = argparse.Namespace(
    86→        confirm=stage,
    87→        attestation=attestation,
    88→        state=getattr(args, "state", None),
    89→    )
    90→
    91→    from ..confirmations.router import cmd_confirm_stage
    92→
    93→    cmd_confirm_stage(confirm_args, services=services)
    94→
    95→    plan = services.load_plan()
    96→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    97→    stages_data = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
    98→    elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - stage_start)
    99→    if stage in stages_data and stages_data[stage].get("confirmed_at"):
   100→        print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: confirmed ({elapsed}s).", "green"))
   101→        append_run_log(f"stage-confirmed stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s")
   102→        report = stages_data.get(stage, {}).get("report", "")
   103→        return (
   104→            True,
   105→            {"status": "confirmed", "elapsed_seconds": elapsed},
   106→            report,
   107→        )
   108→
   109→    print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: auto-confirmation did not take effect.", "red"))
   110→    print(colorize("  Re-run to resume.", "dim"))
   111→    append_run_log(f"stage-confirm-failed stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s")
   112→    return (
   113→        False,
   114→        {"status": "confirm_failed", "elapsed_seconds": elapsed},
   115→        "",
   116→    )
   117→
   118→
   119→def build_completion_strategy(stages_data: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
   120→    """Derive a completion strategy from stage reports."""
   121→    strategy_parts: list[str] = []
   122→    recorded_stages: list[str] = []
   123→    for stage in STAGES:
   124→        report = str(stages_data.get(stage, {}).get("report", ""))
   125→        if report:
   126→            recorded_stages.append(stage)
   127→            strategy_parts.append(f"[{stage}] {report[:200]}")
   128→    strategy = " ".join(strategy_parts)
   129→    if len(strategy) < 200:
   130→        if recorded_stages:
   131→            stage_list = ", ".join(recorded_stages)
   132→            summary = (
   133→                f"Triage covered {len(recorded_stages)} stage(s): {stage_list}. "
   134→                "Use the recorded stage reports as the execution plan, preserve the "
   135→                "observe and reflect evidence trail, keep organize and enrich changes "
   136→                "aligned with the recorded dispositions, and verify each cluster before completion."
   137→            )
   138→            strategy = f"{strategy} {summary}".strip()
   139→        else:
   140→            strategy = (
   141→                "Triage completed without recorded stage reports. Before completion, capture "
   142→                "the execution order, the cluster or skip decisions that will change plan state, "
   143→                "and the verification steps needed to confirm the resulting queue is correct."
   144→            )
   145→    return strategy
   146→
   147→
   148→def complete_pipeline(
   149→    *,
   150→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   151→    services: TriageServices,
   152→    plan: Mapping[str, Any],
   153→    strategy: str,
   154→    triage_input: Any,
   155→) -> bool:
   156→    """Run the triage completion coordinator and report success."""
   157→    completed_before = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("last_completed_at")
   158→
   159→    print(colorize("\n  Completing triage...", "bold"))
   160→
   161→    attestation = build_auto_attestation("value-check", plan, triage_input)
   162→    complete_args = argparse.Namespace(
   163→        complete=True,
   164→        strategy=strategy[:2000],
   165→        attestation=attestation,
   166→        state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   167→    )
   168→
   169→    from ..stages.completion import _cmd_triage_complete
   170→
   171→    _cmd_triage_complete(complete_args, services=services)
   172→
   173→    completed_after = (
   174→        services.load_plan().get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("last_completed_at")
   175→    )
   176→    return bool(completed_after and completed_after != completed_before)
   177→
   178→
   179→__all__ = [
   180→    "all_stage_results_successful",

   183→    "is_full_stage_run",
   184→    "print_not_finalized_message",
   185→    "validate_and_confirm_stage",
   186→]

    44→def _print_completion_coverage_warning(*, organized: int, total: int) -> None:
    45→    """Print warnings for incomplete cluster coverage at completion time."""
    46→    if total <= 0:
    47→        return

    50→        print(colorize(f"  {total} issues are waiting.", "dim"))
    51→        return
    52→    if organized < total:
    53→        remaining = total - organized
    54→        print(
    55→            colorize(
    56→                f"  Warning: {remaining}/{total} issues are not yet in any cluster.",
    57→                "yellow",
    58→            )
    59→        )

I now have a thorough understanding of the codebase. Let me compile the complete implementation plan.

---

## Implementation Plan: Fold value-check into sense-check as 3rd subagent

### Summary

This plan merges the "value-check" stage into "sense-check" as a third parallel subagent, reducing the triage pipeline from 7 stages (observe, reflect, organize, enrich, sense-check, value-check, commit) to 6 (observe, reflect, organize, enrich, sense-check, commit). The value subagent runs in parallel with the existing content and structure subagents, and the Decision Ledger validation moves into the sense-check confirmation gate.

### Validated Architecture

After reading all relevant files, the task description is accurate. Here are the precise changes, grouped by execution phase.

---

### Phase 1: Foundation constants and playbook

**`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/constants.py`**
- Remove `"triage::value-check"` from `TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS` tuple (line 20). This cascades to `TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS`, `TRIAGE_STAGE_ORDER`, `TRIAGE_IDS`, and `_TRIAGE_STAGE_NAMES` automatically since they are all derived from `TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS`.

**`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/triage/playbook.py`**
- Remove the `("value-check", "Decide item-by-item keep/skip value")` entry from `TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS` (line 13).
- Remove `TRIAGE_CMD_VALUE_CHECK` constant (lines 39-42).
- Remove `"value-check": TRIAGE_CMD_VALUE_CHECK` from `_MANUAL_STAGE_COMMANDS` (line 76).
- Remove the `"value-check"` entry from `TRIAGE_STAGE_PREREQUISITES` (lines 125-127).
- Change `"commit"` prerequisite from `StagePrerequisite("value-check")` to `StagePrerequisite("sense-check", require_confirmation=True)` (lines 128-130). This is a key semantic change: commit now depends directly on sense-check being confirmed.
- Remove `TRIAGE_CMD_VALUE_CHECK` from `__all__`.
- Update `TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES` derivation is automatic.

### Phase 11: Data migration

For existing `plan.json` files that have `triage_stages.value-check` data:
- The simplest approach: old value-check data is simply ignored. Since `_TRIAGE_STAGE_NAMES` no longer includes it, `_resolve_triage_stages` and `confirmed_triage_stage_names` will not recognize it as a stage, and it becomes inert data.
- No migration code needed per the backward compat policy (data migration is kept, but this is ephemeral report data, not critical state).

All test files listed in the git status that reference value-check need updates. The pattern is consistent: remove value-check from stage lists, update stage counts, update dependency assertions, remove value-check-specific test cases.

### Potential Risks

1. **Commit gate dependency change**: Currently commit depends on value-check (which depends on sense-check confirmed). After this change, commit depends on sense-check confirmed. The sense-check confirmation now also validates the Decision Ledger, so the gate is equivalent but simpler.

2. **Value subagent prompt needs plan + state access**: The value subagent needs `value_check_targets()` which requires both plan and state. The existing `run_sense_check()` in `orchestrator_codex_sense.py` only receives `plan`, not `state`. We need to either pass state through or compute targets from plan data only. Looking at `value_check_targets()`, it calls `manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)` and `unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)`. The state is optional (defaults to scanning queue_order). For the codex pipeline, state may not be readily available in the sense-check orchestrator. Solution: pass the targets list computed in the pipeline context, or pass state through.

3. **Parallel execution ordering**: The value subagent should run in parallel with structure (both are global). Content batches run first (when apply_updates is true), then structure and value can run together. This needs care in the sequencing logic in `run_sense_check()`.
